# Changelog

All notable changes to this project are documented in this file. The format is based on [Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/en/1.1.0/), and this project adheres to Semantic Versioning.

## [Unreleased]

### Added

- `HistoryRequest`, `TradeRequest` and `DepthRequest` carry an explicit range, `since` (inclusive) with the existing `before` (exclusive), alongside `count`. `Chart::scroll_to_time` bounds the pages it asks for by the target time. An empty `HistoryPage`, `TradePage` or `DepthPage` may name `continue_before`, where older data continues, so a weekend or other gap no longer ends history and the next request starts there instead of repeating; a continuation that does not lie before the request stops paging with `FeedError::InvalidData`. Charts page older trades into the held ones when the view reaches the oldest trade, while bubbles, a footprint or a study use them, merged by the existing id rules and retried and cancelled with the other requests, and study sources on other symbols follow continuations. `FeedState::trade_history_complete` and `depth_history_complete` report whether more can be loaded. Migration: `HistoryPage` is now `#[non_exhaustive]`; replace `HistoryPage { bars, end_of_history }` with `HistoryPage::new(bars, end_of_history)`. ([#1080](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1080))
- `Chart::set_conversion` carries a host-chosen currency or unit through the feed: `Datafeed::resolve_converted` describes the symbol in it, and `HistoryRequest`, `TradeRequest`, `DepthRequest` and `Subscription` gain `conversion`, which also reaches the studies on other symbols. A changed choice drops the data held, discards replies and subscriptions of the previous choice and reloads, and the legend shows the resolved instrument's currency and unit only once it arrives. A feed that cannot convert answers `NotSupported` (the default) and the chart shows no data instead of unconverted data. The library converts nothing. The request structs are `#[non_exhaustive]`, so hosts that only read them need no change. ([#1126](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1126))
- `services::Sharing` lets a host share layouts and watchlists: `offers` reports per `ShareKind` whether `link` and `copy` are available, `link` answers a view-only `ShareLink` that says whether a recipient may save a copy, and `copy` saves a copy and answers its name. `LayoutMenu::set_sharing` and `WatchlistPanel::set_sharing` add Copy link and Save a copy rows only for what the host offers, disable them while an operation is pending, copy the link to the clipboard and show the reported copy policy, the saved name or the host's rejection. A layout copy appears in the menu once re-listed from `Storage`; a watchlist copy is added to the panel's lists. Accounts and cloud storage stay with the host. ([#1099](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1099))
- `Datafeed::capabilities` reports what a feed offers as a whole before any symbol resolves: `FeedCapabilities` lists its intervals, exchanges, symbol classes and optional `FeedService`s, each either unreported (`None`, which restricts nothing) or a list, where an empty list means none. The chart asks once when the feed attaches and `Chart::feed_capabilities` returns the answer. `TimeframeSelector::set_feed_capabilities` limits the interval choices to the feed's, alongside a symbol's own `set_supported_timeframes`, and `SymbolSearchDialog::set_feed_capabilities` takes the source menu's exchanges and the category tabs from the feed. `FeedCapabilities::offers` tells a host whether to show the controls of a service. The default answers nothing, so existing feeds are unchanged. ([#1098](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1098))
- `ChartMenus::series_click_price` returns the price of the series menu's last right-click, which `SeriesContextMenuAction::CopyPrice` does not carry. ([#1214](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1214))
- `WatchlistPanel::show_wide` draws the active list as a wide table with every `WatchlistColumn::WIDE` column and Earnings, Dividends and News tabs filled from the host's `WatchlistDetails`; its view button returns `WatchlistAction::CompactView`, and `AdvancedView` asks for the wide view. The tabs list the rows of the selected symbol, or of every shown symbol while none is selected, and clicking a symbol selects it. Selection, sorting, the active list and the flag filter belong to the panel, so switching views keeps them. `SymbolDetailsPanel` gains Performance and Seasonality sections: `take_market_requests` returns a `MarketDetailRequest` for each section a symbol still lacks and marks it loading, and the host answers with `set_period_returns` (`PeriodReturn`) and `set_seasonality` (`Seasonality`, `SeasonalityMonth`); answers for another symbol are ignored. Every host-supplied section is a `MarketDetail` and says whether it is loading, empty, unavailable or failed. A host that never asks sees none of it. ([#1056](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1056))
- Watchlists duplicate, filter by flag and show more quote columns. The list menu duplicates the active list as "Name copy" (`Watchlist::duplicate`), keeping sections, symbol order and flags, and the Flagged symbols swatches show the symbols flagged one colour across every list, each once, so the red-flag view follows flag changes; a flag change or removal in that view applies to every list that flags the symbol that colour (`WatchlistPanel::set_flag_filter`, `flag_filter`); `symbols` then returns the flagged symbols so the quote subscription follows. `WatchlistPanel::set_columns` adds `WatchlistColumn::Volume`, `ExtendedLast`, `ExtendedChange` and `ExtendedChangePercent`, measured from the regular last price, which sort with missing figures last and show a dash when the quote lacks them; they follow the live quote stream. `ChartMenus::set_watchlists` adds a submenu to the chart menu that returns `ContextMenuAction::AddToWatchlist { list, symbol }`, applied with `WatchlistPanel::add_symbol_to`, which refuses a symbol the list already holds. `WatchlistColumn` is now `#[non_exhaustive]`. Migration: matches on `WatchlistColumn` add a wildcard arm. ([#1055](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1055))
- The order ticket offers its risk percent sizing for limit, stop and stop-limit entries, sized from the entry price with the same validation as a market entry. Orders from the depth ladder take the ticket's enabled take profit and stop loss, priced from the ladder order's own side, entry and quantity, and the confirmation lists them; an invalid exit refuses the order and says why. `Capabilities::close`, `reverse` and `preview` are reported independently: the Close and Flatten controls, the Reverse controls and the new Preview order button appear only when set, and the ladder's disabled buttons explain why. `Broker::preview` answers an `OrderPreview` (cost, commission, margin and warnings) without placing the order; the ticket then confirms that exact request, and a refused preview shows an error. Migration: an account that closes and reverses positions through its market orders sets `close` and `reverse`, which `PaperBroker` now does; without them the Close, Flatten and Reverse controls are hidden. `Broker` implementations need no other change. ([#1054](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1054))
- The layout menu stars saved layouts and filters the list to the starred ones with a Favorites toggle, keeping the current layout selected. `LayoutMenu::favorites` holds the starred names, follows a rename, loses a deleted layout and is kept between sessions in the new `UserSettings::layout_favorites`; documents written before read with none. ([#1051](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1051))
- `AlertsPanel` orders its list by name, symbol or status, ignoring case, in either direction, with equal values in arrival order, and offers a Duplicate button that returns `AlertPanelAction::DuplicateAlert` with a copy that has no identifier and is active, for `AlertDialog::edit`. A Quick alert form creates price, study-output and volume alerts with the dialog's condition editor and `Alert::is_valid`, starts on the symbol given to `AlertsPanel::set_symbol`, offers the outputs given to `AlertsPanel::set_sources`, and sends them through the connected `Alerts` service; a save or removal waiting for the service is shown, a failure keeps the form and its error, and the list changes only when the service pushes. ([#1046](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1046))
- The market heatmap shows a universe chosen from `MarketHeatmapPanel::universes`, sizes its tiles by one `MarketHeatmapMetric` and colours them by another, both chosen independently in the panel from the host's metrics with their labels, units and availability, and draws a legend of the size metric and the colour scale. `MarketHeatmapPanel::selection` (`MarketHeatmapSelection`, also `UserSettings::market_heatmap`) holds the choices for settings restore; a chosen universe or metric the host does not offer or has no data for stays chosen and shows as unavailable. Migration: replace `MarketHeatmapPanel::entries` with `universes`, put the former `value` and `change_percent` of each `MarketHeatmapEntry` into `values` under the identifiers of two `MarketHeatmapMetric`s listed in `metrics` (the second may set `saturation: Some(5.0)` for the former fixed scale). ([#1124](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1124))
- The screener sends the market, a text description and a page offset in `ScreenerQuery::universe`, `text` and `offset`. `ScreenerPanel::set_universes` offers the host's `ScreenerUniverse`s (stocks, funds, bonds, coins, exchange pairs) in a selector; `set_text_query` adds a field whose description the feed interprets in the optional `Datafeed::screen_text`, answering the rows with an explanation (`ScreenerTextAnswer`) shown above them; a full answer offers Show more for the next page. `set_storage` lets the user save, rename, load and delete named screens in the new `StoreKind::Screens`, each holding its universe, column set, filters, sort order and description, so a restored screen sends the same request. The chosen criteria stay through loading, failures, paging and CSV export. ([#1096](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1096))
- `Chats` offers optional host operations, enabled through `Chats::capabilities`: `find_users` to choose recipients, `create_room` for direct, private and group rooms (`NewChatRoom`), and `add_member` and `remove_member` for rooms whose members the host pushes with `ChatEvent::Members`. Users and rooms keep the host's stable identifiers (`ChatUser`). The chats panel header offers New chat and Members where the host allows them, shows each action while it is pending and when the host refuses it, keeps the form after a refusal, opens a created room once pushed, and clears the form, recipients and pending actions when `ChatsPanel::set_service` attaches another service. Accounts, authorization and delivery stay in the host. ([#1094](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1094))
- `StrategyReport::summary_csv` writes the summary metrics and run properties the report panel shows as a table with the columns `section`, `name`, `value`, `unit` and `currency`: money in the report's currency, percentages as 100 for 100%, and `N/A` for an unavailable value. The panel's new Export summary CSV button returns `StrategyReportAction::ExportSummaryCsv { filename, content }` beside the trades export. ([#1121](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1121))
- `NewsQuery` carries `symbols`, `topics` and `providers`, and the news panel offers them: `NewsPanel::set_symbols` adds symbols to the followed one, and `set_topics` and `set_providers` list the topic values and provider identifiers the feed supports. Every change sends the complete selection through `Datafeed::news`, a superseded reply is ignored, and the choices stay selected through loading, empty and failed states and while the host offers the same values again. Migration: read `NewsQuery::symbols`, empty for the whole market, instead of `NewsQuery::symbol`. ([#1101](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1101))
- The depth ladder's View menu shows columns for the order count of each side, the observed size change of each side and the volume traded at the bid and the ask, independently, beside the resting size and the last account fill. The hover text uses the same values: a change is one observed between book snapshots, not confirmed execution or cancellation, and an order count the feed did not send is shown as unavailable. The price column's profile is total volume, delta or bid and ask volume, summed over the retained trades, the trades since the open of the current exchange session, or the trades since a manual reset, which later live trades extend. ([#1077](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1077))

- Order flow studies sum the same executions three more ways, added like any indicator: Multi-Range Delta (`OrderFlowKind::SizeDelta`) gives the buy minus sell volume of three independent inclusive size ranges, Rolling Volume and Delta (`FlowSum`) sums the volume and the delta over a number of bars, drawn as histograms or lines, and Size-Filtered CVD (`FilteredCvd`) accumulates the delta of executions in a size range in its own pane. A bar without executions is a gap; a rolling sum over a gap or before its warm-up is a gap, and a cumulative sum carries over a gap. Trades of unknown side add to the volume but to no delta. Live updates, history merges and evictions follow the trade rate's, and `update` recomputes only from the first changed bar. ([#1078](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1078))

- `OrderFlowKind::AggressorRate` gives the bar total and the rolling and instant rates per second of distinct explicit aggressor order ids, counting an order once however many fills it has and neither merging nor counting fills without an id. `OrderFlowKind::LevelsExecuted` counts the distinct prices that buy and sell executions printed at, per bar or over a rolling window, optionally restarting a side when its executions reverse; unlike `LevelsTradedThrough` it ignores quote movement, so cancellations add nothing. Both are Order flow studies (Aggressor Rate, Levels Executed) and follow history merges and evictions as the trade rate does. `CombinedTrade::consumed` lists the resting orders (`ConsumedOrder`: id, price, size) that an aggressive order's retained fills took, summing to its size, and `CombinedTrade::complete` says whether its aggressor and every resting order are named. Time and sales shows that breakdown when hovering a fill with an aggressor order id. ([#1072](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1072))

- Trade profiles choose volume nodes through `FootprintConfig::node_sensitivity` (`NodeSensitivity`): `smoothing` averages each price row with that many rows on each side, and `prominence` is the smallest rise or drop against the neighbouring plateaus, as a share of the largest smoothed volume. Untraded prices count as zero volume, so a gap between traded prices is a valley, found as one span however wide. Rows of equal smoothed volume form one plateau, reported once at its lowest row. `TradeProfile::nodes` takes the sensitivity, whose default compares neighbouring rows as before. `FootprintConfig::value_area_high` and `value_area_low` draw the VAH and VAL guides on the outer edges of the shaded rows, each with its own `Stroke` or hidden with `None`. Migration: `FootprintConfig` literals add `value_area_high`, `value_area_low` and `node_sensitivity`, and `TradeProfile::nodes` callers pass `NodeSensitivity::default()`. ([#1079](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1079))
- Book size, the liquidity heatmap and the depth ladder count individual resting orders through one `data::OrderFilter`: a minimum order size applied before aggregation, a minimum resting age measured from the placement time the feed supplies, and excluded participants. `RestingOrder::with_participant` and `with_placed` carry that metadata, never inferred. `OrderFlowStudy::with_order_filter` (and the study's Minimum order size and Minimum resting seconds inputs) filter book size, `DepthHistoryConfig::orders` samples the heatmap from the accepted orders instead of the aggregate prices, and `Chart::set_order_book_view` applies `chart::overlays::OrderBookView` to the heatmap's profile and the ladder: the profile measures the total resting size or the largest known individual order at each price (`BookProfile`), and two `OrderSizeTiers` highlight prices whose largest known order is notable or large, in the profile and the ladder's size cells. `OrderQueue::filtered` gives hosts the same summed book. With aggregate depth only, or when no order carries the participant or placement time the filter needs, book size has no value, the heatmap has no column, the ladder shows no sizes and the pane says so (`OrderFilterUnavailable`), rather than showing unfiltered sizes. Migration: `DepthHistoryConfig` literals add `orders: None` or use `..Default::default()`; `RestingOrder` is `#[non_exhaustive]` and gains `participant` and `placed`. ([#1073](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1073))
- `cargo bench --bench heatmap` measures the liquidity heatmap of a book of 1,000 levels a side at a 50 ms column cadence: sink time per delta, frame time, texture upload, textures and heap, with the history filled to every resolution. `docs/order-flow.md` documents the configuration, the measured budget on a stated machine (a frame under 5 ms and 56 MB of heap) and the degradation policy. `DepthHistory` now shares its columns, so `LiquidityHeatmap` tells a changed column by identity instead of keeping a copy of every column and comparing each frame: for 1,000 levels a side and a full history its heap falls from 110 MB to 56 MB and its median frame from 9.8 ms to 2.1 ms. ([#1112](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1112))
- Footprint studies take a `FootprintStudyConfig` and report where each level stands. `delta_percent` and `ratio` mark bars whose absolute delta, as a share of all volume with unknown side included, or buy-to-sell volume ratio reaches a threshold (equality counts; a side with volume against none meets any ratio) as `FootprintStudyKind::DeltaAsk`, `DeltaBid`, `RatioAsk` and `RatioBid`, drawn as triangles above or below the bar. `min_delta` gives the delta POC a minimum absolute net delta, with ties still going to the lower price. `UnfinishedAuction` is replaced by `UnfinishedHigh`, marked when sell volume traded at the bar's high, and `UnfinishedLow`, when buy volume traded at its low, even with the other side empty; both extend into later bars until a revisit, like stacks and naked POCs, and `extend_bars` expires any of them after that many later bars. `diagonal` false builds imbalance zones from the same price instead of the adjacent one. `FootprintStudy::state` is `Local`, `Unvisited`, `Revisited` or `Expired`, and `render_footprint_studies` dims revisited and expired levels. Migration: `footprint_studies(flow, ratio, stack, cluster_share, single_print_max)` becomes `footprint_studies(flow, &FootprintStudyConfig { imbalance_ratio: ratio, stack, cluster_share: Some(share), single_print_max: Some(max), ..Default::default() })`, where a share outside 0 to 1 or a maximum of zero is still off, `UnfinishedAuction` matches become `UnfinishedHigh | UnfinishedLow`, and `FootprintStudy` literals add `state`. ([#1076](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1076))
- Footprint cells offer more presentation choices through `FootprintConfig`. `FootprintColor::Fixed` fills every cell with the neutral colour and `DeltaOpacity` colours by the sign of the delta with opacity following its share of the bar's largest; `FootprintMode::DominantSide` shows only the larger of the sell and buy volume, at the left or right of the cell. `abbreviate` writes 1.2K and 3.4M, `show_zero` false leaves zero volumes blank, and `font_size` sizes each row's number by its volume between two limits held within 8 and 24 points, leaving out a number whose size exceeds its row's height. `extreme_ratio` writes the ask/bid ratio above the highest and below the lowest row of each bar, or `A/B n/a` when the bid is zero, and `pullback` outlines the rows chosen by `overlays::pullback_range`: from the close to the extreme the bar left, none when it closed on that extreme. Executed totals are never changed, `Footprint::level_of` gives the level a price falls in, and `FootprintConfig` and its enums are serializable, with fields missing from older documents taking their defaults. Migration: `FootprintConfig` literals add the new fields or use `..Default::default()`, and matches on `FootprintMode` and `FootprintColor` add the new variants. ([#1075](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1075))
- Volume bubbles take their grouping from `VolumeBubblesConfig::grouping`: `BubbleGrouping::Automatic` keeps one bubble per bar and price level, `Ungrouped` draws every execution at its own time and price, so fills of one instant and price stay separate circles, `TimeWindow` and `Executions` group by a window of time or a number of consecutive executions whatever the bar interval, and `PriceStep` sets the height of a level. Bubbles that do not sit on a bar's centre are placed in its slot by their time; groups of executions keep their edges as the bounded trade store evicts the oldest trades. `BubbleColor` gains `Split` (a circle per side, sized by its own volume), `AbsoluteDelta` (the sign picks the side, the absolute delta sets the opacity against the largest visible one), `DeltaPercent` (the delta as a share of all volume, unknown side included) and `Volume` (one colour). `VolumeBubblesConfig` and its enums are serializable, and fields missing from older documents take their defaults. Migration: `VolumeBubblesConfig::price_step: Some(step)` becomes `grouping: BubbleGrouping::PriceStep(step)`; `None` becomes `BubbleGrouping::Automatic`. ([#1074](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1074))
- `services::AlertEngine`, behind the new `alert-engine` feature, is an `Alerts` service that keeps alerts in memory and evaluates them on the bars the host feeds it through `update(symbol, timeframe, bars, now, value)`, where `value` resolves study and drawing sources at a bar index. It needs no egui, so a server can run the same code. Crossings compare each value with the previous evaluation of the same alert and symbol, every rule is judged on the same bar, an update that changed nothing or whose newest bar is older than one already evaluated does nothing, so reloads, replay rewinds and timeframe switches never fire again, and `tick(now)` expires alerts without data. `with_alerts` and `alerts` restore and persist the list, and `set_watchlist` supplies the members of a watchlist alert. `Alert::timeframe` names the timeframe an alert is evaluated on; the engine fixes an alert that has none to the first timeframe it evaluates it on, and the demo fills it from the chart. Stored alerts load unchanged. ([#1132](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1132))
- `Chart::alert_value(source, bar)` returns the value an alert source has at a bar of the main series: the close for price, the volume for volume, the study output named by its series identifier in decimal, and the level of a drawing named as `Drawing::alert_source` does, so a chart can feed `AlertEngine::update` or any other evaluator. `Chart::alert_sources` lists every output of every study and every level of every completed drawing with the names the alert dialog shows, for `AlertDialog::sources`. Both need the `drawings` feature. The demo uses them instead of its own copies. ([#1133](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1133))
- `Alert::expand_message` fills `{{symbol}}`, `{{timeframe}}`, `{{time}}`, `{{open}}`, `{{high}}`, `{{low}}`, `{{close}}`, `{{volume}}`, `{{value}}` (the first source's value) and `{{output_N}}` (output N of the first source's study) from the evaluated bar; an unknown placeholder stays as written and a missing value becomes empty text. `AlertEvent::TriggeredForSymbol::message` carries the result, which `AlertEngine` fills on every trigger, and `AlertEngine::on_trigger` gives the host the alert and the event on each one, for email, push notifications or web requests. The alerts panel shows the message in the popup and the log, `AlertTrigger::message` keeps it and the CSV export gains a final `message` column. `AlertsPanel::set_sound` takes the user's alert sound, or none when sound is off, and `take_sound` returns it after a poll that received a trigger, as `Trading::take_execution_sound` does for fills; the demo plays it from there. Migration: `AlertEvent::TriggeredForSymbol` literals and patterns add `message` (use `String::new()` or `..`); `AlertTrigger` literals add `message`; readers of the CSV export expect five columns. ([#1134](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1134))
- The demo evaluates alerts with `AlertEngine` instead of its own copy of the rules, so it shows what a host gets from the crate. The bars of each symbol and timeframe its alerts watch, from the hidden monitors of live charts, are fed to the engine once a frame through `Chart::alert_value`, and a chart in replay is not fed, so no alert fires again on entering or leaving replay. `tick` runs every frame and the demo repaints at least once a second while an alert with an expiry is active. The demo keeps the alerts with its other session data, restoring them with `with_alerts`, and delivers trigger notifications through `on_trigger`. ([#1136](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1136))

- The volume profile studies take a Source interval input. A finer interval than the chart's is requested from the host through `Indicator::data_requests`, and each finer bar is distributed over its own price rows in place of the chart bar's high to low estimate, for the visible range, fixed range, session, periodic, anchored and auto anchored profiles alike. A chart bar without finer bars keeps the estimate so no volume is lost, a reply for another interval is dropped, and `profile_inputs` returns for each profile the share of its bars the finer data covers. The default, chart bars, is unchanged. A feed hint for the interval and zoom-adaptive row detail are not part of this change. ([#1087](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1087))

- `ChartPatterns::new(PatternFamily::Reversal)`, registered as Reversal Chart Patterns, finds cup and handle, inverted cup and handle, double and triple tops and bottoms, and head and shoulders and its inverse, on the same confirmed swing pivots, rules and `formations()` results as the continuation patterns. Tolerance, depth, handle pullback and length limits are study inputs and the rules are documented on `ChartPatterns`. A formation confirms on the first close beyond its neckline or rim from the bar its last pivot is known, so a neckline broken before then confirms on that bar, and no confirmation reads a later bar. Reversal formations draw only their neckline or breakout level. ([#1085](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1085))

- `ChartPatterns::new(PatternFamily::Continuation)`, registered as Continuation Chart Patterns in the Patterns category, finds bull and bear flags and pennants, rectangles and ascending, descending and symmetrical triangles in the bars. Swing pivots confirm `strength` bars after the pivot; each formation's pivots, boundary segments, direction and confirmation bar are returned by `formations()`, developing formations apart from confirmed ones, and a confirmed formation reads no bar after its confirmation bar. Strength, minimum and maximum length, tolerance, touches per boundary, pole size and a toggle per formation are study inputs, confirmations are marked and labelled, and boundaries are generated as drawings. The retained pivots, developing and confirmed formations are bounded. ([#1084](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1084))

- `BollingerBands` takes a basis, simple by default, through `with_basis` and the Basis MA type input: exponential, weighted, smoothed or volume weighted. It sets the middle band and the centre of both outer bands, while the deviation stays the population deviation about the window's simple mean. `BollingerBandsWidth` adds Highest Expansion and Lowest Contraction outputs, the rolling highest and lowest width over lookbacks of 125 bars each by default, set through `with_lookbacks` and two inputs. A bar with a non-finite close is skipped in the width and its extrema, so later values equal those of the series without it. Migration: `BollingerBandsWidth` now has three lines instead of one, so colour lists of one entry keep only the width colour. ([#1088](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1088))

- `ChartEvent::MarkClick { id, series, time }` reports a primary click on a bar mark that has a `Marker::id`, through `Chart::drain_events`. The hit area is the one that shows the mark's tooltip, so only visible marks respond; of overlapping marks the one painted on top is reported, and the click neither selects nor deselects a series or study. Drawing tools and the eraser take the click first. ([#1106](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1106))

- `KineticScrollOptions::mouse` continues a released mouse drag with the same damping, speed limits, minimum movement and cancellation as touch inertia, which `enabled` keeps controlling on its own. Migration: `KineticScrollOptions` literals add `mouse: false` or use defaults. ([#1065](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1065))
- The study catalogue lists Lunar Phases, which marks each new moon with an upward triangle at the low and each full moon with a downward triangle at the high of the bar whose interval holds it, the two styled and shown independently. `studies::lunar_phases` returns the new and full moons between two UTC instants for 1900 to 2100 from the algorithm of Meeus, chapter 49, converted with the ΔT polynomials of Espenak and Meeus; its times are within about a minute of the published almanac times. Marks depend only on the bar times, so paging history, scrolling or restoring a template never repeats or moves a phase. ([#1120](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1120))
- Woodies CCI columns show three stages by the run of CCI 14 values on one side of zero: a first value or a zero is neutral, two to five values are a transition, and six or more are established, in the columns colour above zero and the new Established down colour below it. Bars without a value are skipped and a zero ends the run. The established down, neutral and transition colours are study inputs, and a saved columns colour is kept. Columns were two-coloured by the five previous values before, so the bar that crosses zero after an established run is now neutral and the bars before an established run are no longer coloured by their sign. ([#1118](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1118))
- `DrawingToolbar::show_floating` lists the favorite tools in `ToolbarState::favorites`, so starring or unstarring one changes the strip at once, instead of six fixed tools. The strip can be dragged, `ToolbarState::floating_pos` holds where it was left, and its close button clears `ToolbarState::floating_visible`. `CrosshairStyle::Magic` and `CursorType::Magic` select a trail that follows the pointer while the primary button is held, which then does not pan the chart, and fades after release, without creating drawings; switching cursor drops the trail. Alt+Shift+R selects the rectangle tool by default, is listed in the shortcut help and is replaced by a rebinding in `ShortcutMap`. Migration: match arms over `CrosshairStyle` and `CursorType` add `Magic`; `ToolbarState` literals add the two floating fields. ([#1052](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1052))
- `DrawingToolbar::show` and `show_with_action` draw the favourite tools strip beside the toolbar and return a tool picked on it like one picked in the toolbar, so a host no longer has to place it; `show_floating` remains for hosts that position the strip themselves and draws nothing a second time in the same pass. A dotted grip at the strip's leading edge drags it, kept on screen, and tool buttons click without moving it. A star button after the Hide menu shows and hides the strip, selected while it is shown, and starring a tool shows it again. A new `ToolbarState` starts with no favourites, so the strip appears with the first starred tool, and the strip no longer carries a magnet toggle or tool menu. `UserSettings::drawing_favorites_hidden` and `drawing_favorites_pos` keep the strip's visibility and position with `drawing_favorites`, and the demo restores them. ([#1194](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1194))
- Ghost Feed projects candles along its clicked path: `GhostFeedConfig::spacing` sets the bars each candle spans, `wick` the extra wick as a fraction of its range, and `GhostFeedConfig::candles` returns the open, high, low and close of each, at most `MAX_GHOST_CANDLES`. They are drawn through the active time and price scales, rising ones filled and falling ones hollow in the translucent drawing colour, and follow path edits, copy and paste, undo, templates and saved drawings because they are built from the path and the stored config. The drawing dialog edits the spacing and wick; non-finite values are refused on save. Migration: `Drawing` literals add `ghost_feed`. ([#1109](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1109))
- Gann squares draw their grid, fan and arcs independently, each shown or hidden by `GannConfig::show_grid`, `show_fan` and `show_arcs`. `fan_ratios` and `fan_direction` (forward from the first anchor, reverse from the opposite corner or both) set the fan lines, and `arc_ratios` sets arcs around the first anchor as fractions of the side, cut at the square's edge. They replace the fixed diagonals, cross lines, circles and degree markers, are edited in the drawing dialog, are checked for positive finite ratios, and are kept in templates, undo and saved drawings; older saved drawings take the defaults. Migration: `GannConfig` literals add the new fields or `..Default::default()`. ([#1110](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1110))
- Four support and resistance studies join the catalogue. `IntradayPivotPoints` takes the levels of each block of an exchange trading session, of a chosen number of minutes from the session's first bar, from the block before it in the same session. `ProjectedPivotPoints` draws, beyond the last bar, the levels the next period will use if the current one ended now, beside the confirmed lines of Pivot Points Standard. `MissedPivots` keeps the swing highs and lows that price has not returned to, confirmed `right` bars after their bar and revisited when a bar reaches them within a percentage tolerance, drawing each from its pivot to its revisit. `KeyLevels` clusters swing highs and lows within a percentage tolerance into levels of a minimum number of touches, drawing each and listing it with its contributing pivots in a table. Left and right pivot bars, tolerance, touches and source period are study inputs that persist with templates. ([#1091](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1091))

- Applied VWAP studies edit and persist their bands and event anchors through study inputs. "Bands" sets the number of bands, up to ten, and "Band n multiplier" each finite non-negative multiplier in the unit chosen by "Percentage bands"; new bands continue the sequence 1, 2, 3. "Event time zone" names the IANA zone or fixed offset in which "Events" and "Event n" write local times as `YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS`; the event anchor mode applies them sorted and deduplicated, a skipped local time is rejected and a repeated one takes its first occurrence. Templates, copies and undo restore both, and `ConfiguredStudy::apply_settings` keeps the styles of existing plots and gives plots added or removed by an input the study's own styles. `InputKind::Text` and `InputValue::Text` carry a single line of text a study parses itself, and the study settings dialog edits it. `with_std_dev_bands` and `with_percentage_bands` keep at most ten bands. Migration: code matching `InputKind` or `InputValue` adds a `Text` arm. ([#1092](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1092))

- `TrendStrengthIndex` plots the rolling Pearson correlation of the source price with bar position over its own period, from -1 to 1 in its own pane with a zero line, and is listed in the catalogue as "Trend Strength Index". A bar has no value during warmup, when its window holds a missing price, or when the window's prices are all equal; the latest value equals `LinearRegressionChannel::correlation` for the same period. ([#1115](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1115))

- PPO and PVO choose their short and long averages and their signal line independently as `OscillatorAverage::Exponential` or `Simple` through the "Oscillator MA" and "Signal MA" study inputs and `with_averages`. Both default to exponential, which keeps the former values; the choices persist with the study inputs, templates and snapshots, and changing either recomputes the history. A simple average is the mean of the last `length` defined values and is defined once that many exist, like the exponential one. ([#1116](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1116))

- Floating, held and tracking data readouts lead with the instrument's symbol, and follow a change of instrument. The main series' legend title row offers hide or show, settings and remove buttons that run the chart's typed operations on `SeriesId::MAIN`, leaving studies and other series in place; settings arrive through `Chart::take_indicator_settings_request` as `SeriesId::MAIN`. A hidden main series keeps its dimmed title and buttons so it can be shown again, and the buttons are reachable with Tab. ([#1063](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1063))
- `SeriesStyle::alerts` (`SeriesAlerts`: `visible`, `labels`, `color`) holds how one series' alert levels are drawn, and the series settings dialog edits it under Scales and lines, Alert levels, through `SeriesSettings::alerts`. The host passes `labels` and `color` to `render_alert_levels` for the alerts it resolves to the series and draws none while `visible` is unset. The main series' choice is read and applied by `Chart::series_settings` and `apply_series_settings`, kept by `ChartSnapshot::main_series_alerts` (older layouts take the defaults) and by undo and redo, and another series' choice is read and written through `Chart::series_style` and `series_style_mut`, which its options keep through undo. Migration: `SeriesStyle`, `SeriesSettings` and `ChartSnapshot` literals add the new field or use defaults. ([#1128](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1128))
- `Chart::export_svg` and `Trading::export_svg` write the frame of `export_image` as an SVG document of the options' size: text stays text, fades keep their gradients and the frame's textures, such as legend icons, are embedded as PNG. Host paint callbacks, host-bound textures, rotated or underlined text and gradient strokes return an error that names them instead of being left out, and `export_png` likewise fails on host paint callbacks and host-bound textures, such as a watermark image, that it used to leave out. `ImageExportOptions::include_watermark` (default on, as on screen) omits the symbol watermark and `set_watermark` text independently of the title and timestamp, in PNG, SVG and frame exports, and the export dialog offers it as a checkbox and `ExportFormat::Svg` to hosts that list it. Images now include the footprint, volume bubbles, order book and liquidity heatmap that the chart's feed holds. Migration: `ImageExportOptions` literals inside `egui-charts` add `include_watermark` (the struct is non-exhaustive, so hosts are unaffected); `match` arms over `ExportFormat` add `Svg`. Capturing the whole terminal layout and drawing host textures remain open. ([#1050](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1050))
- `Chart::data_table` returns the cells of `export_csv` as a `DataTable` (`headers`, `rows`, and `csv` and `tsv` of chosen columns), and `DataTableDialog` lists them with a column menu, copy of the displayed columns as tab-separated text and a `DataTableAction::Export` carrying their CSV for the host to save. Only the visible rows are painted, so a long history stays smooth, missing values show as an en dash and stay empty in copied and exported text, and the table lists only loaded bars, the replay cutoff's bars during replay and the source bars on price-based chart types. The chart context menu offers `ContextMenuAction::OpenDataTable`, which the host answers with `DataTableDialog::open`. The table rebuilds when the symbol, interval, bar count, first or last bar, replay cutoff or study list changes. Migration: `match` arms over `ContextMenuAction` add `OpenDataTable`. ([#1093](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1093))
- `StudyEditor` is a study source editing panel that embeds in any layout: it holds a document title and text, shows an unsaved-changes mark, lists host `Diagnostic`s (`Severity`, message and byte `span`) and selects and scrolls to a span when its message is clicked. `StudyEditorAction` carries the open, save and apply requests with the title and text; the host answers with `open_document`, `mark_saved`, `set_diagnostics` and `apply_result`, and a rejected apply leaves the edits in place. The library does not own the study language, files or compilation. ([#1057](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1057))
- `IdeasPanel` is an ideas composer and feed that embeds in any layout: it edits a title, body and attach-chart choice for the symbol and interval set with `set_context`, lists `Idea`s and short posts (`IdeaKind`) with author, time, instrument and attachment, pages with `add_page` and `IdeasAction::LoadMore`, and emits `Publish`, `Open` and `OpenChart` intents. The host answers a publish with `publish_succeeded` (identifier and link) or `publish_failed`, which keeps the draft, and `set_publishing` and `set_browsing` hide the controls of a service the host does not offer. Accounts, storage and the network stay with the host. ([#1095](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1095))

- `PriceAxisOptions::show_symbol_name` and `show_study_names`, and the Scales and lines settings checkboxes of the same names, write the symbol or study name before its last value tag on the price scale, so equal values of different series stay apart. They are off by default, independent of the legend titles and the value visibility flags, and persist with the settings. Added series write their own name on their tags. Migration: `PriceAxisOptions` and `ScalesAndLinesSettings` literals add `show_symbol_name: false` and `show_study_names: false` or use defaults. ([#1064](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1064))

- Point and Figure builds from high/low input, as before, or from closes through `ChartTypeParams::pnf_source` and the series settings Symbol tab. The choice is part of the cached build, so a change rebuilds the columns, and it persists with the chart type parameters. Migration: `ChartTypeParams` literals add `pnf_source: PriceBasedSource::HighLow` or use `..Default::default()`. ([#1062](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1062))

- The HLC area chart's fill opacity and close line width are set through `ChartTypeStyles::hlc_area` and the series settings Symbol tab, and persist with the chart type parameters. Defaults keep the former look; opacities outside 0 to 1 are clamped and a non-positive width hides the line. Migration: `ChartTypeStyles` literals add `hlc_area: HlcAreaStyle::default()` or use `..Default::default()`. ([#1103](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1103))
- A star on each chart type menu row adds the type to the favourites or removes it. The toolbar then shows a button for each favourite in menu order, the last selected non-favourite and a menu chevron, with the same rules as the interval buttons. A new selector has no favourites. `ChartTypeSelector::preferences` and `set_preferences` exchange serializable `ChartTypePreferences` for host user settings, and `ChartTypeAction::ToggleFavorite` reports a toggle; the demo saves and restores them through application storage. ([#1042](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1042))

- The documentation status page shows feature completion by area from the maintained feature list, as a feature map coloured by status and one table per area. The same build produces per-area status badges and the feature map image for embedding. ([#1129](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1129))

- `Terminal` of the `egui-charts-terminal` package in `terminal/` assembles the top toolbar, the chart context menus and one or more chart slots in a `ChartLayout` on the host's `Datafeed`, and performs their actions: chart type, undo and redo across the whole chart history, the light and dark theme (following the system appearance until the user chooses one, on every slot) and fullscreen. `Terminal::show` performs what the controls emitted in that frame, in order, and returns the actions the host intercepted through `with_intercept`, the `Host` entries of the toolbar and menus, and every action the terminal has no default for; `Terminal::run` performs an action whatever the intercept says, waiting for the first frame when called before it. `Action` wraps each component's own action enum unchanged. Services are passed as `Arc<dyn Trait>` (`with_storage`, `with_marks`, repeatable `with_signals`, `with_symbol_search`, `with_alerts`, repeatable `with_broker`, `with_quotes`, `with_notifications`, `with_chats`) and files and sounds as callbacks (`with_file_export`, `with_sounds`); with `with_storage` the theme, symbol and interval persist through `SettingsStore`. Controls of features the terminal does not perform yet stay hidden, and `hide` hides any other. `terminal/examples/terminal.rs` runs it on the synthetic feed. The package uses only the library's public API and is not published. ([#205](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/205))
- `Terminal` performs the indicator dialog, which also lists the metrics the feed offers, and indicator settings, chart settings from the top toolbar, the chart and series menus, a double click on the main series and the price axis, symbol search when the host passes `with_symbol_search`, the keyboard shortcuts dialog and the study menu's host entries (add a study from a study, add to favourites, copy, settings). Starred studies are saved with `UserSettings::indicator_favorites`, settings the chart rejects leave the previous ones in place and are reported, and the series menu's Copy price copies the clicked price at the instrument's precision. ([#205](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/205))
- `Chart::set_study_limit` caps the studies a chart takes from the interface, with `Chart::study_limit` to read it and `Chart::try_add_indicator`, which refuses a study beyond the cap with an error. Pasting a copied study respects it, `add_indicator` does not, and `IndicatorDialog::set_full` makes the dialog say so and add nothing. Hosts call `terminal.chart_mut().set_study_limit(Some(n))`. ([#205](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/205))

- `Terminal` shows the drawing toolbar beside the chart and keeps it in step with the active chart every frame, and performs its actions, the drawing menu, the drawing properties dialog's Delete and Clone, and the object tree's actions as undoable chart edits; zoom stays outside undo, and a colour dragged in the object tree ends as one step when the pointer is released. The object tree and the data window open in the side panel from the rail's Object Tree icon and from the chart and study menus, and the data window reads the hovered bar. Pasted drawing text is added through the chart's drawing clipboard. The per-tool drawing defaults saved from the properties dialog persist in `UserSettings::drawing_defaults`. Hide all and lock all drawings set the state the toolbar shows instead of toggling the drawings' current state. ([#205](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/205))

- `Chart::set_signal_sources` connects several `Signals` services to one chart, each with its own stream, so that a `Clear` or a lagged stream of one source leaves the signals of the others. `set_signal_source` remains for a single source. ([#205](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/205))
- `UserSettings` keeps indicator and interval favourites, drawing tool defaults, watchlists and the replay session to resume; documents written before read with their defaults. ([#205](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/205))

- `ElliottWave` ("Elliott Wave", Support and resistance) finds five-wave impulses in confirmed swing pivots under the supported rules: wave 2 stays above the start of wave 1, wave 3 passes the end of wave 1, wave 4 does not overlap wave 1 and wave 3 is not the shortest of waves 1, 3 and 5, mirrored for bearish impulses. A construction is developing from wave 3, with its wave number as the label of the latest anchor, and is invalidated by a close beyond the end of wave 1 or by a later pivot that breaks a rule. It is confirmed on the bar that confirms its fifth anchor, and confirmed constructions never change. The table lists the developing construction and the last `history` confirmed ones with the price of every anchor and the confirmation time. ([#1117](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1117))

- `RisingWedge` and `FallingWedge` ("Rising Wedge" and "Falling Wedge", Support and resistance) detect converging trendlines through confirmed swing pivots. The latest `touches` pivot highs and lows must keep the wedge's direction, the lines must converge by at least `convergence` percent and the anchors span at most `max_bars` bars. The developing lines are output on every bar until the close leaves the wedge: below the lower line of a rising wedge or above the upper line of a falling one confirms it, the other side or the lines meeting ends it. The table lists the developing wedge and the last `history` confirmed ones with their anchor and confirmation times. A found or confirmed wedge does not change with later bars, and a confirmed wedge consumes its pivots. ([#1086](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1086))

- `AdxBreakout` ("ADX Breakout", Trend), `KnoxvilleDivergence` ("Knoxville Divergence", Momentum) and `Reversal` ("Reversal", Support and resistance) add signal studies with separate upward and downward outputs, editable periods and thresholds, and documented rules. ADX Breakout marks a close beyond the high or low of the last `range` bars after an ADX at or below the threshold. Knoxville Divergence compares consecutive price pivots with the momentum at them, gated by an oversold or overbought RSI, and marks the confirmation bar of the second pivot. Reversal marks a new extreme of the lookback that closes beyond the previous close. Each signal is final on the bar that shows it and does not depend on later bars. ([#1090](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1090))

- `Ratio`, `Spread` and `Correlation` ("Ratio", "Spread" and "Correlation Coefficient", Statistics) combine two series, each a price source of the chart symbol or of a host-supplied symbol at the chart's interval, chosen by the `symbol_0`, `source_0`, `symbol_1` and `source_1` inputs. Series align to chart bars by interval; a bar where either has no bar or a non-finite value has no output, and nothing is carried forward or taken from the chart in its place. The correlation is the Pearson correlation of the last `length` values, or of log returns with the `log_returns` input, and has no value while its window holds a missing bar. A zero ratio denominator, a non-positive price in the log-return mode and a series constant over the window are unavailable. Hosts supply the histories through `Chart::study_data_requests` and `set_study_data`; no market data service is included. ([#1081](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1081))

- `TechnicalRatings` ("Technical Ratings", Momentum) scores the buy, sell and neutral votes of simple and exponential moving averages of three lengths and of the RSI, Stochastic, CCI, MACD and Momentum from -1 to 1 as separate moving-average, oscillator and summary outputs, each with its coverage, the share of enabled components that had a vote. A component in its warm-up or on a bar with a non-finite high, low or close has no vote, so it leaves the mean and lowers the coverage. Every component, its period and the toggles for the averages and oscillators are study inputs. The table shows the latest score, its Strong Buy, Buy, Neutral, Sell or Strong Sell rating at the thresholds of 0.5 and 0.1 and the coverage of each group. ([#1119](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1119))

- Trading settings persist line extension, left, centre or right label placement, and whether `Trading::export_image` and `export_png` include visible trading overlays. Migration: `TradingSettings` literals add `extend_lines: true`, `label_alignment: TradingLabelAlignment::Right` and `include_in_image: false`, or use defaults; `TradingLineOptions` literals add `extend_lines` and `label_alignment`. ([#994](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/994))

- The snapshot menu offers Download image, Copy image and Open in new tab. Hosts with publishing enable `TopToolbarState::can_publish_image` and handle Copy link, Post image and Publish through their service. The demo copies images or opens them in a browser tab or native viewer. Migration: `TopToolbarState` literals add `can_publish_image: false` or use defaults. ([#1006](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1006))

- The save menu offers Save layout, Download chart data, Create new layout and Open layout as 32-point rows, retaining rename, save-as, image export and autosave. The demo routes data download and layout opening through its existing handlers. Save and snapshot menus scroll in short windows. ([#1005](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1005))
- The object tree creates named folders from selected drawings, nests folders and moves selected drawings between folders or back to the root. Folder visibility and locking apply to all descendants as one undoable edit. Drawing folder paths persist with drawing identities, independently of host groups; hosts retain empty folders through `ObjectTreeState::folders`. Migration: `Drawing` and `SourceItem` literals add `folder: Vec::new()`; `ObjectTreeState` literals use `..Default::default()` for its folder and rendered-order state; copy `Drawing::folder` into live drawing rows. Older saved drawings start at the root. ([#978](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/978))

- Scales and lines settings expose currency and unit headers, scale-mode and price-action visibility, a numeric price-to-bar ratio lock, label overlap policy, and symbol, study and extended-session value colours and visibility. Units come from host metadata without converting data. ([#993](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/993))

- Alert settings filter active levels and set trigger notification lifetime. Inactive levels can be shown dimmed; the existing local sound controls apply the selected volume. ([#995](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/995))

- Event settings independently show earnings badges and dashed earnings breaks, news badges and host news notifications, and supplied ideas. The demo delivers enabled headlines through its notification service and suppresses repeated requests while they remain in its recent log. Economic-event visibility is preserved. ([#996](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/996))

- Symbol settings independently hide candle bodies, borders and wicks, colour candles by the previous close, and request dividend-adjusted history from the host. Candle borders draw inside the body; older saved settings retain borderless defaults. Status line settings show the supplied logo and last day change and set the study legend backdrop opacity, including studies in separate panes. Zero opacity hides the backdrop. ([#992](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/992))

- Alert expiry uses separate date and 24-hour time fields in `AlertDialog::timezone` (UTC by default), with a No expiry choice. Invalid or past times block saving; skipped local times are rejected and newly entered repeated times use their first occurrence. ([#981](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/981))

- `ChartType::VolumeFootprint` and `ChartType::SessionVolumeProfile` draw the bid and ask volume at each price of every bar, and the volume at each price of every session of the visible bars with its point of control, value area and developing point of control. Both are summed from the trades the feed sends and keep up with them while it streams. They need a time-based interval and the instrument's tick size; unsupported intervals and bars before the first held trade draw as candles, and the session profile needs the `studies` feature. The chart type menu lists them around Time Price Opportunity, followed by Heikin Ashi, Renko, Line break, Kagi, Point & Figure and Range, with 13 point gaps between its groups. The demo drops its synthetic footprint and trade profile menu items, which paused the feed, for the chart types. ([#967](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/967))
- The broker picker's compare button shows brokers side by side, two at first, with an Add broker control for other offered brokers and a Connect button under each. `BrokerTile::facts` supplies their rows, such as pricing, leverage and features, as `BrokerFact::Value` or `BrokerFact::Supported`. Each label gets one row across the brokers; a broker without it shows a dash read as not available, unlike the cross of an unsupported feature. The demo gives its brokers example facts. ([#1003](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1003))
- `ui::financials::FinancialsPanel` shows a company's host-supplied `CompanyFinancials` in place of the chart: a pill per `FinancialPage`, a column chart of the chosen row over the page's periods and the table of all its rows, with a Back to chart button, and a message when the host has no figures. The symbol details panel's More menu offers Financials, which `SymbolDetailsPanel::take_financials_request` reports. The demo opens synthetic statements and statistics for its stocks. ([#1002](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1002))
- Symbol search validates arithmetic expressions such as `AAPL-MSFT`, reports invalid symbols and syntax, and returns a synthetic instrument. `services::SymbolExpression` evaluates expressions and aligns component histories by timestamp; the demo charts spreads in their own panes. ([#976](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/976))

- Symbol search offers an exchange source menu through `SymbolSearchDialog::set_sources` and expands instruments sharing `Instrument::root`. Results and recents retain separate exchange listings, and selection returns the exact instrument. The demo includes two futures expiries for drilldown. ([#975](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/975))

- Compare lists recent symbols through `CompareSymbolsDialog::set_storage`, including with a local catalog. Aligned 56-point rows show host marks, descriptions, type badges and optional `Instrument::country`. Rows offer Same % scale, New price scale and New pane, with an Add menu in narrow windows. Migration: match `CompareAction::AddSymbol(symbol, placement)`; `ComparePlacement::SamePercentScale` preserves the former behaviour. ([#999](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/999), [#962](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/962))
- The indicator templates menu has Indicators and Templates pages for one-click study additions and saved-template application. Hosts supply starred studies through `TopToolbar::favorite_indicators` and handle `TopToolbarAction::AddIndicator`. Save indicator template remains available, and empty pages explain how to fill them. ([#1004](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1004))

- Alt+Shift+Left and Home navigate to the first bar; Alt+Shift+Right and End return to the latest, preserving zoom. Period, Alt+D and Alt+Shift+E request Open layout, Data window and session switching through `ShortcutMap`; text fields keep their keys. The demo scrolls its saved-layout chooser and toggles SAMPLE between regular and extended hours, including from its initial 24/7 session. Continuous markets retain their session. ([#983](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/983))
- The drawing bar's text group and the demo command search offer the image, post and idea drawings in a content section. Placing or double-clicking one opens an editor for its content, kept in the drawing's text: an image drawing shows the picture at that address, fitted inside its frame, through the image loaders the host installs, with an empty, loading or failed state instead of sample art, and the post and idea cards show the supplied author or title on the first line and the text after it, growing to fit. The demo installs the PNG and JPEG file loaders. ([#985](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/985))
- The table drawing is offered with the text and note tools and in the demo command search. Placing or double-clicking a table opens a cell editor that adds and removes rows and columns; the cells are saved in the drawing's text, one row per line and one tab-separated cell per column, and the table paints them with its first row as the header and each column as wide as its widest cell, instead of fixed sample cells. ([#984](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/984))
- The drawing bar's projection group and the demo command search offer the ghost feed tool, which places and edits its path through the existing multi-point anchors. ([#986](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/986))
- The drawing context menu and the object tree row menu move a drawing to the price pane or another existing pane with `Chart::move_drawing_to_pane`, as one undoable edit that keeps its anchors and style; `Chart::drawing_pane` reports the pane drawing it and `SourceItem::with_panes` lists the choices of a tree row. Migration: `SourceItem` literals add `panes: Vec::new()`. ([#977](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/977))
- The drawing context menu offers an alert on the price crossing a drawing with price levels, and `FloatingSelectionToolbar::set_alert_available` adds an Add alert button. `Drawing::alert_levels` evaluates a line, a horizontal level, a rectangle's top and bottom, a position's entry, target and stop and each visible Fibonacci retracement or extension level at a bar; `Drawing::alert_source` and `alert_level` name a level by the drawing's stored identity, so alerts keep their level after a save and restore. The demo lists every level as an alert source and evaluates them from the current drawing geometry. ([#980](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/980))
- Interval entries can be hidden and restored from the menu without changing the selected interval. Hidden entries are unpinned and persist through host-owned `TimeframePreferences`, including across source changes. Migration: `TimeframePreferences` literals add `hidden_timeframes: Vec::new()` or use `..Default::default()`; older saved preferences default to no hidden entries. ([#974](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/974))
- `Chart::set_bar_limit` optionally keeps only the newest bars of each series, including feed history, streaming ticks and retained replay history. The default remains unlimited. `remove_oldest_bars` returns evicted bars explicitly. Replay eviction also drops the matching finer-source slots. Studies restart their warm-up on the retained window, drawings keep their timestamps and the viewport keeps its time position where possible. Full windows pause feed scroll-back until the limit is raised or cleared; markers, raw trades and host data retain their independent lifetimes. ([#945](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/945))

- `Chart::forget_symbol` releases an inactive symbol's drawings, markers and storage reader without deleting host storage. Pending local storage edits must finish first. Symbol changes no longer cache empty drawing managers, marker lists or inactive empty storage readers; pending drawing sync notifications are retained. ([#949](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/949))

- The demo quick search runs a sample backtest on completed synthetic bars, marks equity at their closes and opens the strategy report below the chart. Reports can be replaced or cleared; selecting a trade restores its symbol and interval, scrolls to its bars and highlights its span. Entry and exit markers share the signals service with the API samples, including in newly added chart slots. Trades CSV is saved or downloaded by the host. Strategy execution, market data and storage remain host-owned. ([#225](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/225))

- The documentation site at `/egui-charts/docs/` brings installation, first-chart and feature guides together with the all-feature Rust API reference at `/egui-charts/docs/api/`. Existing guides are included directly, Rust samples compile in CI, and the searchable guide follows system light/dark preferences with responsive navigation, installation steps and feature choices. The demo URLs are unchanged. ([#929](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/929))
- Study menus publish source preselection through `Chart::take_indicator_source_request`, read alert values from displayed price-based elements, and pin studies and overlay volume to shared right, left, additional or hidden scales with `StudyScale::Shared`. Shared groups keep their pane's units and participate in scale fitting. Interval ranges also apply to custom durations and trade-count families through `StudyIntervalRange`. Migration: exhaustive `StudyScale` matches handle `Shared`; `StudySettings` literals add `interval_range: Default::default()`, while older saved settings retain their exact hidden intervals. `IndicatorDialog::open_for_chart` offers price fields and chart study outputs as sources, with optional preselection for adding a study on another study. Added studies retain their source and edited parameters; unavailable outputs are rejected and studies without a source input cannot silently ignore a preselection. Host-supplied source inputs keep their own validation, and rejected inputs show an error without closing the dialog. Long source captions truncate within the configuration panel. Favorites appear first in each matching indicator list. The demo passes chart sources through toolbar, shortcut and study-menu opens. The Add control stays reachable in narrow windows. The demo routes study actions to its alert service, clipboard and selected object-tree row. ([#850](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/850))

- `Primitive::draw_merged` receives the chart's physical-pixel merge factor and `PrimitiveView::point_groups` supplies aligned source ranges for custom aggregation, with no groups for empty views; existing renderers keep their `draw` behavior. Hovered added series and mixed study/series panes follow `hovered_series_on_top` with timestamp-aware hit testing and visual ordering. Cached merge groups also supply exact visible price ranges without scanning every bar each frame. The demo saves encoded PNG bytes directly, with optional crosshair inclusion. ([#834](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/834))

- `Chart::set_volume_formatter` formats volume readouts, widget-only volume axis tags and volume-formatted studies; `set_series_formatter` overrides one bar series' raw prices while relative scales keep the percentage formatter. Chart price and percentage hooks also reach OHLC legends and tooltips, and image exports retain the hooks. `Indicator::set_value_formatter` lets configured studies retain host formatting. Migration: `Legend` literals add `formatters: ValueFormatters::default()` or use `Legend::new`; `StudyValue` literals add `formatted: None`; `render_tooltip_with_options` takes `ValueFormatters::default()` as its final argument for the existing formatting. Hosts supply numeric separators and currency conventions through their formatters. ([#203](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/203))

- Pointer events include hidden added series and their payloads. Unchanged whitespace slots emit no repeated move events, while changes to their host payloads remain observable. `Chart::set_pointer_kind` lets host input integrations identify pens, which egui does not distinguish from touch. `None` restores automatic mouse/touch detection. The demo API samples render a host legend and its tooltip from the saved event alone. ([#829](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/829))

- `ui::strategy_report::StrategyReportPanel` renders a replaceable host report with summary metrics, equity and drawdown curves, a virtualized trade list and run properties. It returns original-index trade selection intents and valid-trade CSV for the host, and draws no controls without a report. Fill prices and timestamps retain their precision; undefined metrics and overflowing percentages display as N/A. `StrategyPerformance` adds largest winning/losing trades and consecutive win/loss runs in exit order; `StrategyReport::comparison` computes fee-free buy-and-hold and bar-resolution MAE/MFE from host source bars, counting invalid input. `equity_curve` shares curve validation with the view. The backtesting example opens the standalone report with `ui` and retains its headless workflow. Chart navigation and terminal mounting remain host integration work. ([#225](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/225))

- `localization::set_translator` installs a host-owned UI text lookup for one egui context; `clear_translator` restores English. Toolbars, menus, dialogs, panels, shared controls and accessibility names resolve text before layout, including when the language changes while a dialog is open. Missing translations retain the crate's existing English text. Searches accept translated labels and original English names. `localization::text` shares the lookup with host controls, and `format_text` substitutes named message values after translation without interpreting braces inside those values. Hosts supply other languages and fonts; chart date and number formatters remain independent. ([#204](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/204))

- Standalone examples cover custom studies, timestamped drawing save/load, a host-run backtest, host script evaluation and UTC chart synchronization across intervals. The historical examples share one generated OHLCV helper, and existing CI checks compile every declared example. The demo retains its execution-based history. Data feeds, durable storage, strategy execution and scripting runtimes remain host-owned. ([#249](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/249))

- docs.rs builds all features and labels optional modules with their feature requirements. The builder guide is published in rustdoc and covers history, streaming, custom studies, drawing persistence, chart synchronization and host strategy and script integration. Study descriptions appear on their public types, and missing documentation is linted in `studies`, `drawings`, `widget` and `ext`. ([#248](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/248))

- A compact `frame_time --regression <scenario>` suite covers three history sizes, smoothed candles, tick and new-bar frames with and without studies, batch study calculation and 500 drawings. Pull-request CI compares Cachegrind instruction deltas against the same workloads compiled with the base revision and rejects growth above 5%, subtracting setup and warm-up. The existing bare-chart check and baseline are unchanged. ([#246](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/246))
- `Datafeed::options_volume` optionally streams complete call/put contract-volume snapshots at each strike for an underlying and an inclusive expiry range. Existing feeds supply none by default. `OptionsVolumeProfile::new` subscribes explicitly with a host repaint callback and draws the figures on the price pane, calls and puts in separate colours, with lengths relative to the largest combined row total. Rows are centered on strike multiples of the chosen price height; coarser rows sum nearby strikes without spreading their volume. Snapshots replace earlier totals, empty snapshots clear the profile, and dropping the last study clone deactivates its sink. `VolumeProfile::from_options` exposes the same grouping for host calculations, with calls in `up()` and puts in `down()`. The demo's “Options volume profile” quick-search action connects a live synthetic options feed. Options data and expiry selection remain host-owned; recreate the study to change its query. Profiles hide when chart metadata names another underlying; the demo retains options queries across symbol changes. ([#550](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/550))
- `Datafeed::metrics`, `metric_history` and `subscribe_metric` expose host-published instrument metrics with names, units and value formats; existing feeds offer none by default. `MetricStudy` draws each series in its own pane, holding the latest point at or before each chart bar without looking ahead, and its output can feed another study. Live corrections refresh both the metric and its consumers through the existing study subscription path. `IndicatorDialog::set_metric_source` lists the current instrument's metrics and ignores old replies after a source change. The demo supplies synthetic five-minute open interest; external metrics remain host-owned. Migration: `StudyDataRequest` literals add `metric: None` for price histories or the host's metric key for metric histories; saved requests default to price histories. Direct hosts convert `MetricPoint` into `Bar` for `Chart::set_study_data`. ([#547](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/547))

- `studies::helpers` exposes scalar-series simple, exponential (first-value and SMA-seeded), Wilder and weighted averages, rolling sum, highest, lowest, population standard deviation, change and crossing tests. `studies::Stepper` lets custom studies resume after a changed last bar or appended bars, including without the `studies` feature. Built-in studies share these definitions while retaining their seeding and reference outputs; the driver example checks both update cases against a fresh calculation. ([#545](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/545))
- `data::ReplayClock` merges trades, quotes and depth for multiple symbols by timestamp and sequence, advances at a host-selected speed and seeks depth from periodic snapshots. `ReplayEvent::push_to` uses the live feed path; `Sink::reset_replay` clears future bars, order flow, streaming study values and their heartbeat on rewind. `RecordingWriter` and `RecordingReader` persist compact versioned binary events through host-owned streams, preserving nanosecond times, ids and conditions. See `docs/order-flow-replay.md` for routing, recording and seek integration. ([#240](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/240))
- Trade-based `Timeframe::Volume`, `Range` and `Delta` intervals build bars directly from executions and appear in the interval menu, subject to the source interval list. Volume and absolute signed delta split fills exactly at the threshold; raw fills stay intact. Typed interval changes also update the demo interval button. Range is measured in instrument ticks and closes on the first real fill reaching it, with possible overshoot across skipped prices. Parse `100v`, `10r` or `100delta`; trade-based bars keep exact opening times and have no countdown or time-based resampling. Migration: exhaustive `Timeframe` and `TimeframeUnit` matches handle the new variants. ([#239](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/239))
- `Sink::push_order_snapshot` and `push_order_event` accept sequenced resting orders, modifications, cancellations and executions. `Chart::order_queue` exposes queue priority, the largest order at a price and levels filtered by minimum order size; the usual aggregated book and depth history continue to work. Known execution trades carry `Tick::resting_order_id`. Gaps, invalid events and the 10,000-order per-side limit require a fresh snapshot, with bounded buffering during resync. ([#555](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/555))
- `Datafeed::depth_history` optionally pages recorded depth through `DepthRequest` and `DepthPage`. Charts with a depth history load past columns and request older pages when visible time reaches the oldest depth column, preserve live columns on overlap, retain time gaps and stop at the end of history or when a page adds no older column. Depth times whose cadence boundary exceeds the nanosecond range are rejected without overflowing. Feeds without recorded depth keep their existing behaviour. ([#554](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/554))
- `Tick::with_aggressor_order_id` identifies fills of one aggressive order. `Chart::combined_trades` and `data::combine_trades` expose their retained size, first and last prices, volume-weighted price and fill count; fills without an id stay separate, and raw trades continue to feed per-price volume. ([#553](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/553))
- The factory adds "Quoted Spread" (last, arithmetic mean and maximum in instrument ticks), "Book Size" (last synchronized bid/ask sizes and their difference over a level count or price distance), "Trade Rate" (bar total, rolling and instant rates for execution count or volume) and "Levels Traded Through" (absolute bid/ask movement in ticks, per bar or rolling seconds). Each has a separate pane. `Indicator::order_flow_input` connects their bounded `OrderFlowInput` before quotes and depth are coalesced; executions use the feed's deduplicated historical/live join. Missing streams stay missing, book loss clears its current values and breaks the movement path, and heartbeats expire idle rates. Hosts can also push events through the input handle. Changed aggregation inputs start a new source; charts replay retained executions for newly added rates without resetting other live sources or their idle clock. Clearing a source or evicting its oldest bar also clears earlier cached plot values. Shared sources receive each event once. Heartbeats expire rates after queued trades are merged and expire rolling inside-market movement without consuming event capacity; newly attached rates keep that idle clock. See [order-flow integration](docs/order-flow.md) for definitions and retention limits. Order counts require host-side fill grouping using explicit aggressor order ids; Trade Rate counts raw executions. ([#552](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/552))
- `studies::{TradeProfileRanges, trade_profiles}` builds bid/ask and delta profiles from exact footprint rows over host-supplied session ranges. Profiles retain unknown volume, developing POC, value area and local high/low volume nodes. `chart::overlays::render_trade_profiles` draws them on the price pane, and `ui::trade_profile_menu` merges neighbouring profiles or splits one at a selected bar boundary.
