The footprint demo exposes these commands for the last clicked bar and its profile. Session boundaries and historical/streaming trades remain host-owned. ([#241](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/241))
- `Chart::set_liquidity_heatmap` draws retained texture tiles from the feed's `DepthHistory`, with logarithmic per-column contrast, sampled bid/ask paths and a current-book size column. New or changed columns use partial uploads; expired columns release their slots and tiles stay within the backend's texture limit. The demo menu enables it with execution bubbles. `HeatmapStrip::add_cell` now normalizes its cells and `volume_weighted` uses volume for gradient intensity or solid opacity, and solid cells without delta stay transparent. ([#235](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/235))

- `VisibleAveragePrice` ("Visible Average Price") averages the selected price source, close by default, over the visible bars and follows pan, zoom and resize before the price scale is fitted. `Indicator::set_visible_range` supplies the end-exclusive source range and is forwarded by configured studies. `UpDownVolume` ("Up/Down Volume") sums host-supplied intrabar volume by candle direction, using the previous intrabar close for dojis, with up volume above zero, down volume below and a delta marker coloured by the prevailing direction. The study declares intrabars through `Indicator::data_requests` and consumes `update_data`; connected feeds load and subscribe automatically, while hosts supplying bars directly use `Chart::study_data_requests` and `set_study_data`. Missing histories stay unavailable. Subsecond and tick-count charts have no lower-timeframe request. The library does not fetch a market data feed. ([#176](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/176))
- `Seasonality` adds its yearly monthly-change heatmap, including the developing month and the monthly average, population standard deviation and positive percentage. Its final projection reaches the next month boundary in future whitespace through `Indicator::projections` and `StudyProjection`, with fractional time-axis endpoints so missing dates do not lengthen the box. Projected bands participate in selection and autoscaling, including future whitespace. Projection statistics still use earlier years only. Configured studies forward both tables and projections. ([#176](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/176))
- `Performance` shows percentage changes over one week, one, three and six months, year to date and one year in a labelled table on the chart. References use the last finite close on or before the target trading date in the instrument's calendar, clamping shorter months to their last day; insufficient history or a zero reference displays N/A. It uses the chart's loaded bars on every interval. `Indicator::table` supplies a `StudyTable` independently of plots, forwarded by configured studies and drawn over the pane and overlay plots, including image exports. Tables scroll within narrow or short panes and stack above the study geometry. ([#568](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/568))

- `ui::widget_bar::MarketHeatmapPanel` renders host-supplied symbols as a grouped weighted treemap, with theme-based diverging percentage colours, values on hover and a selected-symbol intent. Its public entries accept updates without reconstructing the panel. `RightBarIcon::MarketHeatmap` adds its rail entry, and the demo feeds synthetic weights and live quote changes. Layout checks use hand-computed tile areas across groups and updated weights, including large finite inputs. Market data remains host-owned. ([#557](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/557))

- Charts consume `Signals` through `set_signal_source`, rendering host markers, levels and zones in theme colours with hover text and click callbacks. Time anchors follow history replacement, zoom and price-based elements, and priced markers can appear in visible whitespace; symbol and interval changes end old streams immediately, and lagged streams start fresh. The Signals menu hides categories without losing their data. The demo supplies live synthetic analytics through its feed when API samples are enabled. Analytics and external services remain host-owned. ([#381](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/381))
- `Datafeed::price_targets` optionally answers `services::PriceTargets`: high, average and low estimates, analyst count, publication and horizon dates. Unsupported feeds silently show nothing. The Symbol chart setting enables horizon-anchored projections from the last close, price and percentage labels and details on hover; stale symbol replies are ignored. Targets participate in automatic price fitting only where projections reach the visible time range, and their visibility participates in undo and layout copies. Labels use the host price formatter in instrument units on every scale, stay below the chart legend and quote controls in narrow views and retain their hover details when the horizon is offscreen. Hosts leave future whitespace in the logical range to show the horizon. The demo supplies targets for SAMPLE and has a daily-view quick-search action. Migration: `ChartSettingsState` literals add `show_price_targets: false` or use `..Default::default()`; saved settings default it to false. ([#556](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/556))

- Symbol and comparison dialogs consume `services::SymbolSearch`, ignore superseded replies and request another page as the user scrolls. Without a service the supplied symbol list still works. Symbol search keeps recently opened instruments through `Storage` in `StoreKind::RecentSymbols`; failed reads prevent replacement. Empty searches with a category or exchange filter query the host catalog rather than restricting the results to recents. The demo connects its sample instrument catalog and application storage, including quick search. Migration: `SymbolQuery` literals add `offset: 0`; services apply `offset` before `limit`. ([#380](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/380))

- Right-clicking a TPO chart profile offers merging with the previous or next profile and splitting a composite back into its sessions. Commands update `ChartTypeParams::tpo_composites` as one undoable chart action, including neighbours outside the visible range; existing chart settings save the result. Hit testing shares the renderer's placement and row widths, including profiles clipped at the plot's left edge. Split extended-hours profiles can be merged across trading days. Migration: exhaustive `ContextMenuAction` matches handle `MergeTpoPrevious`, `MergeTpoNext` and `SplitTpoProfile`; `ChartMenus` has already applied these actions before returning them. ([#106](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/106))

- Studies declare source, colour, interval and symbol inputs alongside numbers, choices and toggles, with defaults retained separately from edited values. The settings dialog uses these declarations for editing, validation and resetting; `CustomIndicator::with_inputs` supplies validated inputs to a host calculation. `ConfiguredStudy` exposes a source, plot offset and calculation symbol/interval for single-series studies. Price sources include open, high, low, close, hl2, hlc3 and ohlc4; `StudySource::Study` selects another study's undisplaced output by stable chart id and column. The chart calculates dependencies before their consumers, including hidden sources, and refreshes them after edits, live bars, undo and export. Substituting a source retains the input bars' colours and host payloads. `Chart::set_indicator_source` rejects missing plots and cycles. Migration: `StudyInput` literals add `default` or use `StudyInput::new(...).with_default(...)`; exhaustive `InputValue` and `InputKind` matches handle `Source`, `Color`, `Timeframe` and `Symbol`. Hosts restoring studies into a different chart remap stored source ids to the newly created studies. The demo stores template source positions and remaps them after loading; layout restoration uses its saved study-id map for source references as well as drawings. Single-study clipboard paste rejects chart-local study output references, which need host remapping. Existing numeric source choices remain accepted by studies that previously used them. ([#168](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/168), [#174](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/174))
- `Indicator::data_requests` and `update_data` let studies consume additional host histories. The chart resolves, loads and subscribes to requested symbols and intervals through its connected `Datafeed`; hosts supplying bars directly use `Chart::study_data_requests`, `set_study_data` and `study_data_error`. Requested bars align by their calendar slots even when stamped inside them, missing slots leave gaps, and resuming a paused feed reloads missed history. Higher-interval calculations appear after the source bar closes; turning off “Wait for interval close” exposes the newest developing value on the latest chart bar. Advance/Decline Ratio divides advancing by declining issues, Advance/Decline Line accumulates their difference, and Cumulative Volume Index accumulates advancing minus declining volume. These studies use the close of each host-supplied market series, leave gaps for missing pairs and zero ratio denominators, and keep their running total across gaps. Hosts map the editable advancing/declining symbol keys to their own data service; no market data service is included. ([#175](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/175))
- Visible, fixed, session, periodic, anchored and auto-anchored volume profile studies draw horizontal up/down histograms with total-based POC, value area, VAH and VAL. Visible profiles use only the current viewport; saved fixed and anchored times survive prepended history, and session/calendar profiles include the whole exchange trading period. Inputs set row count or price height, value area and volume display and retain their declared defaults for resetting; the host supplies bars and instrument session metadata. `VolumeProfile::from_bars` also computes distributions directly from host-supplied finer bars. Studies and drawings share the same calculation and rendering, including negative prices and flat bars. Migration: the old per-bar volume-bin columns of `VolProfileVisible` and `VolProfileFixed` are removed; read `Indicator::volume_profiles(visible_range)` for source spans, price boundaries, total/up/down volumes and POC/value area. `VolProfileFixed::with_range` and `VolProfileAnchored::with_anchor` take UTC times. ([#167](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/167))
- Volume profile drawings can use a fixed price height per row through Inputs or `VolumeProfileConfig::with_row_size`. Rows align to multiples of that height and grow at the edges on new highs and lows, reusing completed-bar volume. Equal rows over the span remain the default and old saved drawings retain it. Nonpositive or nonfinite heights are rejected; a span needing more than 10,000 fixed rows is not drawn. ([#359](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/359))
- Volume profile drawings offer Total, Up / down and Delta displays. Rows split volume by bar direction (unchanged closes count as up), retain total-based POC and value area, and follow a forming bar when its direction changes. Display mode and direction colours are editable in Inputs and persist with the drawing; older files retain the Total default. ([#565](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/565))
- The `drawings` feature separates drawing tools, geometry, hit tests, history and persistence from `widget`; `ui` enables it, so default builds retain their tools. Widget-only charts keep series, studies, markers, primitives and keyboard action variants. `config` exports `DrawingToolType`, `DrawingInteractionMode`, `ParseToolTypeError` and `FontWeight`; their existing drawing imports remain available with `drawings`. Migration: applications using drawing APIs with `default-features = false` add `drawings` alongside `widget`. Drawing keyboard actions do nothing without the feature; Delete still removes a selected study. ([#449](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/449))
- `DrawingToolRegistry` registers stable host ids, names, anchor counts and shared `DrawingRenderer` implementations. `Drawing::custom` retains the id and JSON settings; the manager supplies multi-click placement, coordinate projection, handles, undo and storage, dispatches rendering and authoritative hit tests, and applies the renderer's settings editor through `show_custom_settings`. Unknown ids remain stored until their implementation is registered. `ChartBuilder::with_drawing_registry` installs the catalogue before construction. Built-in availability filters registry picker entries and rejects disabled creation; clone the registry into `DrawingToolbar::registry` to hide or disable its drawing rows, glyphs, measure button and shortcuts. Hosts build custom pickers from `custom_tools` and activate entries with `set_custom_tool`. Migration: `Drawing` literals add `custom_tool: None` and `custom_settings: serde_json::Value::Null`, or use `Drawing::new`. `DrawingRenderer` now receives the current drawing and chart view; implementations provide `render`, `hit_test` and optional `settings`, while the manager supplies anchor handles. Its existing rendering-module import is retained. ([#152](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/152))
- `Chart::create_drawing` creates validated drawings from UTC times and prices using `Drawing` for style, locking, layer and study ownership. `drawing_points` and `set_drawing_points` round-trip and edit those anchors; the existing `drawing_point_at` and `drawing_point_time` conversions are public. `DrawingManager::add_drawing` validates chart-coordinate creation and records undo. Drawings add `selectable`, `saveable`, `listed` and a host-defined `group`; `listed_drawings`, `group_drawings`, `set_group` and `select_group` support host object lists and group actions. Non-saveable drawings stay out of chart saves and persistence notifications, including undo, while remaining visible. Existing anchored text and notes use `screen_anchor` pane fractions. Hosts own durable storage and retain or remap study ids. Migration: `Drawing` literals add the four fields, or use `Drawing::new`; older saved drawings default to selectable, saveable and listed, without a group. ([#154](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/154))

- `ZigZag::pivots` exposes the source-bar indices, prices and high/low kinds used by its drawn swings; the last pivot remains provisional. With `drawings` and `studies`, `AutoDrawing` registers trend line, parallel channel, Fibonacci retracement and extension, and pitchfork studies, each with Zig Zag deviation and depth inputs. Trend lines join the latest same-kind pivots; channels pass their parallel through the intervening pivot; retracements use the latest confirmed swing and extensions project that swing from the moving last extreme. `Indicator::drawings` exposes their results, and the study renderer and hit test reuse the existing drawing geometry and pane mapping. Updating host bars replaces the anchors. Anchor and Fibonacci level prices participate in autoscaling even when the anchors are outside the visible bars. Custom presentation wrappers forward `drawings`, as `ConfiguredStudy` does. ([#178](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/178))

- `FibonacciConfig::logarithmic` measures retracement and trend-based extension levels in logarithmic price, with the same prices used for lines, labels, bands and hit testing in every price scale. It defaults to off, including in older saved drawings, and the Fibonacci properties page edits it. Nonpositive anchors retain arithmetic levels. Extensions project the logarithmic swing from the third anchor. Level labels sit above their lines and omit overlapping labels when levels crowd together. Migration: `FibonacciConfig` literals add `logarithmic: false` or use `..Default::default()`. ([#564](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/564))
- `Trading::show` draws fills in the chart's symbol as buy/sell arrows outside their bars. Fills on one side of a bar share a mark with up to ten chevrons; optional labels give total quantity and weighted average price, and crowded neighbouring labels group together. Hover lists every fill's side, quantity, price and time. Marker labels retain the chart background for contrast. `Trading::poll` announces fills, partial-fill progress and rejection reasons; the host plays requested execution sounds. ([#551](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/551))
- Trading lines have joined quantity, P&L and close cells, separate reverse and dotted TP/SL controls, teal/orange brackets and connectors. Working orders have outlined price tags; projected orders offer Buy/Sell submit buttons. Drag previews remain until confirmation or discard. Hidden layers retain the account context needed to value brackets and avoid duplicate exits. The legend offers filled quote buttons, a quantity calculator and a ticket dropdown. Bid and Ask labels stay visible on either price axis. Trading notifications wrap in narrow windows.
- The Trading settings tab controls positions, orders, executions and their labels, P&L units, reverse controls, quote labels, instant placement, notifications and host-provided execution sounds. Settings persist with `ChartSettingsState`; omitted fields use their defaults. Migration: handle `SettingsTab::Trading`, `TradingLineAction::Exit` and `QuoteSide::Ticket` in exhaustive matches. The ticket intent opens a ticket even with instant placement enabled. `Trading::one_click` moves to `Trading::display.instant_orders`; `TradingSettings::show_buy_sell_btns` is replaced by `StatusLineOptions::show_quote_boxes`. `TradingLineOptions` literals add `show_orders`, `show_positions`, `submit`, `reverse`, `take_profit`, `stop_loss`, `pnl` and `bracket_pnl`; use `submit` only for projected orders and handle `TradingLines::submitted`. `TradingLines::outlined` identifies outlined tags. `UiColors` adds `take_profit` and `stop_loss`; `Toast` adds optional `trading` details. Brokers push an order before its fills and preserve `OrderRequest::position_bracket` on position exits. The paper account keeps those exits from increasing exposure after a close or reversal and rejects exposure beyond the balance at its configured leverage.
- The order ticket is a panel beside the chart. Its header holds the symbol, a menu with the presets and one-click trading (the same `Trading::display.instant_orders` setting), and a close button above an Order and DOM switch. The Order tab shows the bid and ask as Sell and Buy halves with the spread on the seam, the chosen side filled; Market, Limit, Stop and Stop limit tabs; the price with its distance in ticks from the ask or bid, or the distance itself after the swap button; the quantity in units or as an amount with the order's value, and on market orders the risk at the stop loss in percent of the account's balance; collapsible exits with their risk to reward ratio, each exit switched on and off and entered as a price, ticks, a percentage or money while it keeps its price; the time in force; and the order's estimated margin against the account's balance, leverage, tick value, trade value, reward and risk, above a two-line send button in the side's colour. `BracketMode::Money` enters an exit as the profit or loss at it, `TimeInForce::{Week, Month}` request expiry at the end of the week or the month from brokers that advertise them, and `TradingRules::leverage` estimates the margin an order needs. Migration: call `Trading::show_ticket` before laying out the chart, since `Trading::show` no longer draws the ticket; exhaustive `BracketMode` matches handle `Money`. Risk sizing takes a percentage of the account's balance instead of an amount of money. The broker contract accepts one exit pair per order.
- The ticket's DOM tab is a depth ladder of the chart's order book: the bid and ask sizes at each price as tinted cells, prices above the last trade in the sell colour and below it in the buy colour, the price of the last trade marked, a level's order count and latest observed size change on hover, and the totals of the sizes in view. A profile behind the prices sums the feed trades retained by the chart; each level's tooltip separates volume traded at bid, at ask and of unknown side. The latest account fill for the symbol is outlined separately from the last trade. Clicking a bid or ask cell places a limit order on the passive side of the market and a stop order beyond it, the user's working orders show as `LIMIT` and `STOP` tags in the margin of their side at their price, and dragging a tag moves its order and right-clicking it cancels it, each after confirmation. Orders at the same price share a tag with a chooser for the order to drag or cancel. The ladder centres on the current bid/ask midpoint, or the last trade without quotes, and scrolls, keeps prices fixed through a press or drag, and its lock keeps it from re-centring. Short panels scroll to the controls. Below it, the user cancels every buy or every sell, sees the position and its profit or loss, flattens, cancels all orders and reverses, buys and sells the ladder's quantity at market and sets the time in force. Bulk cancellations also wait for confirmation. A new order from the ladder waits for confirmation unless one-click trading is on. ([#236](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/236))
- `Chart::snapshot` and `restore` keep the symbol, interval, chart type, display and interaction settings, study inputs and styles, pane order and collapse state, manual scales, view and timestamped drawings. `SavedLayout` writes version 2 documents and reads version 1 with defaults for the newly saved fields. Unregistered studies fail before saving. The layout menu lists, opens, saves, renames and deletes layouts through `Storage`, offers debounced autosave and handles Ctrl+S (Cmd+S on macOS) in the demo. Storage requests run in order. Migration: build snapshots through `Chart::snapshot`; literals of `ChartSnapshot` and `SavedPane` need the new fields, and `SavedPane` is cloned rather than copied. ([#197](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/197))
- `Chart::set_storage` saves and restores timestamped drawings per symbol through the host service, including edits made while a read is pending or before switching symbols. Unreadable documents are kept intact and failed writes retain the drawings in memory and report one error per run of failures. `SettingsStore` restores versioned `UserSettings` for the theme, last symbol and interval, drawing favourites and open layout, then saves after a one-second debounce. Its exit flush finishes available replies before writing the latest settings. The demo implements `Storage` with eframe persistence on native and web builds and imports its earlier drawings. ([#198](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/198))
- `ui::trading::BrokerPicker` is the dialog in which the user chooses a broker: a grid of the host's `BrokerTile`s, each with a name, an optional logo, subtitle or rating, a featured badge and a favourite star the user toggles, after the built-in paper account, with a search and a "Need a broker?" link that returns `BrokerPickerAction::NeedBroker`. Picking a tile shows its connect card, whose Connect button returns `BrokerPickerAction::Connect` with the tile's id for the host to connect `Trading`, such as to a `PaperAccount` for `BrokerTile::PAPER`. `Trading::show_account_panel` shows the account panel in a host panel: a tab named after the broker with a Trade button and minimize and maximize buttons, an account selector that switches between the host's `AccountPanel::accounts` or disconnects, a currency, a settings button, and the account's balance, equity, realised and unrealised profit and loss, margin, available funds and orders margin as the broker reports them. Its Positions, Orders, Order History, Balance History and Trading Journal tabs list what the broker pushes; Order History switches between orders and individual executions, each with its own CSV export, with right-aligned figures, profit and loss in the rising and falling colours, the chart's symbol highlighted, a column chooser and an export as comma-separated values; clicking a symbol returns `AccountPanelAction::SelectSymbol`, and a hovered position or order offers to close it or cancel it after confirmation, or to change an order's quantity, prices and brackets. `Trading::disconnect` and `is_connected` end and report the connection. The demo's Trade button and Buy and Sell buttons open the picker until an account is connected, after which Trade shows or hides the account panel under the chart, and its settings button resets the paper account. ([#220](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/220), [#216](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/216))
- `services::PaperAccount` is a ready-made `Broker` that simulates an account. The host chooses its currency and starting balance, and with `leverage` and `commission` the leverage its margin follows and the commission each fill pays as a fraction of its value. Fed the last prices with `set_price`, live or replayed, it fills market orders at the last price and limit and stop orders at the first price that reaches them, a limit at that price or better, works a triggered stop-limit order on as a limit order, turns an order's take profit and stop loss into exit orders that cancel each other, nets one position per symbol at its average price, and books the realised profit or loss and the commission of each fill to the balance. It reports the equity, the realised profit or loss, the margin of the positions and of the working orders and the available funds, values each position at the last price with its trade and market value, leverage and margin, and keeps the most recent 1,000 finished orders, executions, balance changes and journal entries, which it pushes again on every connection; `reset` returns it to the starting balance. `Account` gains `realized_pnl`, `margin`, `orders_margin` and `available_funds`, `Position` gains `last_price`, `trade_value`, `market_value`, `leverage`, `margin` and `expiration`, and `Order` gains `fill_price`, `placed` and `closed`, all optional, and `AccountEvent::Balance` and `AccountEvent::Journal` carry a `BalanceChange` and a `JournalEntry` for brokers that keep history; the terminal skips pushes with non-finite values. The demo trades the same paper account live and during replay, feeding every traversed replay close, including when playback skips several bars. During replay the order ticket uses that close instead of the live bid and ask. ([#221](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/221))
- The top toolbar ends with Publish, Trade and a host call to action, as the right end of the default layout does. Publish, a light bulb with a plus, emits `TopToolbarAction::Publish` for the host to capture the chart with `Chart::export_image` and share it through its own service; `Control::Publish` hides it. Trade, an outlined pill, emits `TopToolbarAction::ToggleTrading`, which `Trading::toggle_ticket` answers by opening the order ticket for the chart symbol or closing it; it shows only while `TopToolbarState::can_trade` reports an attached broker and `Control::Trading` leaves it visible. `TopToolbar::call_to_action` takes an id and label for a filled pill after the host actions that returns `TopToolbarAction::Host(id)`; it is `None` by default and then nothing is drawn. The demo copies a published image to the clipboard, turns Trade on with its paper account and keeps it available during replay, and reports its Upgrade call to action. Migration: exhaustive matches on `TopToolbarAction` handle or ignore `Publish` and `ToggleTrading`. ([#844](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/844))
- A quote contract feeds the watchlist, the symbol details panel, the symbol header and the order ticket. `model::Quote` holds a symbol's last price and previous close, from which `change` and `change_percent` follow, its update time, session volume, best bid and ask, and latest pre-market or post-market trade (`ExtendedHoursQuote`). The host streams quotes through the new `services::Quotes` service: `WatchlistPanel::set_service` attaches it, and `WatchlistPanel::poll`, called each frame, subscribes to the symbols of the active list and the selected symbol, subscribes again with a new stream when they change or the stream lagged, which stops the previous one, and applies the pushed quotes. `WatchlistPanel::quote` reads a symbol's quote back for `SymbolDetailsPanel::set_quote`, `SymbolHeader::quote` and the new `Trading::set_quote`, with which the ticket enters a market buy at the ask and a market sell at the bid instead of the last price. `WatchlistPanel::set_price_decimals` sets a symbol's price decimals. The demo streams its watchlist through a quote service. ([#222](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/222))
- The `order_flow` bench pushes the order flow of a busy instrument into a chart's feed: a synthetic book of 200 levels a side with 2,000 trades a second, each followed by its depth delta, 6 orders added or cancelled near the touch and a quote, 18,000 messages a second, drawing a frame for every 1/60 s of market time. It checks that the chart's book matches the generator's and prints the messages pushed per second, the frame time, the heap allocations per message and the heap the chart holds, with the depth history sampled every second and with one filled to all its resolutions: `cargo bench --bench order_flow`. Over 300 s of market time it pushes about 6.5 million messages a second with 0.02 allocations per message, all from the per-frame trade queue, and holds 7.8 MB, or 22 MB with a filled depth history. The demo's live feed pushes the same synthetic book: each trade takes the liquidity in its way, the book quotes again around the new last price, orders come and go near the touch, and the quote is the book's best bid and ask with their sizes. ([#228](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/228), [#229](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/229))
- `Chart::set_depth_history` keeps the order book the feed sends over time in a `data::DepthHistory`, read with `Chart::depth_history`: columns of the resting size per price row on a fixed time grid, for liquidity heatmaps. `DepthHistoryConfig` sets the cadence (1 second by default), the rows kept per side nearest the touch (100), the ticks grouped per row (1) and the columns kept at each of 8 resolutions (600). The book is sampled as feed updates arrive, at the end of each period, so a column does not depend on the frame rate, a book that did not change for several periods gives one column spanning them, and an update stamped more than a minute after the feed's time samples nothing. When the newest columns overflow, the two oldest merge into one spanning both, each row's size averaged over their spans, so the history spans at least 42 hours in about 15 MB with the defaults. `OrderBook::is_crossed` reports a best bid above the best ask. ([#229](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/229))
- Trades carry the sale conditions the exchange reports: `Tick::conditions` holds `TradeConditions` flags (`OFF_EXCHANGE`, `AUCTION`, `ODD_LOT`, `OUT_OF_SEQUENCE`, `EXTENDED_HOURS`), set with `Tick::with_conditions` and tested with `intersects`, so studies and overlays can leave out prints such as off-exchange or auction trades. ([#228](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/228))
- Horizontal axes other than time, such as option strikes and yield curve maturities. A `scales::HorizontalScale` decides which keys of the horizontal axis are labelled, how they read and how the crosshair label reads, and `Chart::set_horizontal_scale` sets one; the time-based chart uses the same trait through its `TimeFormatter`. A key is the `time` of a bar, an instant that sorts as the key does, so price scales, panes, series, the crosshair, markers, price lines and drawings work on any scale, and `scales::key_time` and `scales::time_key` encode numeric keys. `PriceHorizontalScale` labels prices with a set number of decimals, multiples of 100 first, then of 50, 25, 10, 5 and 1, then half prices. `MaturityScale` labels months as `3M`, `2Y` or `2Y6M`, multiples of ten years first, and its `whitespace` bars give the main series a slot every month, so curves added as series are spaced by maturity. On these scales the most significant marks in view are bold. Session breaks, extended-hours shading and the time spans of the measuring tools are drawn on the time scale only, and `Chart::format_time` and the time column of `Chart::export_csv` format a key as the crosshair does. The `volatility_smile` and `yield_curve` examples draw an implied volatility smile by strike and a yield curve by maturity with the public API alone. Migration: `TickMark` literals add `bold`, which the time axis sets for day, month and year marks; `chart::render_crosshair_full` takes a `&dyn HorizontalScale` in place of a `&dyn TimeFormatter`, so pass `&formatter` for a `Box<dyn TimeFormatter>`; `drawings::rendering::ChartView` literals add `time_axis: true`. ([#835](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/835))

- `Chart::set_time_formatter`, `set_price_formatter` and `set_percentage_formatter` take host formatters for the time axis, crosshair, tooltip and price scale labels, and `set_locale` and `set_date_format` set the language of month and weekday names and the `strftime` pattern of crosshair and tooltip dates. A time formatter writes axis marks with `TimeFormatter::format`, which receives the tick mark type, and hover labels and `Chart::format_time` with `format_crosshair`, in the chart's time zone. The price formatter writes crosshair labels and price tags with `PriceFormatter::format` and axis ticks with the new `PriceFormatter::format_tick`, which defaults to `format`, on every price scale in the normal and logarithmic modes; the percentage formatter does the same with the percentage or index value in the percentage and indexed-to-100 modes. Studies keep their own formats. `LocaleTimeFormatter` now writes month and weekday names and the AM/PM marker in its locale's language instead of English and honours `show_seconds`; the localized names come from chrono's locale tables behind the new `locales` cargo feature, on by default, and without it, as in a build with `default-features = false`, they stay English. `TimeFormatterBuilder::with_date_format` and `with_formatter` expose the same options. Migration: `DefaultTimeFormatter` struct literals add `date_format: None` or use `..Default::default()`, `LocaleTimeFormatter` literals add `show_seconds: true` and `date_format: None`, and `chart::price_axis_width` takes the price formatter as a fourth argument; pass `None` for the built-in labels. ([#203](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/203))

- A `Bar` can stand for a single value, hold an empty slot of the time axis, carry colours of its own and carry the host's own data. `Bar::from_value` makes a point of a line, area, baseline or column series without filling OHLC. `Bar::whitespace`, whose values are all NaN (`Bar::is_whitespace`), keeps its slot when a series is loaded or updated, so a gap such as a halted session stays visible and a time of another series has a slot: nothing is drawn there, lines, areas and baselines break across it, the crosshair moves through it without a magnet snap, the legend and tooltip show no values for it, and the last-price line, countdown, previous close and percentage base use the nearest bar with a value. Studies take it as a bar without a value, as they take other non-finite input, price-based chart types and their ATR box sizes skip it, and trades in its slot fill it. `Bar::colors` holds an optional `BarColors`: the body (`color`), border and wick of a candle, the colour of an OHLC bar, a column or a line marker, the colour of the line from the bar to the next on line, step line, area and HLC area charts, and the top and bottom line and fill colours of a baseline segment; a candle border takes its colour when the series draws borders, and the last-value tag takes the bar's `color`. `Bar::payload` holds an `Arc<serde_json::Value>` the chart does not read, which comes back with the bar from `Chart::bars` and in `ChartPointer::bar` and `series_bars`; at an empty slot `ChartPointer::bar` is the whitespace bar. JSON and CSV bars accept a date alone, such as `2024-03-15`, as midnight UTC, the open of its day bar. A JSON bar with missing or `null` values is whitespace, and colours and payloads round-trip through JSON. Resampled bars leave out the colours and payloads of their source bars. Migration: `Bar` literals add `colors: None, payload: None`, or use `Bar::new`; struct updates from a borrowed bar, `Bar { time, ..*bar }`, become `..bar.clone()`, since the new fields are not `Copy`. A bar whose values are all NaN, formerly dropped as invalid, is now kept as whitespace. ([#826](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/826))

- `Chart::export_png` renders the image of `Chart::export_image` on the CPU and returns it encoded as PNG, so a host needs no renderer or GPU readback. `ImageExportOptions::include_crosshair` draws the crosshair where the pointer last placed it over the plots, or where `Chart::set_crosshair_position` or a synchronized chart placed it since, and the export dialog offers it as "Crosshair". Migration: build `ImageExportOptions` with `..Default::default()` where every field was listed. ([#834](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/834))
- Chart options change at runtime: `Chart::set_chart_options` replaces the options that `Chart::chart_options` reads back and keeps the view unless the bar spacing, its bounds or the right offset change. `Chart::set_size` gives the chart an explicit size in points instead of the space `show` is given. `Chart::set_crosshair_position` shows the crosshair at a time and a main-pane price, with the horizontal line and price label at that price. `ChartOptions::hovered_series_on_top` draws the main series or overlay study under the pointer above the others, in the priority a click selects them. Migration: build `ChartOptions` with `..Default::default()` where every field was listed. ([#834](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/834))
- A study's legend row adds a more-actions button after its hide, settings and remove buttons, and the remove button shows a bin. The button, or a secondary click on the row, outlines the row and opens the study's menu, the one a secondary click on the study's plot now opens, in the chart menu's style with its icons and shortcut column. Its rows add an alert on the study's value at the legend's bar, open the indicators dialog, add the study to the dialog's favorites, and open submenus for the visual order, the visibility on intervals (the chart's interval and above, and below, alone, or every interval, kept in `StudySettings::hidden_intervals`), moving the study to a new pane above or below its own, to another pane or to the main pane, and pinning it to the pane's price scale, a new left scale or no scale. Copy, Hide, Remove, Object tree and Settings follow. Context menu rows can open submenus, which open beside their row on the side with room. `ChartMenus` applies the chart's own actions as one undoable user action and returns every action as `ChartContextAction::Study`; `StudyContextMenuAction::AddAlert` carries the boxed `Alert` for the host's alerts service, on the study's output or, for volume, on `AlertSource::Volume`. `Chart::reorder_indicator` takes a `VisualOrder` for a study's place in the draw order of its pane, where the main pane's price series is one of the items and takes clicks before the studies drawn under it, `Chart::set_indicator_scale` and `Indicator::set_scale` pin a study to a `StudyScale`, and `Chart::copy_indicator` and `Chart::paste_indicator` carry a study with its inputs, styles, interval visibility, placement and scale to another chart as clipboard text. Delete and Backspace remove the selected study when no drawing is selected. The demo saves the alert, opens the indicators dialog, the favorites and the object tree with the study selected, and copies and pastes studies with Ctrl+C and Ctrl+V. Adding an indicator does not yet take the study as its source, studies have no shared left scale, and a volume overlay keeps its own band. Migration: `PaneTarget::Pane` uses the stable `PaneId` of an existing pane; `StudyAction` adds `Menu { index, pos }` and is no longer `Eq`, and `PaneInteraction` adds `menu`; exhaustive matches on `StudyAction` and `ChartContextAction` add the new variants, and a host that handled a study's `ChartContextAction::Series` actions handles `ChartContextAction::Study`. ([#850](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/850))
- Primitives draw host content inside the chart. `Chart::attach_primitive` attaches a `Primitive` to the main series, an added bar series or a study, and it draws in the pane of that series on its price scale: behind the series and studies, above them, or above the drawings as well (`PrimitivePass`), inside the price and time axes behind their labels, and as `PriceAxisLabel` and `TimeAxisLabel` tags that the axis labels give way to. Its `autoscale` range joins the fit of the series' scale, its `hit_test` reports the pointer as `PointerTarget::Primitive` and sets the cursor, and its `attached`, `detached` and `data_changed` hooks follow the series, with the egui context to request repaints. `Chart::add_custom_series` adds a series type the host defines: a study supplies the values that the legend, crosshair, last-value tag and autoscale read, and a primitive draws its points in place of the study's plots and hit tests them, selecting the series. Image exports draw the same primitives. `examples/primitives.rs` shades the regular session behind the bars with labels on the time axis, and draws the range of each bar as a custom series in its own pane. ([#830](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/830), [#88](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/88))
- The rest of the widget bar's panel set. `HotlistsPanel` shows the hotlists the host names (such as top gainers) through the new `Datafeed::hotlist`, answered as `HotlistEntry` symbols with their last price, change in percent and optional price decimals, and returns `HotlistsAction::SelectSymbol`. `NotificationsPanel` shows the log the new `services::Notifications` service pushes as `Notification`s (time, title, text and symbol), keeps the most recent 1,000, counts entries not yet shown for the bar's badge and starts over after a lagged stream. `ChatsPanel` shows the rooms and messages the new `services::Chats` service pushes as `ChatEvent`s and posts what the user writes through `Chats::send`; a posted message appears once the host pushes it. `ProductsPanel` lists the host's `Product`s and returns `ProductsAction::Open`. Accounts, rooms, moderation and the products themselves stay with the host. The demo feeds every entry of the bar: synthetic hotlists, a log of its messages and alert triggers with a badge on the Notifications entry, two chat rooms that echo what the user posts, and a launcher of its own panels. ([#845](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/845))
- `ScreenerPanel` shows the rows the feed answers through the new `Datafeed::screen` in a table with the host's column sets (`ScreenerColumnSet` of `ScreenerColumn`s). The user picks a set, adds filters on its columns (`ScreenerFilter`: a column, `ScreenerOp::Above` or `Below`, a value) and sorts by clicking a heading; every change asks again with a `ScreenerQuery` of the set's columns, the filters and the sort, and the feed screens, sorts and limits. `ScreenerRow` carries the symbol and one formatted value per column. The panel returns `ScreenerAction::SelectSymbol`, `ExportCsv` with the shown rows (every field quoted) and `SendToWatchlist` with their symbols. The demo screens its catalog symbols on their synthetic quotes and sends results to a new watchlist. ([#224](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/224))
- `CalendarPanel` shows the economic, earnings, dividend and IPO calendars the feed answers through the new `Datafeed::calendar`, grouped by day with each event's time, country, importance and figures. The user picks the calendar, the days (yesterday, today, tomorrow, or this or next Monday-to-Sunday week, in UTC), the least importance of economic events and, with `CalendarPanel::set_countries`, the countries; every change asks again with a `CalendarQuery` and the feed applies the filters. `CalendarEvent` carries the kind, time, country, title, symbol, `Importance` and the actual, forecast and prior figures already formatted. A company event returns `CalendarAction::SelectSymbol`. The demo answers synthetic releases, earnings, dividends and offerings. ([#244](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/244))
- `NewsPanel` shows the headlines the feed answers through the new `Datafeed::news` for the chart's symbol or the whole market, and `NewsPanel::highlights` shows short summaries under their headlines in the same way. `NewsQuery` carries the symbol (`None` for the market), whether highlights are wanted and a limit, and `NewsStory` the id, publication time, headline, source, text, symbols and link of a story. Each symbol or scope change asks again and only the latest answer shows; a feed keeping the `FeedError::NotSupported` default gets a notice. An opened story shows its text and returns `NewsAction::ShowOnChart` with its time, which the host passes to `Chart::scroll_to_time`, or `NewsAction::OpenUrl`. The demo shows synthetic highlights and headlines for its catalog symbols. ([#243](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/243))
- The widget bar has built-in entries for the full panel set: `RightBarIcon::Watchlist` (the watchlist with the symbol's details) before Alerts and the Object Tree, and a second group at the foot of the bar of `Highlights`, `Screener`, `Hotlists`, `Calendar`, `News`, `Notifications`, `Chats` and `Products`, above the line and Help. `WidgetBar::default` still shows Alerts, the Object Tree and Help; a host adds the entry of each panel whose feed it supplies to `WidgetBar::icons`, and entries draw in `RightBarIcon` order whatever their order in that list. Products, the launcher, draws as a filled round button. New glyphs on the 24 unit grid: `WIDGET_BAR_WATCHLIST`, `WIDGET_BAR_HIGHLIGHTS`, `WIDGET_BAR_SCREENER`, `WIDGET_BAR_HOTLISTS`, `WIDGET_BAR_NEWS`, `WIDGET_BAR_NOTIFICATIONS`, `WIDGET_BAR_CHATS` and `WIDGET_BAR_PRODUCTS`. The demo shows the watchlist through the built-in entry and the data window in the Object Tree, and no longer has a Data Window entry. Migration: `RightBarIcon` is `#[non_exhaustive]`, so a `match` on it adds a wildcard arm.
- Studies can draw price bands as `StudyZone` values, a band between two source bars in the style of one of the study's outputs, returned by `Indicator::zones` and stored per zone instead of per bar. The visible zones of a study count in its price autoscale. `Gaps` draws its gaps as zones, one per extent a gap has held, with "Up gaps" and "Down gaps" outputs for their style, so its memory follows the gaps shown rather than the bars and it shows up to 500 gaps. ([#842](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/842))
- `SeriesStyle` sets a bar series' last value label, its price line's visibility, `PriceLineSource` (the newest bar or the newest bar in view), colour, width and style, a base line at 0% or 100 on percentage and indexed scales, an `AutoscaleProvider` that replaces the range the series contributes to its scale's fit, and the click tolerance of the main series. `Chart::series_style` and `series_style_mut` reach the main series' style and each host series' `SeriesOptions::style`; the chart-wide `show_symbol_last_val` and `show_price_line` switches still hide every label and price line. Host series draw a price line only when asked. Migration: struct literals of `SeriesOptions` set `style` or use `..Default::default()`. ([#832](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/832))
- `AreaStyle::invert_filled_area` fills the Area chart from its line up to the pane top, and turning off `relative_gradient` spreads `top_fill` to `bottom_fill` over the whole price pane instead of between the line's visible extreme and the edge the fill reaches. `ChartTypeParams::columns_base` draws Columns up and down from a price instead of up from the pane bottom. Migration: struct literals of `AreaStyle` and `ChartTypeParams` set the new fields or use `..Default::default()`. ([#832](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/832))
- `LinePlotStyle::line_type` draws the Line, Line with markers, Area and Baseline charts as `LineType::Curved`, a Catmull-Rom spline through every point, or with straight segments. `ChartTypeStyles::crosshair_marker_radius` marks the hovered bar on the line of the line-based charts, ringed in the chart background, and `last_price_animation` pulses at their last price continuously or once per update with `LastPriceAnimation`. Both are off by default. Migration: struct literals of `LinePlotStyle` and `ChartTypeStyles` set the new fields or use `..Default::default()`. ([#832](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/832))
- `ChartTypeStyles::bars` styles the Bars chart: `BarsStyle::open_visible` hides the open tick, and `thin_bars`, on by default, draws bars with the chart's wick width at every zoom level; without it the stroke is half the bar width, at least 1.5 points. Migration: struct literals of `ChartTypeStyles` set `bars` or use `..Default::default()`. ([#832](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/832))
- `CrosshairMode::MagnetClose` snaps the price pane's crosshair to the bar's close only; volume and study panes snap to their values as with `Magnet`. `CrosshairStyle::Hidden` draws neither crosshair lines nor axis labels while legends, the data window and tooltips keep following the pointer. `CrosshairOptions` styles each line with `vert_line_style` and `horz_line_style` and colours each axis label with `vert_label_background_color` (time axis) and `horz_label_background_color` (price axis). Migration: replace `line_style` with both line style fields and `label_background_color` with both background fields; exhaustive matches on `CrosshairMode` and `CrosshairStyle` add the new arms. The settings dialog's single crosshair style sets both lines. ([#832](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/832))
- `ChartConfig::grid_line_style` sets the grid's line style, dotted by default, and `vertical_grid_line_style` overrides it for vertical lines, as `vertical_grid_color` does for colour. Migration: struct literals of `ChartConfig` set both fields or use `..Default::default()`. ([#832](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/832))
- `LineStyle::LargeDashed` and `LineStyle::SparseDotted` add long dashes and widely spaced dots to drawings, series, price lines and their settings menus. `CrosshairLineStyle`, `SessionBreakStyle` and the study `PlotPattern` gain the same two styles. `LineStyle::name` gives each style's menu name. Migration: exhaustive matches on these enums add arms for the two styles. ([#832](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/832))
- Panes are reachable from the API. `Chart::add_pane` creates an empty pane, `remove_pane` removes one with its series and studies, `panes` lists them from top to bottom with `PaneId::MAIN` for the price pane, and `pane_series` and `series_pane` map panes to the series and studies they draw. `move_series_to_pane` places a host series or a study in any pane; below the price pane a host series uses the pane's price scale and draws its line, price lines, markers, legend row and last-value tag there, and crosshair and click events report values in its units, snapping to its close in magnet mode. `move_pane` and `swap_panes` reorder panes, the price pane included, which the pane menu can now also move. Expanded panes share the height by stretch factor, 3 for the price pane and 1 for the others by default: `set_pane_stretch_factor` sets it, `set_pane_height` turns a height in pixels into one, and `pane_height` reads the height drawn in the last frame. `set_preserve_empty_pane` keeps a pane after its last series or study leaves it. `PaneOptions`, set with `Chart::with_pane_options` or `pane_options_mut`, turns separator dragging off and sets the separator and hover colours. The demo's quick search adds a symbol in a new pane above the price pane. ([#827](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/827))
- `ChartConfig::price_axis` takes `PriceAxisOptions`, which draw a border between the plot and the price axis in the theme's border colour or a chosen one, a tick beside each label, and labels for the prices at the top and bottom of the pane in place of the ticks next to them, and choose whether labels the pane edges cut are hidden (the default) or shown clipped. `PriceScaleOptions::tick_marks` gives each scale its `PriceMarkGeneratorConfig`, so `target_density` sets the number of labels and grid lines; `PriceMap::marks` generates them. `Chart::price_axis_width` returns the width of the left or right price axis at the last frame. Migration: `render_price_labels` and `render_price_labels_at_pos` take the `PriceAxisOptions` after the colours; `ChartConfig` and `PriceScaleOptions` literals add the new fields or use `..Default::default()`. ([#833](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/833))
- `Chart::set_watermark` paints a host `Watermark` behind the series of the price pane, alongside the symbol watermark of `ChartConfig`: an optional `egui::Image` above any number of `WatermarkLine`s, each with its own text, size, `FontWeight` and colour, aligned together at any `Align2` with a padding from the pane's edges. The image fits the pane less the padding, keeping its aspect ratio, unless its `max_size` is smaller; its `tint` sets its opacity. Watermarks are kept in exported images. ([#831](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/831))
- `Chart::set_change_markers` marks, on a bar series, where a live update raised or lowered the close of a stored bar: a dot at the new close with a chevron above it for a rise or below it for a fall, in the `ChangeMarkers` up and down colours, for its `duration` (5 seconds by default). Updates from `update` and from a connected feed count; unchanged closes, new bars and history do not, and replacing the bars with `set_data` drops the marks. ([#831](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/831))
- `MarkerPos::{AtPriceTop, AtPriceBottom, AtPriceMiddle}` anchor a marker to `Marker::price` (set with `Marker::at_price`) just above, just below or across that price instead of to its bar, on the main and on added series. `Chart::set_marker_options` takes `MarkerOptions` per bar series: `auto_scale` (default true) widens the autoscaled range to the price-anchored markers in view, and `z_order` paints the markers with their series (`Normal`, the default), over every series and study (`AboveSeries`, where main series markers were painted before) or over the price lines and drawings too (`Top`). `Chart::markers` reads back the markers set on a series. Migration: `Marker` struct literals add `price: None`, and exhaustive `MarkerPos` matches handle the three new positions. ([#831](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/831))
- `Chart::add_price_line` adds one price line to a bar series and returns a `PriceLineId` handle; `update_price_line` moves or restyles that line in place, `remove_price_line` removes it and `price_line` reads it back. `set_price_lines` still replaces the whole list and retires the handles of the lines it replaces. The demo moves or removes a level line through its handle from the chart menu. It opens on a plain chart; Toggle API samples in its quick search adds the level line at the recent high, marks a fill and a stop at their prices, marks live changes of the close and signal bars, and shows a two-line watermark with an image. `PriceLine::axis_label_color`, `axis_label_text_color` and `title_color` colour the axis tag and the title independently of the line, on the main and the added series' axes. Migration: `PriceLine` struct literals add the three fields as `None` or use `..PriceLine::new(price, color)`. ([#831](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/831))
- Pointer events carry what a host legend, tooltip or context action shows. `ChartPointer` adds the rendered slot `index`, the UI-point `pos`, the `pane` counted from the top, the main series `bar` and `previous_close` at that slot, the bars of visible added series at its time, the visible study plots with the id of each study, the drawing, series or study line under the pointer as a `PointerTarget` in click priority, the held `modifiers` and the `PointerKind`. `ChartEvent::DoubleClick` follows the click that completes a primary double click. `CrosshairMoved` also reports a stationary cursor whose bar or modifiers change. egui reports pens as touch. The demo's data window reads the hovered values from the crosshair payload. Migration: `ChartPointer` is no longer `Copy` and is `#[non_exhaustive]`; clone it where it was copied and read its fields rather than building it. ([#829](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/829))
- The study catalogue adds eight oscillators under their conventional titles: `AcceleratorOscillator` ("Accelerator Oscillator", Bill Williams' AO minus its 5-bar average, columns green when rising and red when falling or unchanged), `AroonOscillator` (Aroon Up minus Aroon Down over 14 bars, -100 to 100 with a zero level), `AverageDailyRange` (the 14-bar average of high minus low), `BBTrend` (John Bollinger's comparison of 20 and 50-bar bands at 2 deviations, columns shaded by sign and direction), `ChaikinOscillator` (EMA 3 minus EMA 10 of the Accumulation/Distribution line, seeded with the first value), `BullBearPower` (high plus low minus twice the 13-bar EMA of the close, as Elder Ray seeds it), `AdvanceDeclineRatioBars` ("Advance/Decline Ratio (Bars)", up bars over down bars against their open in the last 9 bars, without a value when no bar closed down) and `UlcerIndex` (Peter Martin's root mean square drawdown from the 14-bar highest close). Their lengths are editable inputs, except the fixed 5, 34 and 5 of the Accelerator Oscillator; each skips bars with non-finite inputs and is checked against values computed independently from its published definition. ([#176](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/176))
- Built-in oscillators `PriceMomentumOscillator` (Swenlin's doubly smoothed one-bar rate of change with an EMA signal, 35/20/10), `PringsSpecialK` (Pring's twelve weighted rate-of-change averages with a twice-smoothed 100/100 signal), `RciRibbon` (short, middle and long rank correlation lines, 10/30/50, levels at +80, 0 and -80), `RelativeVolatilityIndex` (Dorsey's RVI of the 10-bar close deviation with 14-bar EMAs, levels 80/50/20 with a filled band), `SMIErgodicIndicator` and `SMIErgodicOscillator` (Blau's true strength ratio with its EMA signal, and their difference as a histogram, 20/5/5) and `WoodiesCCI` (turbo 6 and 14-bar CCI of the close, with columns coloured by the five previous values, levels at +100, 0 and -100). Each is in the factory catalogue under its conventional title and checked bar by bar against an independent calculation. ([#176](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/176))
- Six price studies join the catalogue. `WilliamsAlligator` draws Bill Williams' jaw, teeth and lips, smoothed averages of the median price over 13, 8 and 5 bars displaced 8, 5 and 3 bars ahead, with editable lengths and offsets. `ChandeKrollStop` draws Chande and Kroll's short and long stops (ATR length 10, coefficient 1, stop length 9). `VolatilityStop` draws Wilder's ATR stop and reverse (length 20, multiplier 2) as crosses coloured by trend. `Median` draws the 3-bar nearest-rank median of the bar midpoint, its EMA with a green or violet fill between them, and bands two 14-bar ATRs away. `ChopZone` colours a unit column per bar in nine bands by the angle of the 34-bar EMA of the close. `BollingerBars` redraws each bar at full width through `Indicator::candles`, with the wicks as blue blocks behind an up or down body. ([#176](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/176))
- `TimeWeightedAvgPrice` (Time Weighted Average Price) is the mean of a price source, ohlc4 by default, over the bars since the start of each anchor period, and `VwapAutoAnchored` (VWAP Auto Anchored) draws one volume weighted average price, of hlc3 by default, from an anchor bar to the latest bar: the first bar of the last session, week, month, quarter, year, decade or century, or the most recent bar with the highest high, lowest low or highest volume among the last `length` bars (20 by default). Its Auto anchor takes the period from the chart interval: the session below a day, the month at a day, the quarter up to 10 days, the year up to 60 days and the decade above. Both count periods in the instrument's exchange trading days as VWAP does, through `AnchorPeriod`. ([#176](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/176))
- `Volume24h` ("24-hour Volume") sums the volume of the bars opened in the last 24 hours on any intraday interval, giving the bar's own volume on daily bars and no value on longer ones. `RelativeVolumeAtTime` ("Relative Volume at Time") divides the volume of the current day, week or month so far (or of the bar alone in Regular mode) by its average at the same point of the previous 10 periods, with trading days from the instrument's time zone and session, as columns around a level at 1. `TradingSessions` ("Trading Sessions") shades the bars that open within the Tokyo, London and New York exchange hours, or three host-defined sessions in their own time zones, on intraday charts. `Seasonality` projects each month from the previous month's close and the average change of that calendar month in earlier years, as a filled box with the average, standard deviation and month count as hidden plots; the yearly heatmap table and the future part of the current month are left to the host. ([#176](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/176))
- `CandlestickPatterns` ("All Candlestick Patterns") marks 44 bullish, bearish and neutral candlestick patterns, from doji, hammer and engulfing to the star, three-method and tasuki-gap formations, each with its own toggle. A `PatternTrend` input requires the conventional downtrend or uptrend against SMA(50), or SMA(50) and SMA(200), or ignores trend, and a `PatternType` input limits the marks to bullish or bearish patterns. Bullish patterns are marked under the low, bearish over the high and neutral ones over the high, each labelled with the abbreviations of the patterns on the bar. ([#177](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/177))
- Four event studies join the catalogue. `WilliamsFractals` ("Williams Fractals", 2 periods) marks Bill Williams' fractals with up and down triangles, accepting up to four equal highs or lows just before the fractal bar. `PivotPointsHighLow` ("Pivot Points High Low", 10 bars on each side for highs and lows) marks swing highs and lows and labels each with its price through `plot_text`. `Gaps` ("Gaps") draws each gap of at least 30% of the 14-bar average range as a green or red zone that extends until price enters it, with inputs to shrink a partly filled gap, cap the number of gaps shown (15, at most 500) and limit their length (300 bars, off). `RsiDivergence` ("RSI Divergence Indicator", RSI 14, pivots 5 by 5, 5 to 60 bars apart) draws the RSI with its regular divergences, and optionally its hidden ones, as segments joining the two RSI pivots and "Bull" or "Bear" marks. ([#176](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/176))
- `Indicator::plot_text` labels a circle, cross or triangle plot at a source bar, drawing the text under an upward triangle and over the other shapes in the shape's colour. Configured studies forward it, so pattern, fractal and pivot studies can name what they mark. ([#176](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/176), [#177](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/177))
- `Chart::fit_content`, `reset_time_scale`, `scroll_to_real_time` and `scroll_to_position` fit every bar, restore the configured bar spacing and right offset, and scroll to the latest bar or to a right offset in bars, optionally animated over 400 ms to the latest bar and one second to a position. Scrolling to the latest bar does not enable tracking mode, and any other change of the view ends an animation. `visible_logical_range` and `set_visible_logical_range` read and set the fractional `model::LogicalRange`, including whitespace. `index_to_time`, `time_to_index`, `index_to_x` and `x_to_index` convert between UTC times, fractional logical indices and screen x, interpolating between bars and extrapolating into whitespace. `time_scale_size` returns the time axis size, and `drain_events` reports `ChartEvent::VisibleLogicalRangeChanged` and `ChartEvent::TimeScaleResized`. `TimeScaleOptions::right_bar_stays_on_scroll` now keeps the right offset when zooming with the wheel, a pinch or the keyboard, `uniform_distribution` shows time marks of one weight all or none, and `allow_bold_labels` turns off bold day, month and year labels. The new `tick_mark_max_character_length` spaces labels for longer custom formats, `minimum_height` sets a minimum time axis height, and `border_visible` with `border_color` draws a line along the top of the time axis. `TickMarkGenerator::with_uniform_distribution` exposes the same mark selection. Fitting the content, also through its shortcut, now leaves the configured right offset, in bars or pixels, free of bars, with every bar in view. Migration: `TimeScaleOptions` literals add `tick_mark_max_character_length`, `minimum_height`, `border_visible` and `border_color`, or use `..Default::default()`; `render_time_labels` takes `&TimeScaleOptions` in place of its `ticks_visible` flag. ([#828](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/828))

- `SymbolDetailsPanel` shows the chart symbol in bold with layout, edit and more actions, its name and exchange with a link to the page in `SymbolDetails::url`, the host-supplied regular-session quote large with its currency, change and last update time in the time zone given to `set_timezone`, the pre-market or post-market trade given to `set_extended_hours` with its change from the regular last price, the session and 30-day average volume given to `set_volume` above a bar split between the two, the key statistics and profile the feed answers through the new `Datafeed::symbol_details`, and the user's notes on the symbol. The layout menu shows or hides the volume, statistics, profile and notes, and the edit button moves to the notes. `SymbolDetails` carries statistics and profile as labelled values in display order; an empty section, or a feed keeping the `FeedError::NotSupported` default, shows nothing, and an answer for an earlier symbol is ignored. Notes are kept per symbol in a versioned `StoreKind::SymbolNotes` document: read before the first write, written as they are edited with one write per document at a time, and never written over after a failed read. The demo shows the details of the chart symbol below its watchlist, with synthetic statistics, volume and notes kept in its app storage, and reports SAMPLE trades outside its regular hours as pre-market or post-market.
- The `TimePriceOpportunity` study draws each session's TPO profile on the price pane of a time-based chart, starting at the session's first bar, as letters or blocks with the point of control, value area and initial balance, for the visible sessions or only the latest one. Its inputs set the period, ticks per row, value area percentage and extended-hours split. `Indicator::tpo` hands its `TPOConfig` to the chart, which builds the profiles through the TPO chart type's code and cache, so a session's profile equals the chart type's under the same settings; the study plots no values. The demo lists it with the other studies. ([#571](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/571))
- TPO profiles follow the chart's sessions, the instrument's exchange sessions or calendar days in its time zone, and calendar days in the chart's time zone without one, instead of the UTC date, and cover the whole sessions of the visible bars instead of the visible bars alone. `TPOConfig::split_sessions` gives separate pre-market, regular and post-market profiles, and `TPOConfig::composites` combines the sessions opening within a range into one profile: a range over adjacent sessions merges them, removing it splits them back, and a range over many sessions builds a composite. Periods are at least the bar interval, so hourly bars take the letters A, B and C instead of A, C and E. The value area shading spans the widest row instead of every period, the initial balance is drawn beside each profile, and a profile narrows its columns to fit within its session, switching to blocks when letters no longer fit. The Symbol tab edits the period, ticks per row, value area percentage and extended-hours split, which serialize with the chart type settings as `ChartTypeParams::{tpo_period_minutes, tpo_row_ticks, tpo_value_area, tpo_split_sessions, tpo_composites}`. Migration: `to_tpo_profiles` takes the bars' `BarClock`, such as `Chart::bar_clock`; pass `&Timeframe::Min1.into()` for the previous UTC days. `render_live_tpo` takes the full bars, the visible range and the clock. `TPOConfig` and `ChartTypeParams` literals add the new fields or use `..Default::default()`, and `CandleDataContext` literals add `clock` and `tick_size`. ([#106](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/106))
- `Trading::show` draws broker orders, brackets and positions with price tags, P&L, cancel, close and reverse actions. Accounts with `Capabilities::modify` can drag lines to tick prices through `Broker::modify`; its default reports unsupported. Changes require confirmation, which is discarded when the order or position changes. `chart::render_trading_lines` exposes the renderer without the `ui` feature. ([#217](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/217))

- `ui::trading::Trading` connects Buy/Sell buttons to a broker-backed ticket with market, limit, stop and stop-limit orders, unit/value/risk sizing, price/tick/percent brackets, time in force and saved presets. Ticket and one-click quantities follow `Broker::rules`; unsupported rules default to whole units and day orders. Prices snap to instrument ticks. The host persists presets and owns the connection; refusals and fills appear as notifications. The demo supplies a session-only paper account whose bracket exits reduce the remaining position. ([#219](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/219))
- `WatchlistPanel` shows named watchlists fed by host quotes. `WatchlistQuote` supplies the last price and the previous close, from which the panel derives the change and the change percent over the absolute previous close. Lists hold symbols and sections, sort by any column inside each section with missing values last, reorder by dragging while unsorted, take colour flags from the row menu, collapse and expand under their section titles, and import and export comma-separated text with `###` section titles through `Watchlist::from_text` and `to_text`. The header shows the list name in bold as the list menu, then add symbol, advanced view and more buttons: add symbol returns `WatchlistAction::AddSymbol` for the host to pick a symbol and pass it to `WatchlistPanel::add_symbol`, advanced view returns `WatchlistAction::AdvancedView`, and the more menu adds sections and imports or exports the list. Clicking a symbol, or the up and down arrow keys after a click, returns `WatchlistAction::SelectSymbol`; file dialogs and persistence of `lists` stay with the host. The demo feeds synthetic quotes, keeps its lists in application storage, switches the chart symbol from the panel and adds the symbols picked in its symbol search. ([#223](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/223))
- `TimeAndSalesPanel` lists the latest 10,000 trades the host pushes from its stream, newest first, with buys and sells in the rising and falling colours and trades of unknown side neutral. Size filters edited in the panel restrict the list to trades reaching one of them, and `push` returns the matching filter with sound that has the largest threshold, so the host can play its sound. `TimeAndSalesConfig` sets the price and size decimals and the time zone. The demo feeds the executions of its synthetic stream and shows a notification in place of a sound. ([#237](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/237))
- `HigherIntervalCandles` overlays higher-period candle bodies and wicks across their source bars, coloured by direction, with editable interval and extended-hours inputs. It uses the shared session aggregation, includes the forming period and rebuilds on live corrections. `Indicator::set_bar_clock` shares the source calendar and `Indicator::candles` exposes period OHLCV and source ranges; `ConfiguredStudy` retains the geometry through styling and cloning. The demo's Indicators picker includes the study with daily defaults. Hosts supply the chart bars; another interval from a feed remains host-owned. ([#546](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/546))

- `data::resample_with_session` derives intervals from host-owned base bars using the instrument's exchange hours, holidays and overnight trading dates. Intraday buckets start at the first selected session window, and the short last bar stops at its session close. Calendar labels use UTC by default; pass source and target `BarClock` values for another selected calendar. Inline parsed arguments name `.parse::<Timeframe>()`. Gaps remain empty and the forming period includes every available input. `Instrument::native_timeframes` reports the intervals a source supplies without aggregation; unknown metadata stays `None`. `BarClock::with_session` and the chart feed preserve those intraday opens through historical and live merging. The demo declares its native minute history. Hosts own fetching and retaining their base data. ([#97](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/97))
- `Chart::export_csv` exports loaded source bars with freshly calculated visible study plots, plot displacement, gaps, chart time zone offsets and instrument/study precision. `Chart::export_image` renders an isolated egui frame with chart title, export timestamp, pixel dimensions and transparent background options, retaining drawings, their interval visibility, comparisons, study panes, loaded source marks and typed events with their visibility settings without changing the live chart or polling feeds. Late image results do not change a reopened export dialog. Hosts render the returned meshes/textures and own encoding and delivery; the demo wires offscreen GPU rendering, PNG/clipboard delivery and CSV saving. The export dialog offers these implemented image options; SVG remains absent. Migration: `ExportConfig` literals add `image: Default::default()` or `..Default::default()`. ([#200](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/200))
- `Chart::set_mark_source` requests host bar annotations, time-axis badges and typed events through `services::Marks` for the visible UTC range, including whitespace. Stale replies are ignored; empty answers clear source marks while manual markers remain independent. Hosts refresh with `reload_marks` and read failures with `take_mark_error`. Bar marker hover text is displayed, and colliding time badges stack with independent hover and click targets. The demo supplies synthetic marks from its own bars and retains event visibility and sources in copied layouts. ([#76](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/76))
- `model::{TimeScaleEvent, EventKind, EventVisibility}` describes host earnings, dividends, splits, economic releases and news with UTC timestamps, headlines, details and stable click IDs. `Chart::set_time_scale_events` accepts events directly; `event_visibility` and the settings dialog's Events tab filter every category without discarding data. Accounts, news and economic data services remain host-owned. Migration: exhaustive `SettingsTab` matches need `Events`; `EventsSettings` is now an alias of `EventVisibility`, and struct literals add `show_news` or use `..Default::default()`. Saved settings default missing event fields. ([#242](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/242))

- `Chart::set_replay_time` retains host history behind an exclusive completion cutoff, hides future bars and live quotes, and restores retained updates and the previous feed pause state on exit. Studies and price-based elements use the same window; rewinding clears future study results. Tick-derived overlays stay hidden during bar replay. `set_replay_source` builds partial chart candles from completed finer bars using the source calendar. `ChartLayout::set_replay_time` drives one cursor across a synchronization group, including hidden slots. `ReplayStartPicker` emits chart-click, UTC-date, random, first-bar and resume intents. `ReplayController::{cutoff, seek_cutoff, session, restore_session}` connects source playback and serializable, symbol-checked checkpoints; restoration pauses and requires the saved timestamp. The demo wires these choices, offers minute updates on coarser charts and saves checkpoints through application storage. Hosts own historical/finer data, randomness and durable storage. Bar timestamps are authoritative in `ReplayController`; its separate timestamp vector remains accepted for compatibility. Migration: exhaustive `ReplayControlAction` matches need `SetUpdateInterval(Timeframe)`; supply available source intervals through `FloatingReplayControl::set_update_intervals`. ([#213](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/213))

- `WidgetBar::register_panel` accepts host widget metadata and a stateful content callback. `show_panel` renders the active registered panel with the shared header, close button and a resizable width retained by egui. Host and built-in selections remain exclusive; replacing a registration preserves its strip position. Existing id-only widgets remain available. The demo registers session notes alongside the data window and built-in panels. Hosts own panel data and persistence. ([#206](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/206))

- `ChartMenus::host_entries` appends conditional `HostMenuEntry` rows to the chart, series and drawing menus. `ChartContextAction::Host` returns the registered id and original target, UTC bar time and clicked value; time is absent outside loaded data. `TopToolbar::host_entries` renders themed buttons and dropdowns in registration order and returns `TopToolbarAction::Host`. The demo lists its research and symbol-copy actions in its main menu; the research action reports the last chart click time and price. The strip scrolls when controls cannot fit. Dropdown choices are one level deep. Hosts supply the services behind these actions. Migration: exhaustive action matches handle the two new `Host` variants. ([#566](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/566))

- `Control` now names every top-toolbar button and the Object Tree and Help rail buttons. `set_control_visible` removes controls from the strip and overflow without leaving separators; hiding an active rail entry clears it.
