Chart and series menu rows share the Settings, Indicators and ObjectTree controls and add ResetView, CopyPrice and ExportData switches; axis menus honor Settings, TimeZone and Session. Empty menus close, and ContextMenus disables all turnkey menus. `ChartOptions` continues to govern interaction behavior. `ChartBuilder::with_theme` now retains the theme for local rendering, so text sizes and overlay palettes no longer come from another chart's context theme. `Chart::set_theme_override` replaces or clears this local style; `set_theme` still themes egui and clears the local override. Component configs retain their public dimension overrides. ([#202](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/202))

- `Chart::drain_events` reports UTC visible-range changes, crosshair moves and exits, plot clicks with time and pane values, successful history loads, and symbol and interval changes. Setter notifications retain occurrence order; unchanged views and stationary cursors emit nothing. Existing polling APIs remain independent. `visible_time_range` and `apply_synced_time_range` already read and set UTC plot edges, including whitespace. The basic example records host-owned click state. Hosts supply history and streaming data. ([#201](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/201))

- `Sector` collects its center, radius endpoint and end ray, sweeping clockwise on screen with selectable borders and optional fill. `ArrowMarkLeft` and `ArrowMarkRight` place directional marks in one click. `Sticker` places bundled vector illustrations through the glyph picker. Path drawings end in an arrowhead by default. Arrow Marker already uses two points, and the presentation cursor already highlights pointer movement. Migration: exhaustive `DrawingToolType` matches need the four new variants. Their toolbar artwork is exported from `icons::icons` alongside the existing drawing icons. ([#147](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/147))
- The Icons/Emojis flyout groups bundled vector icons, Unicode emoji and stickers in a searchable grid, with session recents first and an independent size control. Bundled icons and stickers render without host fonts or image loaders; Unicode emoji retain the host font fallback. The existing `Drawing::font_size` saves the size, and keep-drawing mode retains the picked glyph and size. Migration: handle `DrawingToolbarAction::InsertGlyph { icon_name, size }` by calling `DrawingManager::start_icon_insertion` and setting the prepared drawing's `font_size`; `InsertEmoji` remains available for existing callers. `ToolbarState` literals add `glyph_picker`, or use `..Default::default()`. Hosts continue to own durable drawing storage. ([#742](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/742))
- `Chart::add_series` attaches host-fed close-price lines with stable ids, independent history and streaming updates, markers and price lines. `SeriesScale` assigns bar series to shared right or left axes, hidden overlays or additional labelled scales; `set_series_scale` and `series_price_scale_mut` also accept the main series. `add_comparison` defaults to a shared percent range with a separate first-visible-close reference for each member. `set_series_visible` and `remove_series` cover every bar series, including the main one; hidden members do not stretch auto-scale ranges. Removing the main series pauses its feed and retains its time axis for the remaining series and drawings. Secondary times project onto that axis, including whitespace and price-based elements. Hosts own data feeds. The demo uses native comparison series and retains them across layout copies. ([#87](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/87))
- Added bar series take every time-based chart type of the main series through `SeriesOptions::chart_type`, with `up_color` and `down_color` for rising and falling bars and the baseline sides, and `columns_base` for Columns that rise and fall from a price, which their scale's fit includes, as `ChartTypeParams::columns_base` does for the main series; their scales fit the lows and highs of the OHLC types. In a pane other than the price pane a series keeps its chart type and draws the crosshair above its bars, its legend row hides or removes it or hands its id to `take_indicator_settings_request`, and `series_price_scale` returns the pane's scale. `set_series_options` changes the options of an added series at runtime, and `series_order` and `set_series_order` set the draw order that `series` follows. `pop_bars` removes the last bars of any bar series, `bar_index_at` finds the bar at a time or its nearest neighbour on either side, and `bars_in_logical_range` returns the bars inside a range of logical indices with the bars before and after it. `ChartEvent::DataUpdated` reports live bars from `update` or the chart's feed and removals, once per series in a row. `series_price_map` converts between the prices of any bar series and pixels in whichever pane draws it, and `format_series_price` formats them as its scale does. The symbol the demo's quick search adds in a new pane is drawn as candles, on the left scale once moved to the price pane, and keeps its pane across layout copies. Migration: struct literals of `SeriesOptions` set `chart_type`, `up_color`, `down_color` and `columns_base` or use `..Default::default()`. ([#825](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/825))

- Renko, Kagi, Line Break, Point and Figure and range charts calculate a separate set of study results from element OHLC, retaining the source-bar results for hosts and time-based views. Element outputs share the existing refresh budget, update from the first changed element and drive overlays, pane scales, selection, magnets, legends and readouts. Markers and retained drawing times use element completion intervals, with quiet trailing history on the last element. Point and Figure uses column start intervals so quiet bars stay in the current column. Repeated completions choose the first slot unless a drawing already retains another matching slot. Newly placed drawing anchors retain element times across chart-type switches. `Chart::study_values_at` returns displayed element outputs; `indicators` continues to expose source-bar results. The hidden-annotations notice is removed. ([#712](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/712))

- Drawings can be created, selected, dragged, erased and edited in study panes using each pane's own scale, including manual, inverted and logarithmic ranges. Pane ownership survives drawing storage, undo, pane resizing, maximisation and study merging; hidden or collapsed panes leave no drawing hit targets. Magnet targets use the studies in that pane. `Drawing::pane` identifies the owning study, with `None` retaining the main price pane; hosts restoring studies retain or remap those ids. Migration: add `pane: None` to `Drawing` literals, or use `Drawing::new`. Older saved files default to the main pane. ([#151](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/151))
- Drawing properties expose Inputs for Fibonacci levels, pitchfork levels, Gann divisions, position sizing, volume profile rows and value area, regression bands and channel midlines. Coordinates open with every anchor and accept finite prices and UTC times, retaining fractional and repeated-time anchors. Invalid drafts stay open without changing the chart. `DrawingPropertiesDialog::open_for_chart` and `show_and_apply` read and apply edits in the library with undo, lock checks and persistence notifications; Apply is then a notification the host must not apply again. Common style edits still reach the unlocked selection, tool inputs reach matching tools, and coordinate edits affect only the opened drawing. Save as default retains per-tool properties in `DrawingManager::tool_defaults`; the demo persists these through application storage. New drawings stay visible when the saved default came from a hidden drawing. `DrawingTemplate` is also available under `drawings`, with its existing UI import retained. Hosts own durable storage. Migration: remove the unused `DrawingProps::show_price` field; Fibonacci price labels are edited per level. `DrawingProps` literals add `symbol`, `tool_settings`, `coordinates` and `save_as_default`, or use `..Default::default()`; construct `CoordinatePoint` through `Default`; dialog literals add `error`, and exhaustive `PropertiesTab` matches handle `Inputs`. ([#142](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/142))

- `Indicator::value_format` declares price, abbreviated signed volume, percentage or fixed-decimal units; `scale` chooses the main scale, an independent scale or an independent fit without labels. `CustomIndicator` sets both through builders. Pane axes, legends, cursor tags, tooltips and data-window rows share the format; settings precision overrides retain the units, and only price values inherit instrument precision. Built-in volume totals and percentage studies declare their units without changing placement. Independent overlays reserve separate left-axis gutters that shrink in narrow windows, share their mapping with selection and leave the main price range and magnet unchanged. Study units stay independent of instrument precision in overlay legends. Migration: add `format` to `StudyValue` literals. ([#570](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/570))

- Studies declare step lines, pitch-sized columns, crosses, areas, triangles, per-bar colours, bar/background colouring and bands between plots or levels. Both pane and overlay renderers share the geometry, preserve gaps and apply per-output offsets, including scaling and selection in visible future whitespace. Crossing fills split at their intersection without seams between bars; bar colours preserve candle bodies on inverted scales. Displaced values also drive last-value tags, alerts and drawing magnets; pane markers use the displayed range. MACD uses columns, Awesome Oscillator colours columns by momentum and Parabolic SAR uses dots; Bollinger and Keltner bands have a 20% fill. Existing Supertrend direction outputs and RSI level shading are retained. Migration: handle the new `PlotType` variants in exhaustive matches. Ichimoku `values()` now retains unshifted source calculations, including the latest leading spans; use `plot_value` for displayed values or `plot_offset` when drawing. Displacement counts the current bar, so 26 periods shifts 25 bars. ([#165](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/165))
- Placing or double-clicking a text annotation, note or callout opens an inline multiline editor. Ctrl/Cmd+Enter or a click outside commits one undoable edit; Escape discards the draft, and locked annotations cannot be edited. Long drafts scroll within narrow panes, and clicks inside the editor keep it open. Anchored text and notes retain a pane-relative position through pan, zoom, resize, dragging and persistence; older drawings adopt their first displayed position. `Drawing::update_pane_coords` and `DrawingManager::update_pane_anchors` support standalone rendering. Migration: `Drawing` struct literals must set `screen_anchor: None`, or use `Drawing::new`; saved files default the new field. Clones of anchored annotations move right by 2% of pane width. ([#150](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/150))
- Shift-click toggles drawings in a group selection. Dragging a selected body moves the unlocked group without panning or snapping to its own anchors; deletion, cloning, and demo property edits apply to the group with one undo step. `DrawingManager::{selected_ids, is_selected, toggle_selection, edit_selected, paste_drawings}` exposes the same operations to hosts. `Chart::copy_selected_drawings` returns versioned clipboard text with timestamp anchors, and `paste_drawings` validates it before adding fresh copies three bars to the right. The demo forwards native copy/paste events; clipboard text survives a lost session cache and maps to the current interval of the same symbol. ([#148](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/148))
- Study panes have draggable separators, maximise/restore controls and menus for ordering, collapsing, removal, merging and moving studies between panes or the main chart. Initial and resized axis sizing retain the configured horizontal zoom policy. Heights survive collapse and maximisation; resizing keeps adjacent panes above their minimum size. Each pane has its own draggable price axis, double-click auto-scale reset, scale modes and inversion. `Chart::move_indicator_to_pane` exposes study placement to hosts. `Indicator::pane` supplies `StudyPane` defaults and reference levels without matching names; custom presentation wrappers forward it and implement `set_overlay` to accept placement changes. `ConfiguredStudy` already does both. Migration: `RenderedIndicatorPane` literals add `mapping`; use that complete mapping for vertical coordinates in manual, logarithmic, percentage and indexed scales. `hit_test_pane_indicator` takes its range from that mapping; remove its two range arguments. Pane menu edits and study placement participate in chart undo and redo. ([#89](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/89))

- `Chart::save_drawings` exports the current symbol to `DrawingStorage`; `load_drawings` restores a JSON file with the chart's time mapping and returns its skipped records. Restoring can run before history arrives and rejects a different symbol without changing drawings. Loading preserves drawing controls, and saving rejects timestamps that loading cannot retain. The demo saves and restores per-symbol drawings through application storage. Filesystem, accounts and cloud storage remain host-owned. ([#373](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/373))
- Chart settings Save as prompts for a name. `SettingsDialog::templates` exposes its serializable settings catalog, and `ui::dialogs::templates::{TemplateStore, TemplateDialog, StudyTemplate, DrawingTemplate}` supplies named save, list, load and delete for host chart, study and drawing templates through `Storage`. Documents are versioned, writes wait for reads and outstanding requests, failed saves retain edits, failed deletes retain rows, and unreadable or unknown-version catalogs cannot be overwritten. The demo connects its settings dialog and study and drawing toolbar intents to application storage; drawing templates edit selected drawings without changing anchors or identity, and study templates retain typed inputs and presentation. Long names and catalogs fit short and narrow windows. Accounts and remote storage remain host-owned. Migration: replace `ui::model::{TemplateManager, SettingsTemplate}` with `TemplateStore<ChartSettingsState>`; handle the new `StoreKind::{ChartTemplates, StudyTemplates, DrawingTemplates}` namespaces in host storage and `DrawingToolbarAction::ManageTemplates` to open a template catalog. ([#199](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/199))
- `studies::Volume`, registered as "Volume", draws up/down columns with an optional SMA or EMA, editable length, open or previous-close colouring and price-overlay or separate-pane placement. An unchanged close counts as up; the first previous-close comparison uses the bar's open. Volume uses its own zero-based scale, 80% pitch columns and a one-pixel moving average with a magenta value tag. The demo uses a 12% overlay band. ([#572](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/572))
- With the `studies` feature, `ChartConfig::show_volume` adds a default Volume study on the next frame; disabling it removes that study. User actions restore its settings and pane placement through undo and redo. Its stable id is available through `Chart::indicators` for editing or removal; closing it clears the volume switch. Hosts supplying their own Volume study can turn off the default switch before adding it. Widget-only builds retain the volume band. `Indicator::is_volume` identifies the three volume outputs for independent scaling and interaction; custom study wrappers forward it.

- `Chart::undo`, `redo`, `can_undo` and `can_redo` share one ordered history for user drawing edits, study additions/removals/settings, chart type, interval and chart settings. Shortcuts, chart navigation buttons and the demo toolbar use this history; navigation undo restores the prior view and scale. Host UI/intent handlers wrap edits in `Chart::user_action` (or paired `begin_user_action`/`end_user_action`); direct programmatic API calls and feed/data updates are not recorded. Unchanged actions keep redo; new user edits clear it. Undo restores the affected study or settings snapshot, preserves study ids and unrelated studies/drawings, and keeps drawing persistence notifications. Hosts use `take_history_changed` to synchronize controls and reload their own interval data after undo/redo. Migration: use the chart history API for chart Undo/Redo; `DrawingManager` history remains available for standalone drawing managers. ([#108](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/108))

- `Chart::shortcuts` and `shortcuts_mut` expose an editable `ShortcutMap`, seeded from `config::KeyboardAction::shortcuts` (`egui::KeyboardShortcut` values). The dispatcher consumes exact modifier combinations and shares bindings with menu hints and shortcut help. Earlier bindings take precedence in input and hints; shadowed bindings are omitted. `take_shortcut_requests` returns host actions, typed symbol and interval entry, and clipboard text; hosts supply their own dialogs, layouts and clipboard formats. `dispatch_shortcuts` lets the active chart run before toolbars, and `suppress_shortcuts` reserves a pass for host dialogs. The demo cycles keyboard focus through its visible chart slots, wires these intents and keeps drawing copies for the current symbol and interval within its session. ([#113](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/113))
- Alerts retain a name, host watchlist, typed price, study, drawing or volume source and additional conditions required on the same bar. Conditions include channels, signed value and percentage moves and output comparisons; frequencies include once per bar and once per bar close. The shared dialog creates and edits `model::Alert`, accepts host catalogs and persists named presets through `Storage`. Its form fields fill the available control column, its default body height fits the editor, and smaller windows scroll before the shared Save/Cancel footer with validation feedback, in both themes. ([#293](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/293)) Old single-symbol alerts keep loading. Migration: `Alert` literals add `name`, `source`, `watchlist` and `additional_conditions`, or use `Alert::new`; `AlertCondition` is no longer `Copy`. Dialog `Create` carries `Box<Alert>`; replace the old string form structs and tab-specific data with `AlertDialog::alert`, `sources` and `watchlists`; `AlertDialog::open(symbol, price)` now requires the last or hovered price; `AlertTab` and the obsolete `AlertData` alias are removed; use `model::Alert`. `AlertDialogConfig` retains only `width` and the maximum scrolling-body `height`. Handle `AlertPanelAction::EditAlert` and the new condition, frequency and `StoreKind::AlertPresets` variants. ([#207](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/207), [#209](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/209))
- `Chart::study_values_at` returns each visible plot's title, value, precision and colour, retaining gaps as missing values. `DataWindowInfo::from_chart` populates OHLCV, previous-close change, chart-formatted time and visible study rows from the hovered rendered bar, with latest-bar fallback. The demo uses this library population, and floating and tracking tooltips include the same study rows within their plot bounds. Price-based charts omit source-indexed studies. Migration: `DataWindowInfo` literals include `studies` or use `Default`. ([#119](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/119))
- `TimeframeSelector::set_supported_timeframes` restricts presets and custom choices to the connected source's intervals, retaining unavailable saved preferences. Hosts update it when their source or symbol changes; `None` permits all intervals. `TopToolbarAction::ChartStyleSelected` now carries `model::ChartType` directly. Migration: consume that typed value instead of parsing a display string. ([#103](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/103))

- Interval-menu stars pin and unpin presets and custom intervals without selecting them. Add retains custom intervals without duplicate rows. `TimeframeSelector::preferences` and `set_preferences` exchange serializable `TimeframePreferences` for host user settings; unavailable favourites are retained. The demo saves and restores these lists through application storage. ([#104](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/104))

- `widget::ChartLayout` renders one to sixteen host-owned charts in grid presets or tabs, with active-chart selection, a two-point inside border, two-point row gaps and edge-to-edge columns. Each chart retains its feed, studies, drawings and scales. Its corner menu assigns named synchronization groups and detaches charts into immediate viewports, embedded when the backend cannot create native windows. `ui::multi_chart` re-exports the working container and its types. ([#115](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/115))
- Layout groups synchronize symbol and interval changes, crosshair timestamps, scrolling and zooming, explicit date ranges and drawing edits. Drawing anchors map by time between intervals; synchronization is limited to matching symbols and groups, preserves unrelated drawings and leaves host persistence queues intact. Hosts supplying bars directly replace them after synchronized symbol or interval changes.
- The top toolbar shows the grid selector beside indicator templates and a named-layout menu at the right. `LayoutAction::SelectGrid` selects presets; save, save-as, rename, open and delete actions are host intents. The demo owns session layouts in a tab strip, routes its toolbars and range bar to the active chart and gives new charts independent synthetic subscriptions without regenerating their common history. Persistent and cloud storage remain host-owned.
- Migration: exhaustive `ChartLayoutMode` matches must cover the new five and seven through sixteen chart presets and `Tabs`; exhaustive `LayoutAction` matches must handle `SelectGrid`. `ChartSyncOptions` literals must add `sync_date_range` or `..Default::default()`. `LayoutMenu` now emits save and catalog intents without mutating host-owned names or lists; update `curr_layout` and `layouts` after handling an action. Its initial name is `Unnamed` and its initial catalog is empty.
- Price-based chart settings offer `BoxSizeMethod::Fixed`, Wilder `Atr { period, multiplier }` and `Percentage` for Renko, Kagi, Point and Figure and Range. `Chart::set_chart_type_params` resolves the selected methods from loaded history, waiting for the ATR seed when needed, then freezes the sizes through streaming updates, corrections and feed prepends. Reapplying parameters or replacing the dataset with `set_data` recalculates them; changing the symbol or interval reloads history and recalculates too. Percentage uses the absolute latest close, with 1 meaning 1%. Renko, Kagi and Line Break accept `PriceBasedSource::Close` or `HighLow`; without intrabar ordering, a continuation takes priority when both thresholds are crossed. `renko_wicks` paints the brick's actual high and low; close input tracks close extremes and high/low input tracks bar extremes. The Symbol tab edits these parameters, which serialize with chart type settings. Migration: chart parameter literals add the method, source and wick fields or use defaults; `RenkoConfig`, `KagiConfig` and `LineBreakConfig` literals add `source` or use their constructors; `RenkoBrick` literals add `high` and `low`. `SeriesSettingsAction::Apply` carries its settings in a `Box`; callers constructing that action wrap settings with `Box::new`, and readers can borrow or dereference them. `RenkoConfig::from_atr` and `KagiConfig::from_atr` now use Wilder smoothing and require a complete seed instead of averaging the latest ranges. ([#633](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/633))

- `ChartTypeParams::styles` retains independent line, step line, marker, area and baseline colours, widths and line styles; Area has separate top and bottom fill stops, Baseline has separate line and fill colours on each side, and markers have a configurable radius. The series settings Symbol tab edits the current type. `Chart::series_settings` reads the current values for opening the dialog, and applying them leaves candle colours independent. Chart types, parameters and styles support serde for host-owned saved settings. Migration: `ChartTypeParams` literals add `styles` or use `..Default::default()`; `SeriesSettings` literals add `chart_type` and `chart_type_params`, or use the defaults. ([#562](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/562))

- Visible study outputs have last-value axis tags in their line colours, including separate panes and left axes; hiding both axes hides their tags. Tags are 15 px high at the default axis font size. Colliding tags move to the nearest free row. High/low and host bid/ask labels and lines have independent switches, and volume uses a magenta axis tag. Countdown labels advance with the feed's server clock, respect session closes and calendar months, and accept `Chart::set_server_time` and `set_bar_close_time` for direct-data and irregular-session hosts. `set_right_margin` and the settings dialog apply scale margins; the quick ratio-lock checkbox reaches the scale engine. Migration: `ChartConfig` literals need `show_bid_ask`, `show_high_low` and `lock_price_to_bar_ratio`, or `..Default::default()`. Study ticks, legends and axis tags share `StudySettings::precision`; bounded RSI and Stochastic presets retain two decimals by default. ([#112](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/112))

- Right-clicking either price axis or the time axis opens scale and session controls through `ChartMenus`. Price menus apply auto/manual scale, regular, percent, indexed and logarithmic modes, inversion, placement, label visibility and a price-to-bar ratio lock; time menus apply UTC, local or exchange time and session choices. `PriceScale` exposes the ratio lock for hosts composing their own controls. Long axis menus scroll inside short windows. ([#110](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/110))

- Price axes show the instrument currency in a tick-sized button above their labels. The menu checks the current currency and emits host conversion choices through `Chart::set_currency_choices` and `take_currency_choice`; hosts supply converted bars and metadata together. `chart::render_axis_currency` supports composed axes. ([#734](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/734))
- `ObjectTreePanel::apply_to_chart` applies drawing and study visibility, locking, duplication, removal, selection operations, one-step layer moves and drawing focus. Bind series rows with `SourceItem::with_series_id`, using `SeriesId::MAIN` for the data source. Drawing names live in `Drawing::label`, survive storage and participate in undo; the shared prompt renames drawings. Layer moves are one undo step, including tied layers, and drawing copies have their own undo and save entry through the shared manager creation path. Selected drawings show handles together. Property dialogs and colour gestures remain host intents. Migration: `SourceItem` literals set `series_id`, `Drawing` literals set `label`, and exhaustive matches on `DrawingCommand` handle `Reorder`. Constructors use the defaults. ([#149](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/149))
- `Chart::set_main_series_visible` hides the price series, volume, readout and hit targets while retaining its bars, studies and drawings; `is_main_series_visible` reads it. The object tree's main-series eye uses this operation. Main-series removal and duplication are not offered.
- Duplicating a study from the object tree retains its input caption. Locked drawings can be hidden or renamed while their geometry and style remain protected.

- `AlertsPanel` connects to a host `services::Alerts` with `set_service`, lists host-reported states and retains the latest 10,000 triggers with CSV export. Call `poll(ctx, &mut toasts)` each frame, including while hidden, and render that shared notification list once. Stream recovery preserves pending save and removal replies. Creation returns `AlertPanelAction::CreateAlert` for the shared dialog; `save` submits the completed `model::Alert`, and `ExportLog` carries CSV for host delivery. The demo supplies session-only storage. Migration: replace `show(ui, &AlertManager)` with `show(ui)`, attach a service and poll each frame. The obsolete alert types and `AlertManager` in `ui::model`, inline forms and getters, `AlertType`, `AlertStatusWidget` and `AlertNotification` are removed; use `model::Alert`, the shared dialog and notification components. `AlertsPanelConfig` now contains only `max_height`. ([#208](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/208))

- `Chart::bar_clock` shares the resolved chart, exchange or system-local calendar with host rebuilds. `BarClock::with_timezone` and `with_local_timezone` align intraday and calendar windows, including repeated hours and short or long days. `HistoryRequest::clock` and `Subscription::clock` give hosts the same boundaries as the chart; changing the chart timezone reloads the series. `resample` and `aggregate_trades` accept either a timeframe for UTC or a supplied clock. Migration: inline parsed arguments to those functions specify `.parse::<Timeframe>()`; subscription destructuring includes `clock` or `..`.

- `Sink::advance_time` opens an empty current time bar on a trusted feed heartbeat, carrying the last close with zero volume until the first execution sets its OHLC. Count intervals wait for executions. `data::aggregate_trades` rebuilds chronological trade history with the same aggregation, validation and duplicate-id rules as the live sink.
- The native and web demos backfill to the current clock and stream deterministic synthetic trades and best bid/ask quotes through the public feed API. The default one-minute candle grows on every execution; the interval menu rebuilds second and count bars from a bounded trade buffer, with seven days of minute history built from the same executions for coarse views. Session filtering uses the instrument windows and the chart's extended-hours choice. A small pause/resume control freezes the feed. The footprint sample pauses streaming while it is inspected. Timed repaints follow executions and countdown seconds, with no worker thread.
- `Chart::set_feed_paused` suspends requests and unsubscribes while a host supplies replay bars. Resuming reloads the selected symbol and interval from the host feed. The demo retains replay when switching intervals and restores the preceding pause state when leaving replay.

- `Timeframe::Ticks(NonZeroU64)` and the interval menu offer 1, 10, 100 and 1000 execution bars. `Sink::push_tick` counts accepted trades across frames, retains exact OHLCV and keeps replayed ids from consuming a count. Replaying retained executions rebuilds the partial final bar when changing intervals. Count bar timestamps keep nanosecond precision, advancing by one nanosecond when opening times would collide. Migration: exhaustive `Timeframe` and `TimeframeUnit` matches need a tick arm. `duration_ms()` and `duration()` return zero for count intervals, which have no time duration; check the variant before stepping time. `Timeframe::all()` continues to list time intervals only.

- Applied studies expose typed `StudyInput` schemas and validated setters, including independent average-combination lengths, formulas and sources. `IndicatorSettingsDialog` stages Inputs, per-plot Style and interval Visibility edits; Apply targets the original study id and Cancel discards them. `ConfiguredStudy` applies complete edits atomically and retains serializable `StudySettings`, per-plot colour, width, stroke pattern, line/histogram/circle geometry, visibility and precision. Charts wrap studies once, report legend and pane settings intents through `take_indicator_settings_request`, and exclude hidden plots from scaling, snapping and selection. Hosts supply schemas for their own studies and persist settings themselves. The demo's existing settings action edits the selected study. ([#169](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/169))

- `IndicatorDialog::preferences` and `set_preferences` export and restore serializable `IndicatorPreferences`, including favourites for temporarily unavailable host studies. Hosts save this value in their user settings when `ToggleFavorite` fires; the library owns no storage. Study defaults, editable Keltner and Stochastic parameters and tab filtering were already corrected in #692. ([#170](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/170))

- `MACombination` combines two to fourteen `AverageLine`s with independent simple, exponential, weighted, Wilder, volume-weighted or Hull formulas, lengths, sources and colours. `MACross`, `EMACross` and `MultipleMA` share its calculations with their existing defaults. The factory adds `SMA/EMA Cross`, `Triple MA` and `MA Ribbon`; the ribbon has twelve evenly spaced lengths, individual direction colours, a thicker slowest line and a fading purple bottom fill, with a muted variant available directly. Crossing markers identify changes of side, ignore touches and disappear when a live correction cancels the cross. ([#549](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/549))

- `LinearRegressionChannel` fits the trailing window with upper, middle and lower lines, residual deviation bands with an `n - 1` denominator, selectable price source, Pearson correlation label and optional right extension. Updates clear expired bars and refit the trailing window on every live change, independently of history length. Right extensions project prices before mapping to the axis, including logarithmic and inverted scales. Drawings share the same headless least-squares maths. ([#548](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/548))

- Charts pass instrument time zones and sessions to studies when added and when metadata arrives or changes, so a dialog-created VWAP follows the exchange session. Clearing metadata restores the study's configured time zone and session. VWAP supports quarter and year anchors, percentage bands, and `with_event_anchors` for earnings, dividend or split timestamps supplied by the host. Event anchors reset on the first bar at or after the timestamp, including after a market gap. ([#163](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/163))
- `data::Footprint`, `FootprintBar` and `FootprintLevel` aggregate chronological executions per UTC bar and integer price row, with separate bid, ask and unknown volume, intrabar delta extremes, POC, contiguous value area, horizontal and diagonal imbalances and inclusive trade-size filtering. Invalid, out-of-order or overflowing executions return an error without changing the aggregation; executions with unknown aggressor never contribute to delta.
- `studies::trade_delta` computes exact execution-based delta and cumulative delta candles, with host-defined session or period resets and independent rolling volume and delta totals. `chart::series::render_trade_delta` draws delta histograms or cumulative candles in a supplied pane. The existing bar-estimated `CVD` remains available separately.
- `chart::overlays::render_footprint` draws up to four bid/ask, volume, delta or delta/volume columns per bar, with profile or cell fills, volume, delta or imbalance colours, POC and value-area highlights, manual or automatic row grouping and cached text layouts hidden below the minimum row height. Both dominant diagonals retain their side colours, and sub-unit volumes stay nonzero in labels. `Chart::show_footprint` replaces the price series while keeping the legend, cursor and studies above it. The demo's Menu exposes a Footprint toggle using deterministic synthetic executions from its fixture; turning it off restores the previous time scale.
- `studies::footprint_studies` identifies stacked diagonal imbalance zones, unfinished auctions, naked POCs, delta POCs, volume clusters strictly above the configured volume share and small extreme prints using a host-supplied size threshold. Stacks and naked POCs extend until the first later traded range revisits them. `chart::overlays::render_footprint_studies` and `render_footprint_statistics` paint the annotations and per-bar volume, bid, ask, delta, delta extremes, cumulative delta, delta percentage and ask/bid ratio.
- `data::Sink::push_quote` coalesces best bid/ask quotes per frame and connects the latest matching quote to the chart legend. Invalid, mismatched or older quotes do not replace the current or pending quote; reloading the feed clears it.
- `Drawing::pitchfork` stores per-level colour, visibility and line style, with mirrored levels at 0, 0.5, 1 and 1.5 and filled bands by default. Hidden levels remove both adjacent bands. Old drawings use the defaults. Parallel channels support `channel_midline`, `fill_color`, `extend_left` and `extend_right`, with selection following the lines and filled area. Migration: `Drawing` struct literals set `pitchfork` and `channel_midline`, or use `Drawing::new`.

- `Session::with_extended_windows` and `with_holidays` describe host-owned trading hours and closures. `ExchangeSessionProvider` starts each trading date at its earliest regular or extended window, handles overnight sessions, and resolves repeated opens to their first instant and missing opens to the clock jump. `Chart::set_session_provider` accepts host break logic.
- Extended-hours bars are shaded independently of session dividers, including throughout a repeated hour after an overnight open. `ChartConfig::show_extended_hours` switches shading off, and `extended_hours_color` overrides the faint active-theme accent. Migration: chart configuration literals need these fields or `..Default::default()`.
- `Chart::set_extended_hours` chooses regular hours or regular and extended hours, reloading history and the stream together. `HistoryRequest::extended_hours` and `Subscription::extended_hours` carry that choice to the host feed, including older history pages. Migration: add `extended_hours` to request and subscription literals and destructuring, or use `..` when ignoring it in a pattern. Feeds must honor the field (`false` requests regular hours); hosts supplying bars directly replace their data after a choice.

- Chart settings now retain and apply title format, quote controls and independent study title/input/value visibility. `Chart::set_indicator_inputs` supplies study input captions, retained through host templates; status-line volume no longer hides the histogram. The settings dialog keeps one top edge across tabs through `Modal::top`.
- Price scale placement and independent current/previous close labels and lines now reach rendering on either axis. Countdown visibility is honoured, with its default enabled to retain the existing displayed countdown. Migration: add `..Default::default()` to `ChartConfig`, `settings_menu::data::StatusLineOptions`, `ScalesAndLinesSettings` and `IndicatorPaneConfig` literals, or set the new visibility fields explicitly. Construct `Legend` with `Legend::new` and set its public fields; it has no `Default` implementation. Previous close is the last bar before the latest trading date.

- `TopToolbar::indicator_templates` accepts host-owned saved study names and ids. Its anchored menu emits save/apply intents; the demo keeps cloned study sets in memory for the session. Migration: replace `TopToolbarAction::OpenIndicatorTemplates` matches with `SaveIndicatorTemplate` and `ApplyIndicatorTemplate(id)` handlers. Opening the popup emits no action.

- `Symbol::with_market` supplies an exchange and optional instrument kind for symbol search. Empty queries list the host catalog, with host-supplied recents first, category chips for available kinds and kind/exchange metadata in each row. Migration: `Symbol` struct literals need `exchange` and `symbol_type`, or use `Symbol::new`.

- `TabBar::pills` renders rounded tab chips through the shared tab component; the indicator browser uses them.

- `top_toolbar::buttons::toolbar_tooltip` attaches compact toolbar tips with optional platform shortcut chips. `Modal::rounding` and `Modal::fill` preserve per-dialog styling, and an empty modal title lets a search row serve as the header.

- `Chart::set_instrument`, `set_legend_badge`, `set_legend_statuses` and `set_quote` connect host metadata and current bid/ask to the responsive legend. Historical OHLC and study values follow the pointer independently of quotes, including over the axis add cell. `take_quote_action` retains buy/sell intents until taken; changing symbols clears supplied metadata and quotes. The demo labels generated data with SIM and records quote intents without submitting orders.
- `Chart::set_session_quote` and `set_events` connect independent session prices and event badges. `take_event_click` and `take_scale_settings_request` route clicks to the host; the price-tag add cell opens the existing context menu at the displayed price, including in Magnet mode. Current close tags include a countdown when a supplied quote belongs to that bar, and volume has a separate tag at the pane bottom.
- `CrosshairStyle::Demonstration` and `CursorType::Demonstration` draw a fading pointer trail that expires while stationary. `TooltipOptions::on_long_press` restricts values to a held pointer, waiting half a second for touch; the floating panel shows OHLC, absolute and percentage change from the previous close, and volume, with one snapped guide and hollow series points. `Chart::set_tooltip_enabled` connects the values switch. Migration: exhaustive cursor matches need a Demonstration arm; tooltip struct literals need `on_long_press` or `..Default::default()`.
- The demo exposes the hovered Data Window in the right rail, quick search, a Trade intent notice and Publish through the existing export dialog. `show_data_window` is exported for hosts that display it independently of the object tree.
- `icons::COMPARE_ADD` and `icons::LAYOUT_SINGLE` provide original comparison and single-pane layout glyphs.
- `chart::render_event_band` draws host-supplied `Marker`s as 22 px badges above a supplied time-axis baseline, aligned to the first bar at or after each event time. It uses the event's text, colour, tooltip and ID, and returns the clicked ID to the host. Events outside the supplied data or plot are left out; existing bar annotations keep their appearance.
- `chart::render_quote_tags` paints independent current close, host-supplied `model::quote::SessionQuote` and abbreviated volume readouts, separating colliding tags and returning each rectangle for input and tick occlusion. `chart::price_axis_width` measures ticks and supplied formatted tag labels. Row spacing follows the host font, and off-scale prices do not hide the other quote values. Short panes keep the close before the session and volume rows. Hosts composing their own axes can route `chart::crosshair_price_action` to an add-at-price menu and `chart::axis_settings_button` to scale settings; the two controls require `icons` and return clicks to the host.
- `widget::Legend` composes an in-pane instrument title, host badge and status marks, current outlined sell/buy quotes with spread, and collapsible volume and visible overlay study rows. Its bar and previous close are supplied independently of current quotes; quote clicks return `QuoteSide` for the host to handle.
- `TextSizes` has separate `chart_title`, `toolbar`, `ohlc`, `axis`, `price_tag` and `bottom_bar` roles, defaulting to 16, 14, 13, 12, 12 and 14 points, with matching constants in `styles::typography`. `scaled` scales them with the general size scale; missing serialised fields use their defaults. Migration: add the roles to `TextSizes` struct literals or use `..Default::default()`. Component call sites choose the roles independently.
- `WidgetBar::widgets` accepts host widget icons, names, ids and top or bottom grouping. Clicks return `WidgetBarAction::WidgetClicked(id)` and switch the active entry independently of the built-in panels. The bar scrolls when its entries do not fit. Migration: an exhaustive match on `WidgetBarAction` needs a `WidgetClicked` arm.
- `Chart::set_price_lines(series, lines)` draws horizontal lines at prices on a series, such as an entry, a stop or a target. A `model::PriceLine` has a price, a colour, a width, a line style, an optional title and a price axis label that can be turned off. The lines follow the series' price scale in every mode, are hidden while their price is off the scale or not finite, and label the axis in the scale's units. The setter accepts every bar series. `model::LineStyle` is the line style of drawings and price lines; `drawings::LineStyle` remains a re-export of the same type.
- `Chart::scroll_to_time` scrolls the bar nearest a time to the centre of the chart without changing the zoom. A time outside the data shows the nearest end, and with a feed, an older time loads history until it is reached. The demo's go-to-date dialog uses it.
- The chart draws navigation buttons in a row at the bottom of the main pane: zoom out, zoom in, scroll left, scroll right, reset, and auto-scale, logarithmic and percent toggles that show the price scale's current state. Zoom moves by `KeyboardOptions::zoom_step` around the right edge, scrolling by the visible bar count. `ChartConfig::navigation_buttons` (`NavigationButtons::Always`, `OnHover` or `Never`, `Never` by default) sets when they show, and the chart settings dialog's Navigation row edits it. Migration: `ChartConfig` struct literals must set `navigation_buttons` or use `..ChartConfig::default()`.
- `IndicatorDialog::open_tab` opens the indicator dialog on a given tab; `open` opens it on the Indicators tab.
- `IndicatorDialogConfig` implements `Debug` and `Clone`.
- `ui_kit::modal::Modal::height` fixes a modal's outer height for content that fills it, such as a list in a scroll area. The modal keeps 24 px free above and below it in the window and follows the window when it shrinks; `min_height` sets only a floor, so filling content keeps a modal at its largest size.
- `studies::PercentageVolumeOscillator`, registered in `IndicatorFactory` as "PVO" with lengths 12, 26 and 9: the percentage oscillator of volume, its signal line and its histogram.
- Columns chart type: one column per bar from the bottom of the price pane to the close, coloured by the close versus the previous close.
- `ui_kit::EmptyState::icon` shows an icon above the message, `EmptyState::action` adds a primary button below the text, and `EmptyState::fill_height` centres the empty state vertically in the rest of the `Ui`. The message is in the normal text colour at the body size; only the description is muted.
- `icons::DUPLICATE`, two overlapping squares, which the floating selection toolbar's Duplicate button shows.
- `tokens::SizingTokens::control_height`, the minimum height of egui's built-in controls, 18 in `DESIGN_TOKENS`; `apply_to_egui` applies it.
- `Theme::high_contrast()` is a distinct preset: black surfaces, white text, every text colour except disabled text at 7:1 or better on panels and windows, control outlines at 4.5:1, and a light blue accent with black text. It was a copy of the dark theme.
- `Theme::with_accent` sets the accent and derives its hover and pressed shades, the black or white text on it, and the selection colours. `theme::presets()` lists the three presets by name.
- `Theme::text`, `Theme::spacing` and `Theme::radius` hold the type, spacing and corner radius scales; `apply_to_egui` takes the window and menu margins, item spacing and window and menu corner radii from them. `TextSizes::scaled` multiplies the type scale.
- The `serde` feature derives `Serialize` and `Deserialize` for `Theme`. The dark flag and complete colour palettes are required; omitted text, spacing and radius scales use their defaults.
- `data::Datafeed`, a callback contract for market data: a feed answers instrument and history requests once through a `data::Reply` (dropping it unanswered reports `FeedError::Unanswered`) and pushes live bars, trades, status and errors through a per-subscription `data::Sink`, from any thread, runtime or browser task. The sink rejects a bar or trade whose slot opens more than one slot and more than a minute after the feed's current time (two weeks for bars of a day or longer), the time being reported through `Sink::set_server_time` or else taken from the system clock. Pushes coalesce on arrival into one bar per time slot, so memory follows the bars that changed rather than the update rate. Supporting types: `HistoryRequest`, `HistoryPage`, `Subscription`, `SubscriptionId`, `FeedStatus`, `FeedError`, `FeedState` and `MaybeSend` in `data`, `Instrument` and `BarClock` in `model`. `FeedError`'s messages name no source, since the host services answer with it too: `NotSupported` reads "not supported" and `Unanswered` reads "no answer". A bar of a day or longer is identified by a period of `HistoryRequest::clock`, in UTC by default, such as its day, week from Monday or calendar month, so a feed stamps it inside that period, for example at midnight on its trading date, rather than at a session open on the evening before.
- `model::Bar` implements `PartialEq`.
- `data::resample` builds bars of a coarser interval from bars of a finer one, aligned to `BarClock`, so a feed that serves one base interval can back intervals such as 7 minutes or 2 hours. It returns `None` when the finer bars do not nest in the coarser ones, as 5-minute bars do not in 7-minute bars, or when the bars of one slot do not combine into a valid bar, as when their volumes add up to infinity.
- `theme::ThemeExt::active_theme`, for `egui::Context` and `egui::Ui`, returns the theme last passed to `apply_to_egui`, or the dark theme if none was, so components resolve colours while painting.
- `tokens::UiSemanticTokens` gains `panel_bg_floating_dark`, the fill of popups, menus and windows in dark themes. Struct literals of `UiSemanticTokens` must set it.
- A theme toggle in the top toolbar emits `TopToolbarAction::ThemeToggle`.
- `Indicator::update(data, from)` brings a study up to date after the bars from `from` onward changed or were appended, with exactly the values `calculate` gives on the same bars, and `Indicator::is_incremental` reports whether a last-bar update or an append costs time independent of the series length (an earlier change still recomputes in full). `IndicatorRegistry::update_all` updates every study. Both methods have default implementations (a full `calculate`, and `false`), so existing implementors still compile. `SMA`, `EMA`, `BollingerBands`, `RSI`, `MACD`, `Stochastic`, `ATR`, `VolumeWeightedAvgPrice`, `OnBalanceVolume` and `SuperTrend` update incrementally.
- Icons `CHECK`, `MINUS`, `CHEVRON_UP`, `CHEVRON_LEFT`, `CHEVRON_RIGHT`, `STAR_FILLED`, `CODE`, `USERS`, `CROWN`, `DATABASE`, `PENCIL` and `TEMPLATE` in `icons::icons`.
- `ListItemContent::interactive` lets content take its row out of pointer interaction. It defaults to `true`; a disabled `ButtonContent` returns `false`, so its row is neither highlighted nor clickable.
- `data::Sink::push_tick` also keeps each trade, with its price, size, aggressor side and time, in a bounded store for order flow, and `Datafeed::trades` pages historical trades (`TradeRequest`, `TradePage`) so order flow can be rebuilt after a reload. The default answers `FeedError::NotSupported`. History and live trades join exactly when both carry the new `Tick::id` (an exchange trade id or sequence number that never repeats for the symbol), and a live trade whose id the subscription already took is dropped and counted in `FeedState::repeated_ticks`, so a feed may replay its last trades after a reconnect; without ids, trades at the boundary instant may be dropped but are never counted twice.
- Order book depth on the `Datafeed` contract: `Sink::push_depth_snapshot` and `Sink::push_depth_delta` carry a sequence number; a gap, a delta before any snapshot or an invalid update clears the book until the feed pushes a new snapshot, which is asked for once through `Datafeed::request_depth_snapshot`. A delta numbered at or below the last one applied is ignored, whatever it holds. Deltas pushed while the book waits are held, up to 10,000 levels, and the snapshot applies those numbered above it, so a snapshot fetched while the stream runs heals the book. A level with size 0 is removed, and a book is limited to 10,000 levels per side. Rejected updates are counted in `FeedState::rejected_depth`.
- `model::Instrument` describes the full name (`description`), exchange, instrument type, tick size, price decimals, currency, exchange time zone (`model::TimezoneId`) and trading hours (`model::Session`, `model::SessionWindow`), each unknown by default and set with a `with_` method.
- `Instrument::data_status` (`model::DataStatus`: realtime, delayed or end of day), set with `with_data_status`. The legend marks delayed data with D and end-of-day data with E, in the warning colour ahead of the host's status marks, and names the status on hover. ([#77](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/77))
- `Instrument::supported_timeframes`, set with `with_supported_timeframes`, lists the intervals the feed answers history for; `None`, the default, keeps every interval available. A chart does not ask the feed for history of an interval its instrument rules out: it shows no bars, does not subscribe, and reports `FeedError::NotSupported` through `FeedState::last_error`. A page asked for before the symbol resolved is dropped, and an instrument that allows the interval again asks for it. Pass the list to `TimeframeSelector::set_supported_timeframes` so that the interval picker offers only those intervals. ([#699](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/699))
- `model::Tick` implements `PartialEq`.
- `DialogFooter::danger` fills the primary action with the danger colour.
- `scales::TimezoneMode`, the time axis timezone mode, now lives next to the time formatters that use it. `config::TimezoneMode` remains as a re-export of the same type.
- `ChartState::revision` identifies the bars before the last one: it changes when one of them is corrected or bars are removed, and stays the same when bars are appended or the last bar updates.
- Per-drawing settings: `Drawing::volume_profile` (`drawings::VolumeProfileConfig`, 1 to 1,000 price rows and the value area's share of the volume, 24 rows and 70% by default), `Drawing::regression_trend` (`drawings::RegressionTrendConfig`, the band distance in standard deviations, above 0 and up to 10, 2 by default) and `Drawing::position_size` (`drawings::PositionSize`, a fixed quantity, or the quantity that loses a given amount or a percentage of an account size at the stop). `None` uses the defaults. The constructors of the two configs reject values out of bounds; a position size out of bounds reads as n/a.
- `Drawing`, `DrawingToolType`, `LineStyle`, `ArrowStyle`, `FontWeight`, `TimeframeVisibility`, `FibonacciLevel`, `FibonacciConfig`, `VolumeProfileConfig`, `RegressionTrendConfig` and `PositionSize` implement serde `Serialize` and `Deserialize`. A tool type is written as its variant name; a `Drawing` leaves out its id, points and completion state; the volume profile and regression trend settings are checked against their bounds on load; the per-timeframe visibility set is written sorted, so saving the same drawings gives the same file.
- `services::Storage`, a callback contract through which the host keeps named JSON documents for the library: it lists, loads, saves and deletes the documents of a `services::StoreKind` (`Settings` or `Drawings`) and answers through a `data::Reply`, from any thread, runtime or browser task. Nothing calls it yet. ([#198](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/198))
- `services::SymbolSearch`, a service the host implements to answer symbol searches from any source of instruments, separately from the feed: it takes a `services::SymbolQuery` (the typed text, optional instrument type and exchange filters, and a result limit) and answers with `Instrument`s through a `data::Reply`. `Vec<Instrument>` implements it: it matches the text against symbol and description ignoring case, applies the filters, and lists an exact symbol first, then symbols that start with the text. Nothing calls the service yet. ([#380](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/380))
- `services::Broker`, a callback contract for one trading account at the host's broker, real or simulated: it places and cancels orders, answering through a `data::Reply`, and pushes the account, orders, positions, executions and connection status as `services::AccountEvent`s into an `Events` stream. The trading model is in `model`: `OrderType`, `OrderRequest`, `Order`, `OrderStatus`, `Position` (signed net quantity), `Execution`, `Account` and `Capabilities` (the order types the account accepts). The library holds no balances or positions. Nothing calls the service yet. ([#216](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/216))
- `services::Signals`, through which a host supplies markers, levels and zones from its own analytics (`model::Signal`, `SignalShape`, `SignalId`, `Direction`) as `services::SignalEvent`s, pushed from any thread into a `services::Events` stream that holds at most 10,000 pushes between frames and ends when it lags or the chart stops listening ([#381](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/381)).
- `model::StrategyReport` describes a strategy run from any source: its trades, each with the net profit the host computed, its equity curve and the settings to show. `StrategyReport::performance` computes the metrics with one definition each: net, gross and average profit, profit factor, win rate, maximum drawdown in money and as a fraction of the peak, and the Sharpe and Sortino ratios, annualised by the steps per year the equity curve covers and unavailable for a curve spanning less than 13 weeks. An undefined metric is `None`, invalid trades and equity points are skipped and counted, a non-finite number is saved as `null` in JSON and loads back as NaN, so a report with one loads with the same metrics, and `StrategyReport::trades_csv` exports the valid trades. Supporting types: `StrategyTrade`, `StrategyExit`, `OrderSide` and `StrategyPerformance`.
- `services::Alerts`, through which the host keeps and evaluates price alerts: it pushes its alerts, their changes and their triggers as `services::AlertEvent`s into a `services::Events` stream, including triggers that fired while the application was closed, and saves or removes the alerts the user edits, answering through a `data::Reply`. The alert model (`model::Alert`, `AlertId`, `AlertCondition`, `AlertFrequency`, `AlertStatus`) is serialisable so a server can store and evaluate the same type. The alerts panel consumes the service. ([#211](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/211))
- The Negative and Positive Volume Index plot their signal line, the 255-bar EMA of the index seeded with its first value, so it starts at bar 0 like the index. The index is unchanged. Migration: `NegativeVolumeIndex` and `PositiveVolumeIndex` output `IndicatorValue::Multiple(vec![index, signal])` instead of `Single(index)`, and report two lines and two colours; code that matched `Single` reads the index as the first value.
- `AlertKind::color` and `ToastKind::color` return the theme's status colour for an alert or toast kind.
- `MessageKind::Warning`: `status_message` draws it in the theme's warning colour. Migration: a `match` on `MessageKind` needs a `Warning` arm.
- `TopToolbarState::can_undo` and `can_redo` disable the top toolbar's undo and redo buttons, whose tooltips then read "Nothing to undo" and "Nothing to redo"; both default to `true`. Migration: struct literals of `TopToolbarState` set both fields or use `..Default::default()`.
- `Chart::set_data(series, bars)` replaces the bars, for history or a replay window, and `Chart::update(series, bar)` merges one bar by the rules a feed's `Sink::push_bar` follows, matched by open time, or by the slot of the timeframe for a chart built with a feed: a bar that matches a stored bar replaces it, a newer one is appended and an older one is inserted in order. `Chart::bars(series)` reads the bars, and `Chart::feed_state(SeriesId::MAIN).rejected_bars` counts the bars dropped as invalid. Only `SeriesId::MAIN` holds bars; a call with another id is ignored with a warning. `SeriesId` is re-exported from `widget`.
- `Chart` owns its studies and drawings. `Chart::add_indicator` computes a study at once and returns a `SeriesId` that stays valid when other studies are removed; `remove_series`, `set_indicator_visible`, `indicators` and `indicator_mut` take that id. `Chart::drawings` and `drawings_mut` give the drawing manager. `ChartElementId` is re-exported from `widget`.
- `Chart::config`, `Chart::chart_options`, `Chart::price_scale_mut`, `Chart::set_markers(series, markers)` and `Chart::state` give access to what the public fields held.
- The `bare_chart` example shows one candlestick chart on generated sample data, with a live tick every frame and a new bar every 60, built from the `widget` feature alone: `cargo run --release --example bare_chart --no-default-features --features widget`. The `bare_chart` bench runs that example's frame headlessly and prints its median and 95th percentile time: `cargo bench --bench bare_chart --no-default-features --features widget`.
- CI checks the bare-chart build: on ubuntu-24.04 with the MSRV toolchain it builds `examples/bare_chart.rs` and `benches/bare_chart.rs` with `--no-default-features --features widget` and fails when the stripped size of the example grows by more than 1%, or the instructions of one bench frame under Cachegrind grow by more than 2%, over the figures committed in `benches/bare_chart.baseline`. The job summary reports both figures with the unchecked median and 95th percentile frame time and prints the baseline lines a pull request commits to accept growth. ([#441](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/441))
- The `frame_time` bench renders one chart of 5,000,000 bars zoomed out and zoomed in, four charts of 100,000 bars and eight charts of 100,000 bars with ten studies each, headlessly on sample data, and prints the median frame and tessellation time of each as a Markdown table, then the first frame and the median and 95th percentile of steady frames, of frames with a tick on the last bar and of frames with a new bar, for candles and the Renko, Kagi, Line Break, Point and Figure and range bar charts on 1,000,000 one-minute bars: `cargo bench --bench frame_time`. CONTRIBUTING.md describes how to compare two commits with it.
- The `stream` bench feeds one chart 10,000 trades a second from a thread, with 10 or 30 studies on 100,000 and 1,000,000 bars and frames paced at 60 Hz on the wall clock, and prints the median, 99th percentile and longest frame of each, and how often a study that does not update incrementally is recomputed: `cargo bench --bench stream`.
- `FloatingReplayControl::set_dock_rect` docks the floating replay bar in a rect, usually the chart's: the bar opens centred 8 px above the rect's bottom edge and can be dragged only within it. Without a dock rect the bar docks in the window's content rect.
- `ReplayColors::from_theme` builds the replay colours from a theme, and `ReplayColors` implements `PartialEq`.
- `ui::context_menu::ChartMenus` shows the chart, series and drawing context menus for any `Chart` (feature `ui`): call `menus.show(ctx, &mut chart)` after `chart.show(ui)` to open the menu for the last right-click and read the chosen action. While a menu is open the chart draws no crosshair or tooltip under it.
- `ChartBuilder::with_feed` drives a chart from a `data::Datafeed`. The chart asks for the instrument and the latest page of history without blocking, applies answers and live updates once per frame, asks for older pages as the user scrolls back until the feed reports the end of history, subscribes after the instrument resolves and unsubscribes on every symbol or timeframe change and when it is dropped. A push from any thread wakes the UI once per burst. `Chart::feed_state` reports loading, live status, the last error and dropped updates. After a history or instrument error the chart asks for nothing more until the symbol or timeframe changes.
- `Chart::set_timeframe`, `Chart::symbol`, `Chart::timeframe` and `Chart::instrument`.
- `Chart::trades` and `Chart::order_book` hold the trades and the order book the feed pushes; the chart asks the feed once for recent trades (`Datafeed::trades`) and for a new book snapshot when the book is lost.
- `examples/live_feed.rs`: a feed that serves pages of history and streams trades from a thread.
- `ui::Control` and `set_control_visible` on `TopToolbar`, `DrawingToolbar`, `TimeframeToolbar`, `WidgetBar`, `ChartMenus` and `SettingsDialog`: a host hides the controls it has no service for, such as alerts, trading, replay, layouts, templates, compare, time zone, session or dividend adjustment. A hidden control is not drawn and emits nothing, and a menu of it that is open closes; every control starts visible. In the chart settings dialog, Reset to defaults takes the place of the hidden template button.
- The drawing bar uses the chart's shortcut map for tool keys and hints; standalone toolbars use `DrawingToolShortcuts`. `DrawingToolShortcuts::help_text` formats those bindings as key labels such as "Alt+T". Chart defaults and migration are listed below.
- A star in a drawing bar flyout row adds the tool to the favourites or removes it, without selecting the tool; a favourite shows a filled star in the warning colour.
- `ToolbarState::lock_all` and `ToolbarState::hide_drawings`, `hide_indicators`, `hide_positions` and `hide_orders` hold what the Lock button and the Hide menu show: the lock button is filled with a closed padlock while `lock_all` is set, and the Hide menu checks each hidden group. The bar sets them from the clicks it reports; a host that changes the chart's state elsewhere sets them too. Migration: struct literals of `ToolbarState` set the five fields or use `..ToolbarState::default()`.

### Fixed

- Replayed trades with order ids find their retained fills by id in constant time, including individual pushes, drained bursts and overlapping history pages, so reconnects no longer scan the trade store for every fill. Missing order ids are still completed without counting the fill again. ([#1245](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1245))
- The layout menu's Autosave toggle, the trading lines' order types, take profit and stop loss labels, tick results, tooltips and Buy and Sell button, and the legend's series and study controls, quote boxes, delayed and end-of-day marks and hints and copied values go through the context's translator, so one that changes every key reaches them without recreating the chart. The volume tag on the price scale takes the applied theme's rising or falling candle colour for the last bar instead of a fixed pink, marker outlines take the theme's crosshair label text colour, and event break lines its info colour, so a custom palette reaches them; a marker's own colour is unchanged. ([#1059](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1059))
- A footprint built from the feed's trades drops the contribution of each trade the bounded store evicts, summing the bar that lost trades again from those held so its delta high and low agree with them, and keeps at most 5,000 bars and 50,000 price rows, the oldest bars first. `Footprint::complete_from` gives the open of the oldest bar whose executions are all held; bars before it, cut by eviction or the budget, are drawn as candles instead of as complete footprint bars. ([#1114](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1114))

- Fixed range and anchored volume profile drawings use the executions of `ChartView::executions` when they hold any in the selected bars: `Footprint::profile` sums the bars from the first anchor to the last, each price row shows its buy, sell and unclassified volume as executed, and the POC and value area follow `FootprintBar`, so they equal the public exact profile and follow the anchors and any new trades. `VolumeProfile::source` (`ProfileSource`) tells an estimate from bars, labelled "Estimated from bars" on the chart, from executions, labelled "Partial executions" when none are held from the span's first bar, and `Drawing::volume_profile_of` returns the profile a drawing shows. Executed profiles use the footprint's price rows, so the drawing's row count and row size apply to estimates only; its value area and display settings apply to both. The split display paints unclassified volume apart instead of as sell volume. The chart supplies the executions it holds while its chart type is the volume footprint or the session volume profile. Migration: `ChartView` literals add `executions: None`. ([#1070](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1070))

- TPO charts highlight the rows touched by a single period behind their letters when `TPOConfig::show_single_prints` is on, as `ChartTypeParams::tpo_single_prints` (the series settings Symbol tab's Single prints and the Time Price Opportunity study's Single prints input) sets it, and hovering one names its price. The highlight colour is `TpoRenderConfig::single_print_color`. Turning it off leaves the letters, POC, value area and initial balance as they were. Migration: `ChartTypeParams` literals add `tpo_single_prints: true`. ([#1113](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1113))
- Price scale labels that sit closer than a label is high, such as price lines, order and position lines, the previous close and the values of one study, stack in the order of their prices instead of in the order they were added, centred on where they wanted to be. The last price, extended session and volume labels keep their place, and labels further apart keep their exact positions. ([#1172](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1172))
- Long and short position tools keep their target and stop labels inside the plot: a label that would pass the right edge flips to the inner side of the line's end, and placement elsewhere is unchanged. ([#1171](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1171))
- The upside and downside Tasuki gap patterns need the third candle to open strictly inside the second candle's body and close strictly inside the gap, so an open outside the body, a close on either gap edge or a closed gap is no longer marked. ([#1083](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1083))

- A deselected rectangle draws its border, fill and label only. Its centre guides and size labels appear while it is selected or being drawn, and its corner dots, which duplicated the selection handles, are gone. ([#1169](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1169))

- Footprint cells and statistics columns at the edges of the plot show complete numbers or none; a number the plot edge would cut is left out. ([#1170](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1170))

- The magnet crosshair also snaps to the visible outputs of studies on shared, independent and unlabelled scales and to the values of visible comparison series, each converted through its own scale. Hidden, missing and non-finite values are skipped, and an equal distance keeps the bar's own price first, then studies, then series. ([#1107](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1107))

- `ChartLayout` crosshair sync forwards the source chart's main-pane price with its time, so synchronized charts show the horizontal line and price label at the same price. Over volume or an indicator pane only the time is shared, and both clear when the pointer leaves. ([#1108](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1108))

- Chart undo covers a user's symbol navigation and comparison edits. `Chart::set_symbol` called inside `Chart::user_action` no longer clears history: undo and redo restore the symbol, interval and viewport and reload the feed for it, dropping answers still owed for the other symbol, and earlier steps stay with their own symbol's drawings. Adding, removing, restyling, hiding, reordering and rescaling an added series or comparison are undo steps that keep its `SeriesId`, scale assignment and removed bars. A symbol change outside `user_action` still clears history. Comparison data stays with the host: after `Chart::take_history_changed`, compare `Chart::series` with the host's own subscriptions by `SeriesId` to restart or stop them. ([#1053](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1053))

- Linked drawings in `ChartLayout` include study panes: `Chart::linked_pane` maps a study pane to the pane of another chart holding the same studies (equal names and inputs, then by order among identical panes), and drawings follow their timestamps into the mapped pane. Linked edits, unlocks and deletions now reach locked copies, so unlocking or deleting a drawing no longer leaves a locked copy in the other charts; direct edits of a locked drawing still meet its lock. ([#1067](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1067))

- A typed comma opens interval entry, empty and under the same focus and text-field rules as a leading digit, instead of symbol search. In a multi-chart `ChartLayout`, Alt+Enter shows the focused tile alone and a second press restores the preset with every slot, viewport and synchronization group unchanged, and a lone chart still hands the shortcut to the host. Double-clicking a study pane maximizes or restores it like its button; study settings stay on the legend button and the context menu. ([#1066](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1066))

- The saved scale font size, axis line colour and pane button visibility apply: `ChartConfig::axis_font_size` sizes the price and time axis labels, the line colour draws the border of the main, added-series and study-pane price axes and the time axis, and `ChartConfig::pane_buttons` shows the pane maximize and menu buttons always, on hover or never, in maximized panes too. The settings dialog gains Lines, Font size and Pane buttons controls, and all three read back from the chart and survive snapshots and undo. `ChartConfig::left_axis_scale_mode` sets the main scale when the left axis carries it, as `right_axis_scale_mode` does on the right. Study pane menu buttons now show on hover by default, like the setting says. Migration: `ChartConfig` literals add `axis_font_size: None` and `pane_buttons: NavigationButtons::OnHover` or use defaults. ([#1060](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1060))
- During replay, studies that request another symbol or interval read only the bars their history completed by the replay cursor, so the chart's unfinished bar no longer shows the closing value of the other history's bar in the same slot; that slot has no value until its bar closes. Moving the cursor in either direction recalculates those studies, and leaving replay restores the full histories. Studies that read the bars alone, such as ATR and ADX, offer the Interval and Wait for interval close inputs as single-source studies do, with their own inputs unchanged; studies that request data of their own or read trades do not. ([#1049](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1049))
- The study Style tab lists every plot type, step line, histogram, columns, area, circles, crosses, triangles, bar colour and background colour, instead of line, histogram and circles. A type that cannot be drawn for the study stays visible but disabled with the reason in its tooltip: bar colour for a study outside the price chart, and the zero-based histogram, columns and area on the price scale; a saved choice is never removed. `PlotType::ALL`, `PlotType::name` and `PlotType::incompatibility` expose the list, the labels and the rule. New Net Volume studies draw columns, and new PPO and PVO studies draw their oscillator and signal as lines and their difference as columns; styles already saved keep their plot types. ([#1082](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1082))
- `Chart::restore` keeps the colours the user edited and lets the others follow the active theme, so a layout saved under a light theme no longer opens with a light chart under a dark one. `ChartSnapshot::palette` records the colours the configuration followed; layouts saved without it are compared with the built-in light and dark themes. Migration: `ChartSnapshot` literals add `palette: None`. ([#1163](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1163))
- The triangle pattern draws both boundaries through its zigzag anchors, runs them on to the apex where they meet and fills between them up to it, instead of closing the anchors as one polygon. Boundaries that are parallel, diverge or meet before the first anchor stop at the last anchor, and a far apex is cut at the chart's edge. ([#1111](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1111))
- Kagi lines change thickness where a continuing line crosses the prior shoulder or waist and are painted in two parts there, instead of only at reversals. `ChartTypeStyles::kagi` sets the thick and thin stroke widths, 2.5 and 1.5 points by default, which the series settings Symbol tab edits and which serialize with the chart type settings. Migration: `KagiLine` literals add `crossing: None`; `ChartTypeStyles` literals add `kagi` or use defaults. ([#1061](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1061))
- RSI divergence pivots and divergences treat RSI values within 1e-9 as equal, so a flat bar, which leaves the RSI unchanged, no longer makes a tie between neighbouring pivots depend on floating-point rounding. ([#1089](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1089))

- RSI, Connors RSI and the RSI divergence study compute `100 * gain / (gain + loss)` from Wilder's averages, with no absolute loss floor, so rescaling the prices leaves the values unchanged. A window with no loss is 100, one with no gain is 0, and a fully flat window is 50, for the price and streak components of Connors RSI alike. ([#1089](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1089))

- Value tags and legend values of added series use their own price scale's decimals instead of a fixed two, while a host formatter set for the series still writes them. ([#1064](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1064))

- Volume candle bodies are as wide as the bar's volume over the largest visible volume, with no hidden minimum: a zero or missing volume draws a body as thin as the wick, and without any visible volume every body keeps the full width. ([#1104](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1104))
- The demo page declares the project icon, so the tab shows it and no favicon request fails. The module preload no longer carries an integrity attribute, which browsers ignore for that destination and warn about. ([#1044](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1044))
- Range charts build their bars from the executions the feed supplied, in order with their timestamps and volume and with the incomplete last bar, so two execution paths with the same open, high, low and close show their own bars. History pages, corrections, live trades and the replay cursor rebuild them. Charts without executions keep the approximation from the bars' open, high, low and close, and `Chart::range_bar_source` reports which one is shown as `model::RangeBarSource`.
