([#1105](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1105))

- `Drawing::alert_levels` resolves a sloping line as the drawing is painted: straight on the price axis, so it follows the geometric mean on a logarithmic scale, and gives no price for a vertical line or a non-positive price there. Parallel and disjoint channels return their base line and second line, and a flat top/bottom its trend line and flat line, as levels 0 and 1 under stable alert sources. Migration: `Drawing::alert_levels` takes the chart's `PriceScaleMode` as a second argument. ([#1048](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1048))

- The Elliott impulse projects its 1.618 wave 3 target from the wave 2 end by the wave 1 price leg, rising or falling with wave 1 and unchanged by zoom; `fib_config.logarithmic` projects the leg's log return instead. The Elliott triangle collects an origin and waves A to E, labelled 0 and A to E, with the fill, trendlines and handles on A to E. Migration: `DrawingToolType::ElliottTriangle` needs and requires 6 anchors, and a triangle saved with 5 loads as a skipped drawing (`Elliott Triangle needs 6 anchors, has 5`) in `DrawingStorage::from_json`'s result rather than gaining an invented origin; redraw it. ([#1068](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1068))

- Three drives labels each correction's retracement of the drive before it and each drive's extension of the correction before it, measured in price, and omits a ratio over a zero-length leg. The reversal zone is the price interval 1.272 to 1.618 second-correction lengths beyond the correction's end, painted through the active price scale, in place of a fixed-size badge. ([#1071](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1071))

- Fibonacci retracement and extension fills span the same horizontal bounds as their level lines and hit regions. Channel levels extended left or right run on to the visible pane edge on that side and keep the anchor on a side that is not extended. ([#1069](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1069))
- Returning demo visitors follow the system appearance, including live changes, until they pick a theme. Palettes saved automatically by earlier releases are ignored without discarding other settings; new choices persist in the app and on the loading page. ([#1043](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1043))
- The interval toolbar starts without favourites and shows one button for the current interval. Starred intervals appear in menu order, followed by the last selected non-favourite and an icon-only menu trigger, without repeating an interval. Selecting a favourite retains the non-favourite button. Toolbar buttons abbreviate one day, week and month to `D`, `W` and `M`. ([#1041](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1041))

- Box zoom uses a faint fill and visible border from the active theme's info colour, with unmultiplied alpha. Its handles and dimension labels follow the theme. ([#608](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/608))

- `CurrencyFormatter::eur()` writes `1.234,56 €` for `1234.56`. Matching thousands and decimal separators omit grouping. Migration: `CurrencyFormatter` literals add `decimal_sep` (`','` for EUR, `'.'` for USD, BTC and custom currencies), or use a constructor. ([#676](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/676))

- The price axis menu offers Scale price chart only, and the quick setting applies `ChartConfig::scale_price_chart_only`. It excludes overlay studies and their primitives from the main price range without hiding them; turning it off restores their individual autoscale participation. Other scales keep their own ranges. Saved configurations without the option default to including studies. ([#964](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/964))

- Changing symbols returns the main price scale to autoscaling, clears its price-to-bar ratio lock and discards the previous symbol's zoom history, so prices outside a manually fixed range remain visible. Repeating the symbol or replacing or reloading its data keeps manual scaling. Initial ratio-lock settings are preserved. ([#955](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/955))
- Maximized indicator panes keep plot dragging, wheel and pinch zoom, axis scaling and reset, clicks and drawing placement. Crosshair positions reach host readouts in either pane order. Restoring the layout retains the shared time position and zoom without moving the hidden price pane's manual range. ([#1030](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1030))
- The demo follows the system light or dark appearance at startup and live until a theme is explicitly selected. Explicit choices remain saved between sessions, and the web loading screen uses the same saved choice or system appearance before the app starts. ([#1032](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1032))

- Drawing sync notifications coalesce repeated saves and updates per drawing, and automatic storage disables them, so the queue no longer grows with every edit. Migration: hosts consuming both paths call `chart.drawings_mut().set_sync_enabled(true)` after `Chart::set_storage`. ([#948](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/948))
- Saving an alert with unchanged expiry fields preserves its exact saved instant, including seconds and the later occurrence of a repeated local time when clocks go back. Edited date/time fields still resolve in the explicit entry zone and validate before saving. ([#981](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/981))

- Session volume profiles keep their histogram visible when the session opens before the viewport, retaining the whole session's executed volumes and their proportions. ([#967](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/967))
- Broker comparisons keep long names on one line and values inside their columns, with full text available on hover, so host-supplied descriptions cannot overlap other brokers or misalign their Connect buttons. ([#1003](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1003))
- Financial views keep their return button visible and their tabs, chart and table scrollable in narrow or short windows. Long row names, period labels and figures stay inside their columns. Charts retain period labels for zero-only and unavailable rows, treat non-finite host values as unavailable and scale finite gains and losses without overflowing their range. ([#1002](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1002))
- Symbol search opens at 840 by 680 points, centred and constrained to the window, with 32-point rows and wrapping categories. `Instrument::logo` and `exchange_logo` supply marks with text fallbacks. Search and Compare share 20-point corners and a light backdrop near #F1F1F1 over white; dark themes retain their overlay. `Modal::backdrop` allows a host override. ([#998](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/998), [#1012](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1012))

- Quick search opens with an empty, focused query and an instruction to type. Commands are listed, and the best match highlighted, only once the query matches them; Enter still runs the highlighted match. ([#1000](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1000))
- Study appearance and input edits keep settings snapshots in chart undo history instead of copying calculated columns. Undo and redo restore the saved inputs and presentation and recompute against current data; removed studies retain their original implementation. ([#944](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/944))

- Drawing sync notifications coalesce per drawing and overflow after 1,024 actions into `DrawingSyncAction::ResyncRequired`. Hosts replace their stored set with the manager's saveable drawings, deleting absent records. Automatic storage disables notifications. Migration: hosts consuming both paths call `chart.drawings_mut().set_sync_enabled(true)` after `Chart::set_storage`; enabling requests a full replacement. ([#948](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/948))

- Drawing autosave retains failed documents until storage acknowledges them, retries after one second of input time, and keeps the latest edits through delayed writes and symbol changes. `Chart::flush_stored_drawings` retries immediately and reports whether captured edits are acknowledged; storage remains host-owned. ([#942](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/942))

- Starred intervals appear in the top toolbar as one-click buttons after the interval menu, in the menu's order, with the current interval's button pressed. Unstarring an interval removes its button, and intervals the source cannot serve are left out. ([#973](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/973))
- Table, post and idea drawings follow the active surface and text colours in both themes. Ghost feed labels keep readable text while the path fades. Image states, empty content prompts and ghost labels use the host translation lookup. ([#984](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/984), [#985](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/985), [#986](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/986))
- Table editors keep their cells and wrapping row and column controls within narrow panes by scrolling them together. ([#984](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/984))
- Drawing alert sources escape `%` and `#` in stored drawing identities, keeping a drawing such as `a#1` distinct from level 1 of drawing `a`. Migration: regenerate saved alert sources containing those identity characters with `Drawing::alert_source`; numeric identities are unchanged. ([#980](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/980))
- The demo loads image drawing addresses over HTTP and HTTPS as well as native files, so image input works in browser builds. The host still supplies image storage and access. ([#985](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/985))
- Drawing-menu alerts start on the first visible Fibonacci level even when level zero is hidden; drawings with no visible levels offer no alert. ([#980](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/980))
- The demo plays selected local execution and alert sounds with independent volume and mute controls. Test sound previews the selected tone and enables browser audio; playback failures are reported. `TradingSettings` literals add `execution_sound_volume`; `AlertsSettings` literals add `play_sound`, `sound` and `sound_volume`, or use `..Default::default()`. Saved settings default the new fields. Native demo builds require a C++ compiler and CMake for the audio backend. External notification delivery remains host-owned. ([#982](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/982))

- Demo alerts retain independent live symbol sources and their creation interval across symbol changes and replay. Watchlist members have separate occurrence state and log their actual triggering symbol through `AlertEvent::TriggeredForSymbol`. Editing source references refreshes the retained study definitions without changing the alert's clock or session; expiry also works with an empty watchlist. Monitoring lasts while the demo runs; durable evaluation, market feeds and remote delivery remain host-owned. ([#961](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/961))

- The demo saves comparison symbols and arithmetic spreads, including styles, visibility, pane positions and price scales, beside each layout's chart snapshots, and recreates their synthetic feed bindings on restore. Save As, rename and autosave retain these definitions; older layouts open without comparisons. ([#960](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/960))

- Chart settings use the active representation's Symbol controls for line styles, area and baseline fills, and price-based construction inputs. The demo's top gear, price-axis gear, main-series menu and main-series double-click open the same seven-tab dialog with current values. `ChartSettingsState` literals add `chart_type`, `chart_type_params` and `price_source`, or use `..Default::default()`; older saved settings retain the chart's existing parameters. `SettingsAction::Apply` carries `Box<ChartSettingsState>`; dereference it when passing settings to an owned setter. ([#965](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/965), [#966](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/966))

- The demo selects Range bars with a range interval and sizes them in instrument ticks. Copy price writes the selected price to the clipboard at instrument precision. Fresh sessions start with both crosshair strokes; saved cursor choices are retained. ([#968](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/968), [#969](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/969), [#970](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/970))
- Built-in studies, including KST, Ichimoku and Parabolic SAR, update changed bars and appends without recalculating unaffected history. Retrospective studies refresh affected pivots, legs and calendar periods. Migration: direct callers of `Indicator::update_data` must pass `from = 0` when a requested history changes; nonzero `from` reuses requested histories. The chart supplies this invalidation. ([#946](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/946))
- Point and Figure columns retain their price endpoints and saturate inclusive box counts at `usize::MAX` rather than overflowing, including on 32-bit targets. `PnfColumn::boxes` rejects invalid geometry or expansion above two million boxes and reports allocation failures. Migration: `boxes()` now returns `Result<Vec<f64>, String>`; handle the error before using the prices, or use the column endpoints without expanding. ([#943](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/943))

- Cached Renko and range chart updates apply the same two-million-size work bound as fresh builds, reseeding after an excessive price move and resuming from that price on later bars. Whitespace does not bypass the bound. ([#941](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/941))
- Custom intervals retain positive week and calendar-month counts, with 3, 6 and 12 month presets. Bar boundaries follow the configured calendar timezone, so April 15 belongs to the quarter opening on April 1; session closes and strategy comparisons also retain calendar months. Month durations remain approximate for sizing, and previously saved `Custom(seconds)` intervals keep their fixed-duration meaning. ([#963](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/963))
- The drawing rail optically sizes its trend, Fibonacci and ruler glyphs within their existing button slots. Default light ink is black; dark and custom theme colours, disabled contrast and selected utility fills are retained. ([#1001](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1001))

- The magnet menu independently includes or excludes visible study targets through Snap to indicators, preserving OHLC, drawing anchors and the chosen magnet strength. `DrawingToolbarAction::SetSnapToIndicators` carries the host preference; toolbar snapshots retain it and the demo applies it. Migration: `DrawingManagerOptions`, `ToolbarState` and `DrawingToolbarDomainState` literals add `snap_to_indicators: true` (or use defaults where available); `MagnetButton::show_submenu` takes that preference as its fourth argument. ([#979](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/979))

- Position drawings size risk in lots using point value and lot size, round down to a configured quantity step, display its precision, convert profit and loss, and report required capital using leverage. Both position Inputs tabs expose the economics; saved drawings and defaults retain them, and older drawings keep unit sizing. Summaries wrap inside the chart, retain compact quantities at extreme magnitudes and disappear when panned out of view. Migration: `Drawing` literals add `position_sizing: PositionSizing::default()` or use `Drawing::new`; hosts supply point values and lot conventions in the account currency. ([#959](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/959))

- Two-point drawings accept successive primary clicks with a live preview as well as dragging; secondary drags leave the preview unfinished. Drag and freehand anchors retain the original press, including its snapping, and freehand strokes include the first movement and release. Holding Shift constrains rectangle previews and completed drawings to a square in screen points. Escape cancels unfinished placement. Existing interaction-mode queries and placement cursor feedback are retained. ([#956](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/956), [#957](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/957), [#958](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/958))
- Notifications default to the bottom-left corner with a 16-point inset. The demo fits notices beside the order ticket and hides them while no room remains; trading notices respect the configured width. Hosts can still select any corner with `NotificationPanel::position`. ([#1009](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1009))

- Held values show six base rows: time, open, high, low, close and total volume. Change remains in hover readouts. Volume study plots are opt-in through `TooltipOptions::show_volume_details`; other study values and host data windows retain their existing rows. Older saved tooltip options default to hiding volume details. ([#1008](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1008))
- Selecting a report trade in the demo restores the session used by its backtest after switching symbols, so the selected trade remains visible without changing the report scope.

- The registry documentation guards its built-in study example with the `studies` feature, so the documented headless configuration compiles every example. CI also runs doctests with no default features. ([#951](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/951))

- Restoring a layout rejects main or pane scales requesting more than 1,000 tick marks before changing the chart. Direct tick generation caps the limit at 1,000, and built-in numeric formatters cap precision at 324 places even when fields are set directly, preventing overflow and formatting panics. Migration: lower saved `tick_marks.max_marks` values above 1,000 before restoring; formatter precisions above 324 now produce at most 324 decimal places. ([#947](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/947))

- Bar CSV/JSON imports and exports and `Chart::export_csv` return errors with `Send + Sync` bounds, allowing results and original error values to cross worker-thread boundaries. Migration: update explicitly typed results, function pointers and error adapters from `Box<dyn std::error::Error>` to `Box<dyn std::error::Error + Send + Sync>`. ([#950](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/950))

- Session range lookup clips its final one-minute check to the requested end and uses checked timestamp addition, so sub-minute ranges do not report a later break and ranges at the maximum UTC timestamp do not panic. Empty and reversed ranges remain empty. ([#953](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/953))

- The example dependency locks replace yanked `clipboard-win` 5.4.1 with 5.4.3. CI checks advisories as well as licences and duplicate versions, rejecting yanked packages. Three unmaintained standalone D-Bus packages required by the egui 0.37 accessibility backend have explicit maintenance exceptions pending its migration to zbus 6; these are development/example dependencies and have no patched standalone releases. ([#952](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/952))

- Numeric formatters omit the minus sign for negative zero and values that round to zero at their displayed precision. Currency amounts such as -0.001 dollars read `$0.00`; price, volume and study labels follow the same rule, while percentage changes retain their positive-zero convention. ([#938](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/938))

- `CurrencyFormatter` puts the minus sign of a negative amount before a leading currency symbol and no longer groups it: -123.45 in dollars reads `-$123.45` and -123456 reads `-$123,456.00`, where they read `$-,123.45` and `$-,123,456.00`. ([#607](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/607))

- Selecting a drawing in the object tree reveals offscreen anchors by panning while keeping zoom and the price scale's auto/manual state. Double-click and Zoom to object use the same navigation instead of fitting a manual range to one drawing. `Chart::reset_chart_view` restores configured bar spacing and right margin, scrolls to the latest bar, cancels pending view motion and returns every price scale in every pane to auto-scaling. Chart-menu, keyboard, navigation-button and demo resets use this method. ([#935](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/935))
- The command palette consumes Enter in search and Enter or Space on a focused row before returning a command, so a host can open an export dialog without also submitting its empty save path. ([#834](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/834))

- The scroll-to-latest button is a theme-aware 28-point double chevron at the bottom right of the main pane, clear of the legend and navigation, with matching paint and hit rectangles, a pointer cursor and “Scroll to the most recent bar” tooltip. Clicking or using the return shortcut animates to the configured right margin without changing zoom. The button is hidden at the live edge and available in history or future whitespace. Custom position, size, text and colours remain supported; `realtime_button_text: None` selects the icon and `Color32::PLACEHOLDER` colours follow the active theme. ([#930](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/930))

- The study rotation regression checks that every study gets a refresh with both fast and slow recomputes, without assuming an exact count tied to the runner's speed. ([#927](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/927))
- Replay resets clear remote-symbol, alternate-interval and metric study histories, tick-origin bookkeeping, errors and paging state before merging the new position, and invalidate dependent calculations. ([#900](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/900))
- Study sources retry recoverable resolve and history failures with exponential backoff capped at 60 seconds, honoring longer rate-limit delays. Retries retain the original page boundary and count, success clears the error, and paused or replaced sources cancel pending recovery. Replies from superseded requests are ignored. ([#908](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/908))

- Live subscriptions retain at most 100,000 undrained bar slots independently of raw trade capacity. Overflow evicts the oldest slots, increments `FeedState::rejected_bars` and reports `InvalidData` through `last_error`, asking the host to reload history. Explicit historical execution batches retain their complete output. ([#901](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/901))
- Time-based streams reject executions older than the last accepted execution, including inside the forming candle and across frame drains, so delayed trades cannot overwrite the chronological close. `aggregate_trades` enforces the same ordering contract. Equal timestamps remain valid and replay seeks reset the watermark. ([#903](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/903))

- Drawing saves retain the nanosecond remainder after each millisecond, preserving distinct execution-bar anchors when restored immediately, before history loads, through drawing paste or when older placements gain editable controls. Millisecond-only documents remain readable. Migration: `StoredCoord` literals add `subms_ns: 0`, or use `StoredCoord::new` for millisecond coordinates. ([#899](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/899))

- Watchlist service pushes and `set_quote` reject older timestamps and non-finite required or supplied numeric fields, retaining the last usable quote for the watchlist and host consumers. Equal timestamps can still correct prices. ([#904](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/904))

- Depth snapshots resolve duplicate tick prices before removing zero sizes, so the last supplied level removes earlier liquidity on either side. ([#909](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/909))

- `SettingsStore::flush` retries unchanged settings after a failed save and confirms them only after a successful acknowledgement. Edits made during a pending save keep the latest choice; repeated failures are reported once until a write succeeds. ([#907](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/907))
- The account panel keeps the newest 1,000 entries per completed-history category and releases expired order metadata. Active orders, cumulative partial fills and the latest fill per symbol survive eviction; older history remains host-owned. ([#902](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/902))
- `PaperAccount` checks placement, modification and gap fills against account-wide equity, position margin, working orders and commissions. Refused modifications leave the order intact; reductions and position exits remain available with insufficient funds. Account summaries use the current marks and reserve working-order costs. ([#905](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/905))
- `PaperAccount` accepts only good-till-cancelled orders until session boundaries are available. Migration: direct callers set `OrderRequest::time_in_force` to `TimeInForce::GoodTillCancelled`. The ticket and confirmed position actions use the broker's symbol rules, including for positions outside the chart's symbol. ([#906](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/906))

- Studies sourced from another study continue refreshing during continuous ticks, within the existing full-recompute budget. ([#911](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/911))

- Native trackpad pinches zoom around the pointer and stop tracking and kinetic scrolling. Contact input takes precedence when both are reported, and wheel zoom remains applied once. ([#918](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/918))

- Touch panning respects its horizontal and vertical permissions independently of mouse dragging and pinch scaling, including two-finger translation and the transition back to one finger. Price and time axis drags use their own scaling permissions even when plot panning is disabled and retain the gesture after leaving the axis; disabled axis gestures do not pan the plot. ([#919](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/919), [#920](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/920))

- Small pans and anchored zooms retain fractional movement on large histories. Synchronized UTC ranges, added series and pointer actions keep sub-bar precision. Fitting and reading UTC ranges retain double-precision fractional bar spacing and indices at plot edges, avoiding magnified UTC rounding errors inside session gaps after long histories; the synchronization reference table covers one-day ranges inside three-day and 65.5-hour gaps at multiple widths. Scroll offsets retain double precision even far into history. Migration: `ChartMapping::right_offset`, `IndicatorCoordParams::right_offset` and `ZoomState::right_offset` are now `f64`; convert stored `f32` values with `.into()` in struct literals. Existing constructors accept both types, and `TimeScale::right_offset_precise` returns the retained offset alongside the legacy `f32` getter. ([#916](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/916))
- Main and comparison series use the same ordinary-price fallback when their percentage or indexed reference is zero or unavailable, keeping drawable comparison coordinates finite. ([#913](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/913))
- Logarithmic autoscaling filters drawable positive OHLC prices before reducing their extrema, including opens and closes of bars whose lows cross zero, on the main and added series. ([#914](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/914))
- Each chart and added series retains its own Heikin Ashi history, so nine or more active histories reuse their transforms on unchanged frames and release them with their owners. Price fitting and painting share those transformed bars, including recursive opens carried across price gaps. Migration for low-level renderer callers: add `heikin_ashi: &cache` to `CandleDataContext` literals, retaining one `chart::rendering::candles::HeikinAshiCache::default()` per series. The widget manages its caches automatically. ([#912](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/912), [#915](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/915))
- Automatic price tick labels derive precision from their spacing and use scientific notation beyond eight decimals, so sub-nano prices remain nonzero and distinct on normal and logarithmic axes. Explicit instrument precision is preserved. ([#917](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/917))
- A zero right margin keeps the newest candle and its streaming updates in the visible data and price fit, including when the right edge is fixed. Logical ranges include their rightmost bar slot when converted to end-exclusive slices. ([#910](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/910))

- Chart panning includes the first movement after a pointer press, so a drag completed in one movement frame moves by its full distance. Headless widget tests check the complete pan, pointer-anchored wheel zoom and finite, bounded frames for empty, single-bar and flat histories. ([#245](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/245))
- The indicator list and configuration panel share the dialog's available width, keeping the Add button visible in narrow windows when selecting a metric or another study. ([#547](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/547))

- Scalar rolling sums recover small values after a much larger value leaves the window. ([#545](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/545))

- Force Index waits for a complete seed of price changes, including when the whole history is shorter than its period. It shares the SMA-seeded scalar EMA with the other studies. Envelopes, Guppy averages and the percentage oscillators also use the public scalar helpers while retaining their reference values. ([#545](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/545))

- Starting or rewinding replay in the demo resets the paper account and reconnects its trading view; forward playback keeps the account. `PaperAccount::set_replay_time` timestamps trading history at each traversed source bar, including skipped bars; `None` restores the live clock on exit. Resetting the account retains its host-supplied prices and replay clock. Execution marks stay on revealed candles, including partial candles built from finer updates, and hide fills before history or at and beyond the exclusive cutoff. Replay holds no account of its own. ([#215](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/215))
- Waiting for a higher study interval to close treats the latest chart bar as forming. Its scheduled end no longer confirms a source bar early; historical chart bars retain their close-time alignment. ([#175](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/175))
- VWAP uses the chart interval to distinguish intraday exchange sessions from UTC-labelled daily bars, retaining timestamp inference only without an interval. Changing the interval recalculates existing studies. The inferred Daily flag is hidden from inputs; saved legacy values remain accepted and are ignored. Hosts should supply the interval through `Indicator::set_timeframe` or `Chart::set_timeframe`. ([#672](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/672))

- Custom toolbar buttons, drawing controls, tabs, menus, object rows and symbol lists report their accessible names, roles and selected, checked, expanded and disabled states. Dialogs expose their titles, and form rows, drawing and series settings, study inputs and alert fields associate their labels with their controls. The chart exposes a text summary of its symbol, interval, displayed history and latest or hovered OHLCV and study values, including gaps and empty history. Native examples with `ui` and the demo enable the screen-reader bridge; widget-only examples opt in with `--features widget,eframe/accesskit`. Applications using `eframe` enable its `accesskit` feature. ([#250](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/250), [#280](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/280))
- Object tree rows use themed chevrons, drawing glyphs, settings and trash icons in place of letter and punctuation placeholders. ([#279](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/279))
- The right bar's screener, hotlists, calendars, news, chats, alerts and object tree use flat dropdowns with thin chevrons and hover-filled text actions. Long dropdown menus scroll to keep every choice reachable in short windows. News and highlights keep the market scope reachable beside long symbols in narrow panels. Screener filters use the shared input style; market rows share the watchlist's height and hover fill, with right-aligned tabular values. Calendars keep event metadata on one line and actual, forecast and prior in fixed columns, including missing figures. The screener, hotlists and news entries use fresh radar, mountain and feed glyphs. The demo's Time and sales and Session notes entries appear only with Toggle API samples; hiding them closes an open sample panel and preserves session notes. ([#863](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/863))

- Marker text sits on a background in the chart's colour, so it reads in full over the candles, bars and lines behind it. ([#859](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/859))
- A toast's message wraps short of its close button, so the end of a long message, such as a broker's reason for rejecting an order, is no longer drawn under the button.
- A chart with a feed or server time repaints once a second only while its bar countdown is drawn, so a hidden price axis or a last price outside the visible range no longer keeps an idle chart repainting. ([#852](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/852))
- The Baseline chart fills both sides of its base without faint lines between neighbouring parts of the path, which a curved line made obvious. ([#832](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/832))
- The top toolbar's layout button shows the cells of the current preset, one cell for a single chart, the matching grid otherwise and a tab strip for tabs, instead of a four-cell grid whatever the layout; each preset's glyph is an `icons::LAYOUT_*` constant. Quick Search has its own magnifier with a lightning bolt (`icons::COMMAND_SEARCH`), so it no longer repeats the symbol search glyph, and Settings uses a hex nut (`icons::SETTINGS_NUT`). ([#844](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/844))
- The series legend's values follow the title after one regular gap, and its flag and series actions appear after the values only while the pointer is over the row. The plot's chart layout and overlay study menus appear only while the pointer is over the chart or a menu is open, and no longer draw a button frame at the edge of the plot. ([#817](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/817))
- The top toolbar labels Alert and Replay beside their icons, as it does Indicators, and places the chart layout selector at the right, directly before the layout name and Save. The demo lists its host actions in the main menu, so its default strip shows the built-in controls only. ([#817](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/817))
- Watchlist text rejects ambiguous symbols, row drags stay within their unsorted list, and long panel titles and values leave controls and neighbouring columns readable. Size filters scroll before they hide the trade tape. The demo keeps active-symbol quotes on the chart session and price basis, clears the tape when changing chart slots or layouts, and exports watchlists to a fixed filename. ([#223](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/223), [#237](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/237))
- Continuous exchange sessions close their final intraday bucket at local midnight, so custom intervals retain their short final bar when resampled. ([#97](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/97))

- Higher-interval candles retain their full body and wick range and can be selected by that geometry after moving to a separate pane. ([#546](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/546))

- The main menu button of the top toolbar stays at the left edge while the narrow strip scrolls; only the controls after it move. ([#810](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/810))

- Icons draw their strokes one logical pixel wide at every icon size, two physical pixels at 2x, with horizontal and vertical segments on the pixel grid, so toolbar, rail and menu icons match the weight of separators and text instead of blurring across two or three pixels. Filled shapes keep their look. The chart installs the icon loader beside egui_extras' loaders; an app that shows icons without a chart keeps rasterising them with egui_extras' SVG loader as before. ([#816](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/816))

- The top toolbar keeps every control in its original group and order at narrow widths, scrolling horizontally with touch drags and horizontal wheel or trackpad input. The scroll bar and overflow dropdown are removed. A drag beginning on a button scrolls without activating it; taps and keyboard focus keep working, and focused controls scroll into view. ([#806](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/806))

- The web demo follows the dynamic viewport with a legacy height fallback, hides its right rail below 600 points with panels reachable from the main menu, and shows layout tabs only when there is more than one layout. Narrow legends show close and change instead of four OHLC fields, reserve space for pane actions, and keep title controls clear of values during hover. Study controls appear on pointer hover, keyboard focus or a touch row selection and dismiss on an outside tap. ([#806](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/806))
- Replay reapplies its cutoff on interval changes, includes newly added layout slots while paused, and restores checkpoints from their saved source interval. ([#213](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/213))

- Starting a feed reload no longer reports `DataLoaded` before history arrives. Successful pages and explicit data replacements still notify the host. ([#201](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/201))

- The basic chart example reserves its click readout above the plot and requests repaint after a host click event, keeping the feedback visible. ([#201](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/201))

- Switching between a local chart theme and the host theme retains the chart's widget ids, keyboard focus and retained UI state. ([#202](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/202))

- Host pointer events use every visible magnet target in a merged study pane and retain the volume overlay mapping when a secondary click opens a menu. Volume magnet events report the bar volume rather than the raw pointer value. ([#201](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/201))

- Command palette labels and descriptions stay clear of categories and shortcuts in narrow windows. Long text is shortened to fit its column. ([#308](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/308))
- Shared comparison scales retain manual bounds when members are hidden, removed or reassigned. Comparison lines remain visible beyond the main history, relative axes retain their units in whitespace, and hidden study axes no longer consume comparison gutters. Removing the main series keeps its feed stopped when playback resumes. Copied layouts remap drawing pane ownership to the copied studies. ([#87](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/87))

- Applying drawing templates preserves the target study pane, and saved defaults omit pane ownership. Standalone drawing managers release cached geometry after drawings are deleted. Chart layouts keep study-owned drawings local rather than copying their chart-local study ids into another chart. ([#151](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/151))

- The study refresh regression checks one full recompute per frame without assuming measured update costs preserve registration order. Unequal refresh deadlines exercise that order independently of timer precision. ([#747](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/747))

- Drawing timestamps survive history replacement, interval changes and streaming inserts, including fractional indices and whitespace outside loaded bars. Undo/redo snapshots and linked charts use the same displayed-bar mapping. Drawings and markers are retained per symbol; switching symbols ends active drags and preserves drawing controls; markers between bars interpolate, with the nearest bar supplying their height. Migration: construct `ChartPoint` with `new` instead of struct literals; its editable `bar_idx` and `price` fields remain public. Handle `SaveError::UnmappedAnchor` in exhaustive matches. Switching symbols or replacing drawings through `load_drawings` clears chart undo history. ([#81](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/81))

- Recoverable resolve and history failures retry the same request after 1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32 and then at most every 60 seconds, respecting a longer `RateLimited.retry_after`. Permanent failures remain exposed in `FeedState::last_error` and stop paging until reload. `Chart::reload_feed` explicitly reloads without changing the symbol; `Sink::symbols_changed` advances `FeedState::symbol_catalog_revision` for host search refreshes. Hosts still provide the feed and symbol catalog. ([#71](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/71))

- Configured right-axis price modes reach `Chart::with_config`, `update_config` and changes through `config_mut`. A mode change resets manual scale ranges and zoom history; unchanged settings preserve them. Undo restores manual ranges after a mode edit through `config_mut`. ([#86](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/86))

- CI builds the separate native demo on Linux, macOS and Windows, tests its controller and starts its Linux window under X11 with a software Vulkan backend. Startup must leave a visible window and survive the first rendered frames. ([#282](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/282))

- The demo restores its selected light or dark theme before building the chart and saves the selection through application storage. The existing full theme switch continues to update active and saved chart layouts. ([#283](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/283))

- Series settings Reset to defaults uses the active theme's candle colours, clears custom borders and wicks and retains the selected chart type. Opening and accepting settings continues to preserve the chart's current colours. ([#578](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/578))

- Pane legends identify each visible output with its plot colour. Values below 4.5:1 contrast use a coloured swatch and normal text, retaining the study name and legend controls. ([#580](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/580))
- RSI defaults to a two-pixel blue line, a 20% fill between its two grey 4/4 dashed guides and a 138 px pane when space permits. Other pane presets retain their zone fills. Migration: `IndicatorPaneConfig` literals add `fill_between_zones` or use `..Default::default()`. ([#726](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/726))
- The top toolbar uses a 38 px strip followed by a 4 px gap, measured menu, settings and screenshot glyphs and a leading symbol-search icon. Its dark strip and separators use #0f0f0f and #4a4a4a. Save, quick search, settings, fullscreen and screenshot form the right group; alerts, replay and layouts remain reachable through overflow. Overflow preserves disabled Undo and Redo and the selected Replay state, including when playback stops outside the menu. The demo feed toggle moves into its menu and unsupported Publish and Trade buttons are hidden. ([#287](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/287))
- Save and the layout menu create independent session layout copies; export remains an explicit command. Drawing templates and account-dependent orders and positions controls are hidden until the host supplies them. Persistent layouts and trading accounts remain host-owned. ([#118](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/118))

- The widget bar centres Help on the adjacent 38 px bottom row in every preset, keeping the section rule above it. ([#583](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/583))

- Opening a chart, series or drawing context menu suppresses the crosshair, axis tags and data tooltip on the opening frame as well as while the menu stays open. ([#291](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/291))

- Removed the unused `ContextMenuSizingTokens::char_width`: context menus measure their laid-out labels and shortcuts, and elide labels to keep the shortcut column clear when a narrow window reduces their width. Migration: remove `char_width` from token struct literals. ([#457](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/457))

- Replay transport keys reach the visible replay bar before chart navigation, while dialogs retain keyboard priority. Hosts can call `FloatingReplayControl::shortcut` before dispatching chart input. The demo checks quick search through full application frames, including text arriving with its opening command. ([#113](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/113), [#523](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/523))

- Shortcut dispatch preserves text queued after an opening command, and quick search focuses its input before processing that text. Drawing toolbars defer to the chart's bindings even when keys are disabled or removed. Binding precedence also applies while a field has focus. Shortcut help wraps within narrow windows, and interval entry uses the shared dialog footer. ([#113](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/113), [#120](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/120))

- A bound character key also consumes its text event, so a restored F binding fits the chart without opening symbol search. Typing `?` in a text field edits the field; command search and F1 help remain available there. Opening a host dialog hands subsequent input to it, and typed symbol and interval entry focus their fields before processing queued editing keys. ([#113](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/113))

- Drawing tool shortcuts use the shared exact-modifier matcher, including when the toolbar is used without a chart. An extra Shift no longer selects a tool bound only with Alt. ([#113](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/113))

- Disabling chart keyboard shortcuts retains native arrow-key focus navigation and does not emit chart-focus commands for Tab. Enabled charts continue to keep repeated arrow pans on the chart. ([#113](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/113))

- Keyboard defaults now pan one bar, use Ctrl/Cmd+Left/Right for a page and Ctrl/Cmd+Up/Down for zoom, dispatch drawing undo/redo, quick search, study search, drawing tools and scale toggles, and route date, snapshot, clipboard and chart focus commands to the host. Typing opens symbol search or interval entry instead of selecting fixed intervals; 15 enters fifteen minutes. Help shows the supplied map's bound actions. Migration: exhaustive `KeyboardAction` matches need the new command and `DrawingTool` arms, and labels/descriptions borrow the action. Reset is Alt+R, date entry Alt+G, snapshot Alt+S, inversion Alt+I, logarithmic Alt+L, percentage Alt+P and help Ctrl/Cmd+/. Unmodified F now starts symbol entry; bind `FitContent` explicitly to retain the former F shortcut. Drawing rectangle, text and pitchfork shortcuts no longer overlap these commands; assign custom bindings through the map. ([#113](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/113))

- The demo drawing toolbar zoom controls arm box zoom and restore its previous view through `Chart::zoom_back`, without consuming drawing undo or redo or editing drawing geometry. ([#118](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/118))

- Drawing bulk removal, hide/show and lock-all are single undo steps. Ordering changes and new cloned drawings also enter the existing drawing history, and bulk undo restores every drawing and its list order while queuing host synchronization for the affected records. Property edits were already recorded. Migration: exhaustive `DrawingCommand` matches need the `Snapshot` arm. ([#108](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/108))

- Quick Search finds and executes drawing tools, built-in studies, chart settings and chart actions. The demo builds its entries from the existing tool and study catalogues; Ctrl/Cmd+K, the search button and menu open the same palette. Migration: handle `TopToolbarAction::OpenQuickSearch` by opening the command palette; symbol and compare entries still emit their own requests. ([#120](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/120))

- The demo gives dialogs keyboard input before the chart and replay controls. Escape closes the shortcuts help without changing the drawing selection, armed tool or replay; an open popup keeps the key even when opened during the same frame. ([#523](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/523))

- Drawing Delete, Backspace, Escape and completion keys respect keyboard focus and open popups. Editing a text field leaves the selected drawing and armed tool intact; chart Delete still removes the selection and chart Escape cancels it. ([#640](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/640))

- Chart and series settings use the same modal, sidebar and action chrome, with object-specific titles, measured label columns, 8 px swatch gaps and readable section headings. Series colours use the shared picker; unavailable visibility and overlay controls are omitted. Shared dialog titles are 14 px, modal surfaces dim the app, and frame headers and footers handle Escape and primary Enter without taking keys from focused controls or popups. ([#294](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/294), [#278](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/278))
- `ChartSettingsState::from_chart` and `apply_to_chart` read and apply the dialog's display controls in the library, including background gradients, crosshair styling, precision, watermark, navigation, status, scale and margin settings. Invalid timezone, margins or crosshair width leave the chart unchanged. The demo uses this adapter and reads back current values; trading and events remain host-managed; alert visibility, labels and colour control the demo overlay and survive reopening settings. Main-series line settings now apply and read back. ([#114](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/114))
- Vertical grid lines retain their own colour through `ChartConfig::vertical_grid_color`; `None` follows `grid_color`. `show_grid` now gates both grid directions. Migration: `ChartConfig` literals add `vertical_grid_color: None` or use `..Default::default()`; set `show_grid = true` before selecting individual horizontal and vertical visibility flags.

- Symbol search opens near the top third with 32 px result rows, 13/11 px symbol and description text, trailing exchange/type columns and spaced keyboard hints. Comparison searches the same host catalogue. The demo adds timestamp-aligned close-price overlays from independent synthetic feeds, removes and hides them, and keeps their membership with each chart and saved layout. Add, remove and visibility actions leave the public `compared_symbols` list untouched until the host accepts them. Migration: update `compared_symbols` after handling an accepted intent. ([#296](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/296), [#117](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/117))

- Vertical-only grid settings edit the visible grid colour. Fractional margins retain their text when settings reopen, and an edited right margin survives live updates. Settings controls inherit the shared checkbox, combo and sidebar styling; series candle colours are paired. Remove the ineffective panel-layout control and unused picker state. Migration: use `SettingsDialog::open` instead of the removed `open_with_layout`, `set_layout_style` and `layout_style` methods. ([#114](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/114), [#293](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/293), [#294](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/294))

- Series settings remove the placeholder Alerts, Trading and Events tabs. General settings remove inactive Trading and Events controls; the Alerts controls remain because the demo now applies their visibility, labels and colour to its alert overlay. Migration: remove `SeriesSettingsTab::{Alerts,Trading,Events}` and `SettingsTab::{Trading,Events}` from exhaustive matches. Host-owned trading and event settings data remain available for hosts that implement those surfaces. ([#212](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/212))

- Active price alerts, including channel boundaries and compound price conditions, have labelled draggable levels through `chart::render_alert_levels`; a drag returns a replacement for host submission. The demo applies alert visibility, labels and colour, opens the shared dialog at the latest price and saves hovered-price alerts with Alt+A when the chart owns the hovered point. The existing crosshair add menu remains available. The demo host evaluates its displayed synthetic feed, including consecutive crossings and changed drawing or study outputs while prices are idle, and reports triggers and expiry through `Alerts`, so the existing popup and log show them. Evaluation, sound, background watchlists and durable storage remain host-owned. ([#210](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/210), [#211](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/211))
- `PnlLabel::currency` uses a host-supplied `CurrencyFormatter`, retaining sign placement and zero rounding with the instrument currency and decimals. Migration: pass the formatter as the second argument, for example `PnlLabel::currency(value, &CurrencyFormatter::eur())`. ([#209](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/209))
- Crosshairs honour independent line visibility, colours, widths and label visibility, align x with the selected bar, and snap to visible OHLC and study values in magnet mode. The demo magnet menu sets the crosshair mode too. Hovering price, volume or any study pane shares one bar index across all legends and readouts; horizontal cursors use the hovered pane's scale and precision. Empty-space and synchronized cursors extend through the study panes. Crosshair tags stay inside their axis, including pane edges, with 4 px text padding; clicking the price tag retains the add-at-price intent without a permanent glyph. Pane guides use the theme grid colour, and study legends highlight on hover with visibility, settings and remove controls. Hidden overlay rows retain their show control. Long pane captions leave room for their controls, and floating study readouts fit narrow plots away from the hovered bar. Migration: `Legend::show_with_study_actions` returns `StudyAction` instead of a settings index; `PaneInteraction` literals include `visibility_response` and `settings_response`. ([#107](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/107), [#90](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/90), [#286](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/286), [#288](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/288))
- `Button::text_color` changes a label without tinting its fill or icon. Floating replay uses the shared speed combo, a danger-coloured Exit label on the secondary button and a 28 px hide button. The 32 px bar stays inside an 8 px dock inset and scrolls horizontally when the window is too narrow for its controls. ([#302](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/302))

- The bottom-bar clock requests a repaint at the next whole second while visible, so it keeps advancing without pointer or feed activity. Hidden clocks schedule no wake-up. ([#536](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/536))

- Bottom-bar session and dividend controls show their selected state and describe the current mode. The calendar stays selected while the date picker is open, and range tips use singular interval units in both layouts. Narrow strips shorten the clock to its time, retain the zone in its tooltip and compact the range trigger so session and dividend controls stay visible. ([#303](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/303))

- `DrawingToolbarDomainState` snapshots and restores magnet strength, cursor type and eraser state alongside the active drawing tool. The demo synchronises from the chart before every toolbar frame, clearing finished tools and reflecting externally changed modes without retaining a conflicting zoom highlight. Cursor, eraser and drawing picks leave the previous mode immediately. Migration: snapshot literals supply `magnet_type`, `current_cursor_type` and `eraser_mode_enabled`; hosts call `sync_from_domain_state` before showing the toolbar. ([#519](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/519))

- The magnet flyout gives Off, Weak, Strong and OHLC modes action icons alongside their existing current-mode checks. ([#290](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/290))

- The floating selection toolbar uses shared icon buttons with accessible action names and the kit’s hover, pressed and focus states. Its frame follows popup fill, border and shadow, and group separators use the full theme stroke. ([#468](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/468), [#587](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/587))

- Synchronized ranges interpolate repeated element timestamps without jumping forward; nearest crosshairs select the first element at a repeated time. ([#116](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/116))
- Layout synchronization retains hidden slots, clears stale tab hovers, retries drawings after receiver history loads and repaints mirrored edits. Drawing Delete shortcuts affect only the focused chart. Layout and group inputs stay open while editing, including toolbar overflow, and overlapping popups do not activate the chart underneath. New and copied layouts keep one linked copy of each shared drawing and retain main-series visibility. Layout theme changes use `Chart::set_theme` for active and saved charts. The demo preserves custom chart colours when changing slots and applies both endpoints of custom date ranges. ([#115](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/115))

- `Chart::hover_time`, `set_synced_crosshair_time`, `visible_time_range` and `apply_synced_time_range` exchange UTC timestamps instead of local bar indices. Receivers resolve their own bars, sessions, intervals and plot widths; missing crosshair times clear the cursor, and fractional range edges interpolate across gaps. Existing index and spacing methods remain available for local view restoration. ([#116](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/116))
- Series settings reserve room for their footer and scroll within narrow and short windows. Price-based types omit unsupported border and wick colour controls, and the inactive Template menu is removed. ([#562](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/562), [#633](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/633))

- Baseline dashes keep their spacing across short segments and base crossings. ([#562](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/562))
- Renko wicks retain unfinished price moves beyond the last completed brick when the next brick reverses direction. ([#633](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/633))

- Resetting series settings keeps the active chart type in the dialog, so its style controls remain visible.

- Dashed and dotted series retain their pattern when zooming out makes individual segments shorter than one point.

- The Area chart uses a blue 2 pt line and a vertical fill fading from 45% opacity at the highest visible point to transparent at the plot bottom, behind the volume overlay. Panning and zooming move the gradient with the visible range. ([#735](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/735))

- The shared plot insets from #759 now grow the axis column for wide prices, quotes and study tags, retaining an 8 px outer clearance. Pane plots keep the same left and right edges, with no unused left-axis gutter. Width comes from the rendered tags and ticks, including custom price lines, currency headers and panes with hidden value tags, and updates in the same frame. ([#579](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/579))

- Price ticks, tags, crosshairs, legends and tooltip readouts derive precision from explicit instrument decimals or its minimum tick, including integer ticks, five-decimal quotes and prices quoted beyond sixteen decimal places. Sub-unit ranges retain their coordinates and axis marks. `instrument_price_precision` exposes the same rule to host readouts. `Instrument::with_volume_decimals` applies volume precision to legends, quote tags and tooltips; `DataWindowInfo::with_instrument` applies both precisions and zero is a price. Prices and absolute change share `DataWindowInfo::price_decimals`. Cloned custom formatters retain their closure. Migration: `Instrument` literals need `volume_decimals`, or `..Instrument::new(symbol)`; `DataWindowInfo` literals need `price_decimals` and `volume_precision`, or `..Default::default()`. ([#78](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/78))

- Indicator pane ticks use the price axis text colour in every preset, retaining the shared axis font. Their shared tick generator avoids repeated labels at integer precision. ([#584](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/584))
- The demo alerts panel shares the panel header and New alert icon button, with a centred empty state and Create alert action. Demo notices use the shared opaque, padded toast component; screenshot completion keeps its saved-path feedback. ([#304](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/304))

- Object tree headers use shared icon buttons, the search field and type selector share a 28 px row, and the 11 px footer stays at the panel bottom. The Data Window separates Time and Interval; instrument precision and migration are covered in #78. The demo passes instrument metadata alongside its existing chart-formatted time and previous close. ([#301](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/301))

- Object tree rows open native context menus with menu rows, sentence case labels and ellipses. Menus stay within the window and close on Escape or any outside click. Migration: `ObjectTreeState` no longer exposes `context_menu_open`, `context_menu_pos`, `context_menu_target`, `open_context_menu` or `close_context_menu`; egui owns popup state. ([#530](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/530))

- Object tree selections use the active theme's selection fill and text; hover state clears when the pointer leaves a row. Long names end with an ellipsis 4 px before the row buttons, with the full name in the row tooltip. ([#529](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/529), [#532](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/532))

- Object tree shortcuts leave focused widgets and open popups alone, and consume handled keys so a chart cannot also act on them. ([#527](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/527))

- The grouped object tree shows data sources above its groups and honours the Data Source filter, so the main series agrees with the flat view and object count. ([#526](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/526))

- The demo keeps the right rail selection in step with the open panel, including panels opened from menus and the shortcuts dialog closing. `WidgetBar::active_icon` documents the host synchronisation contract. ([#528](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/528))

- `Chart::set_theme(ctx, theme)` updates chart colours and the context theme while retaining data and viewport. Crosshair labels and their add controls use the active theme's colour pair, rectangle measurements use its axis colour pair, connection and alert statuses follow its semantic colours, and TPO letters retain at least 4.5:1 contrast on both the plot and value-area shading. The settings dialog resolves default background swatches from the theme instead of a fixed dark fill. Explicit crosshair and background colours remain overrides; alert statuses read the active theme directly. Default settings swatches follow runtime theme changes without changing saved values on Cancel. Migration: standalone `crosshair_price_action` calls pass the resolved label text colour as the final argument. ([#275](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/275))

- Custom controls draw keyboard focus rings, including object-tree actions, floating controls, layout menus and the Save arrow. Contiguous list rows keep their ring inside the row so neighbouring fills cannot cover it. Toolbars, custom intervals and dialog footers follow visual order under Tab; search and template fields release focus, and Enter activates the focused control. ([#277](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/277))

- The colour picker measures its popup bounds after layout, so clicking Hex reaches the custom editor. Custom editing starts from the current colour, Escape is consumed, and closing restores the swatch view. Only selection and hover draw a 2 px ring; swatches close to the popup fill get an inside hairline. Wells, swatches and the custom row show keyboard focus. Migration: `ColorPicker::show` returns `egui::InnerResponse<Option<Color32>>`; read `.inner` for a picked colour and `.response` for the well. The unused `color_picker_btn` helper is removed; use `ColorPicker` with persistent `ColorPickerState`. ([#576](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/576), [#650](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/650), part of [#277](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/277))

- Autocomplete keeps its popup alive while open and moves selection to the hovered suggestion before drawing any row. Only that row is highlighted, inset with rounded corners, and Enter confirms it. ([#592](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/592))

- Auto-height dialogs and modals offer scrolling bodies the current window cap each frame, so they grow with content, shrink again and retain their on-screen header when resized. Dialogs also retain their horizontal margins in narrow windows. The shortcuts dialog uses that cap without its own height workaround. ([#641](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/641), [#652](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/652))

- Egui controls and kit buttons share the 4 px small radius. Focus rings follow each control's corners and end 2 px outside it; the text cursor uses the theme text colour. Split buttons, sections and `UiExt` combos use the small chevron icons. Migration: `styles::focus::draw_focus_ring` takes the control's radius as its third argument. ([#586](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/586), [#593](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/593), [#594](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/594), [#595](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/595))

- Command palette highlights are inset from the row edges and use the kit small radius for selected and hovered rows. ([#591](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/591))

- Subdued list text and property labels use the weak text colour while values retain the row text colour. Dark weak text reads at 4.5:1 or better on panels and floating windows. ([#589](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/589))

- Action list rows are transparent at rest, including disabled rows, with disabled text and icons in the theme colour. Primary and danger rows use the shared opaque hover and pressed shades. ([#596](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/596))

- `FormRow` starts its controls after the label column with the same horizontal gap and row pitch as `FormGrid`. ([#590](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/590))

- Kit filter fields clear on Escape only while focused and consume that key, including immediately after focus moves elsewhere. Autocomplete handles confirmation only from its own field and reports a closed popup after a pick. The clear control uses the kit icon button with hover, pressed and keyboard states. ([#588](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/588), [#592](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/592))

- Selected indicator rows take both their fill and text from `IndicatorDialogConfig`, including when the configuration was frozen from another theme. The default follows the active selection pair. Migration: add `sel_text_color` to configuration literals or use `..Default::default()`. ([#437](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/437))

- Modal dialogs, modal handlers and the command palette read `UiColors::modal_overlay` from the active theme. Light dialogs dim white panels to 159 rather than 75; high contrast uses its window outline without dimming. Migration: add `modal_overlay` to colour literals and saved palettes, or construct colours from a preset. ([#585](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/585))

- Active widget rail entries use the selection colour pair and show the pressed step when held. Alert counts use `danger_bg` with `danger_text` instead of mixing a status foreground with the badge text. ([#387](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/387), [#531](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/531))

- List action buttons and `UiExt` primary, secondary and danger helpers share the kit variants, including opaque themed state shades and their matching text. The dark primary blue now meets 4.5:1 with white. Ghost and Outlined controls use egui's hover and pressed fills, and neutral button glyphs use the same foreground states across button types. ([#435](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/435), [#582](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/582))

- Deserialising incomplete theme colour palettes reports a missing-field error instead of silently filling a light theme with dark colours. Text, spacing and radius scales still default independently. Migration: save the complete `Theme` value; hand-written theme files must include `dark`, `ui` and `chart` with all colour fields. ([#434](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/434))

- Links and clickable labels derive a readable colour from the accent on panels and windows. `Theme::with_accent` gives black, white and near-endpoint accents distinct opaque hover and pressed shades, a visible selection and a matching light focus ring while retaining text contrast. ([#432](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/432), [#433](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/433))

- egui buttons, headers and open combo boxes use the theme's fills and strokes in every state. Pressed neutral fills differ from hover by at least 1.2:1. Settings controls use control outlines, active settings icons and timeframe labels use readable text, and secondary surfaces retain readable muted and status colours. ([#431](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/431), [#276](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/276), [#436](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/436), [#581](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/581))
- The drawing properties dialog in the demo opens with the drawing's saved style and interval settings, and applies fill, font size and interval visibility through the existing undo and host-update path. The lock-all button derives its state from the current drawings, including locks changed through context menus. Locked drawings reject property and colour edits until unlocked, as well as movement, handle edits, erasing and deletion. ([#138](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/138), [#140](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/140), [#141](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/141))
- Drawing renderers apply common dash styles, non-default widths and configured fills to their paint primitives, while preserving custom fork levels and position profit/loss colours. Annotation text, tables and icons use the configured font size before sizing their surrounding geometry; the fixed note marker keeps its icon size. Bold adds synthetic weight using the host's existing font family and fallback glyphs. Line endpoints render all stored marker styles in anchor order, including reversed lines and path tangents. Info lines, angle lines and arrows also honour left/right extensions. Migration: new non-arrow tools default to no endpoint marker; saved endpoint settings now render as stored. Icons use their saved font size instead of a size derived from stroke width. ([#141](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/141))
- Drawing selection also follows the clipped shapes emitted by the renderer, including text extents, position zones, data-driven tools, rings and cycle lines. The manager retains the last paint alongside the existing geometric hit tests; standalone interaction hosts can pass paint commands to `DrawingInteraction::hit_test_painted`. Moved anchors and edited properties discard stale painted hits. Geometric hits also respect the last paint clip. ([#137](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/137))
- Strong drawing magnet snaps to the nearest open, high, low or close of the bar under the pointer even outside the weak magnet's pixel threshold. Weak magnet retains nearby bar, overlay and drawing targets. Both apply during placement and handle drags, and the drawing toolbar passes the selected strength to the chart. Migration: `DrawingManagerOptions` literals need `strong_magnet`, or `..Default::default()`; set it with `magnet_mode` for strong snapping. ([#139](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/139))
- Drawing bodies, previews, selected handles and eraser highlights are clipped to the price pane and the caller's clip rectangle. Patterns projected past the pane edge no longer paint across the time axis or other panes. The chart's separate axis labels keep their own painters. ([#653](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/653))
- Selection handles follow the drawing's visibility on the current interval, so a selected drawing hidden by its interval settings leaves no visible or hittable handles behind. Chart interval synchronization, drawing rendering and hit filtering were already applied. ([#140](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/140))
- Selected drawings move from a body grab while retaining the pointer offset. Every anchor moves by the same bar and price delta, including on logarithmic scales, with one undo step and one host update per gesture. Rectangles also have a centre handle, and body grabs suppress chart panning. ([#741](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/741))

- The live demo varies execution arrival intensity in clustered bursts and quieter stretches over a daily activity profile, so minute volume varies several-fold. Price steps, tick sizes and trade-size generation retain their seeded sequence; history and streaming use the same timing. ([#782](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/782))

- The demo opens at its configured bar spacing even when the first canvas is narrow. Charts defer initial visible-bar and resize calculations while the plot is collapsed, preserving zoom and the right edge when a hidden or tiny canvas becomes visible. Pixel right offsets use the first usable bar spacing, locked resizes retain fractional visible ranges, and explicit visible-bar requests take precedence over a simultaneous resize. ([#781](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/781))
- Study selection follows histogram and circle geometry in overlays and panes. The applied-study editor keeps its controls and footer inside narrow and short windows in both themes. ([#169](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/169))

- Study output uses `IndicatorValues`, one contiguous `Vec<f64>` per output line, with NaN for missing values. Rendering, hit testing, snapping and autoscaling read the columns directly. Migration: `Indicator::values` implementations return `&IndicatorValues`; read `line(index)`, `columns()` or `value(line, bar)` instead of indexing rows. `to_rows()` explicitly allocates the former owned row format for exports. Custom calculation closures still accept owned rows and their stored results are converted to columns. Numeric definitions and streaming update results are unchanged. ([#180](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/180))

- KST and RVI use running means of complete windows, STC shares the linear rolling extrema, and RankCorr reuses its sorting buffers and computes Pearson correlation of average ranks for ties. Migration: KST first appears at `max(roc_period + sma_period - 1)`, with its signal `signal_period - 1` bars later; RVI starts at bar `period + 2`, its signal at `period + 5`. These indices are zero-based; at default lengths KST starts at 44 and its signal at 52, RVI at 12 and its signal at 15. Incomplete windows no longer produce averages. Callers must handle `IndicatorValue::None` before the main line and `NaN` for a pending signal, and load enough history before consuming either line. RankCorr values on tied prices follow the rank correlation definition; flat windows have no value. ([#179](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/179))
- KAMA keeps a running sum of its last `period` absolute price changes and resumes last-bar updates and appends through the shared study driver. Full calculations are linear in history length and no longer allocate a price window for every bar. Its SMA seed, efficiency ratio, smoothing and handling of non-finite closes are unchanged. ([#179](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/179))

- Stochastic RSI returns its first value as soon as RSI, stochastic, %K and %D have warmed up, including histories of 32 and 33 bars at the default lengths. ([#714](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/714))

- DEMA and TEMA seed each inner exponential average with its own first full window, so constant input stays constant and the warm-up remains `2 * period - 2` and `3 * period - 3` bars. ([#713](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/713))
- Measurement boxes stay inside the pane when their anchors approach an edge. Gann and trend-time labels omit collisions in small drawings, and harmonic ratio labels move along their comparison chords to avoid anchors.
- Parallel channel extensions follow chart left and right even when the anchors were placed right to left. Rays retain their direction when panned beyond the pane, and leftward Fibonacci time zones keep their fills. Drawing rail menus use the same available entries as drawing categories.

- Pitchforks without custom level settings retain the drawing colour and the existing tine width. Fixed Gann grids without custom divisions retain their quarter grid.

- Three-anchor projections add the first price and bar leg at the third anchor before mapping to screen coordinates, so logarithmic charts preserve the measured move. The target label shows the projected price.

- Gann grid percentages suppress floating-point tails, the fixed-ratio label sizes to its text, and pitchfan labels sit at the visible ray ends where they have room.

- Drawing placement collects the construction anchors: three for extensions, trend-based time, wedges, pitchfans, arcs, rotated rectangles, projections and channels; four for triangle patterns; seven for three drives; three and five for double and triple Elliott combinations.
