- A bar with a non-finite price or volume no longer blanks every later value of KAMA, On Balance Volume, ATR, Fisher Transform, Ehlers Fisher, Chandelier Exit and the Commodity Selection Index: that bar has no value and the study continues past it, as the new `Indicator` section on non-finite bars states.
- The floating replay bar's shortcuts (Space, Home, End and the arrow keys) no longer act while a text field has focus, a popup is open or a control outside the background layer has focus, and take their key when they act, so a space typed in the symbol search no longer toggles playback and Space on a focused button presses that button. Escape no longer hides the bar: a close button on the bar does, `FloatingReplayControl::show_control` shows it again, and the demo's Replay button shows a hidden bar instead of stopping replay. The bar is also drawn in the frame a shortcut acts in.
- The floating replay bar draws Go to start and Previous bar disabled on the first bar, and Next bar and Go to end disabled on the last. `FloatingReplayControl::sync_from` divides the zero-based bar index by the last index instead of the bar count, so the last bar reads 100% instead of 99.7% for 300 bars.
- A bar with a non-finite close no longer blanks every later value of EMA, DEMA, TEMA, SMMA, GMMA, McGinley Dynamic, VIDYA, ZLEMA, EMA Cross, MACD, VW-MACD, PPO, TRIX and Stochastic RSI (VW-MACD also needs a finite volume): that bar has no value and the study continues past it.
- A bar with a non-finite input no longer blanks every later value of Elder Ray, Force Index, Mass Index, TSI, SMI, STC, DMI, Keltner Channels, Chaikin Volatility, VWAP, Anchored VWAP, A/D, CVD, VPT, PVT Enhanced, PVI, Williams AD, the Accumulation Swing Index, Volume Oscillator and both volume profiles (the high, low, close, open or volume the study reads): that bar has no value and the study continues past it.
- A bar with a non-finite input no longer leaves finite but wrong values after it in RSI, Connors RSI, Stochastic, KDJ, Double Stochastic, Williams %R, Ultimate Oscillator, Pretty Good Oscillator, Relative Vigor Index, Know Sure Thing, Trend Intensity Index, Elder Impulse, MFI, VWMA, Ease of Movement, Historical, Close-to-Close, Garman-Klass and Parkinson Volatility, rank correlation, ADX, SuperTrend, Parabolic SAR, Aroon, Ichimoku, Donchian Channels, Price Channel, Pivot Points, 52 Week High/Low and Choppiness, True Range, Vortex, Squeeze Momentum, Majority Rule, Chaikin Money Flow and Klinger (the high, low, close, open or volume the study reads): that bar has no value and every later value equals the one for the series without it.
- "52W HL%" measures its range over the bars within 364 days of the current bar's timestamp on every interval instead of over 252 bars, so on a 1-minute chart it no longer covers 252 minutes, and has no value before 52 weeks of history. The new "52W High/Low" (`Week52HighLowLevels`) draws the highest high and lowest low of the same window as two lines on the price pane. Migration: replace `Week52HighLow::new(period)` with `Week52HighLow::new()`; intraday charts with less than a year of bars show no value. The factory category for both 52W studies changes from "Breadth and spread" to "Market structure".
- Net Volume signs each bar's volume by its close against the previous close (positive up, negative down, zero when equal) instead of against its own open, so a gap no longer inverts the sign, and the first bar has no value. Migration: the first value is blank and signs differ on bars that open away from the previous close.
- `ZeroTrendVol` computed the Parkinson high-low estimator; that calculation is now `ParkinsonVol` in `parkinson_vol.rs`, still registered as "Parkinson Vol" with unchanged values. "Zero-Trend Vol" (`ZeroTrendVol`) is the zero-mean close-to-close estimator: the square root of the mean squared log return, annualised by `sqrt(252) * 100`. Both assume daily bars (#672). Migration: Rust code that built `ZeroTrendVol` for the high-low estimator builds `ParkinsonVol`.
- Balance of Power with smoothing of length `n` has no value on its first `n - 1` bars, and none on any bar of a series shorter than `n`, instead of plotting the unsmoothed ratio there. A bar with a non-finite price has no value and the average is taken without it. Migration: the first 13 bars of the default Balance of Power are blank instead of raw ratios.
- PPO seeds its signal line with the mean of the first `signal` values of the PPO, as MACD defines it, instead of an EMA over zeros before the PPO exists, and returns the histogram (PPO minus signal) as a third line, so `line_cnt`, `line_names` and `colors` report three lines. The new `PercentageVolumeOscillator`, registered as "PVO" with lengths 12, 26 and 9, returns the same three lines computed from volume. Migration: PPO values from bar 33 (for 12, 26 and 9) are `Multiple([ppo, signal, histogram])` and the signal differs on every bar, since the zero start no longer pulls it down; `PriceOscillator::with_colors` and `set_colors` keep the colour of a line they are given none for.
- Zig Zag pivots sit on the swing extremes: when a reversal is confirmed, the new leg starts at the lowest low or highest high since the pivot it reverses from, not at the confirming bar, and the bars after that extreme are judged against the new leg, so the line touches every swing extreme. `depth` is now the minimum number of bars from a pivot to the bar that confirms the next reversal, and two pivots can be fewer than `depth` bars apart. Migration: values change on any series with a swing extreme fewer than `depth` bars before its confirming bar, which with the default depth of 10 is most series, so swing levels read from the previous values need checking, and a depth chosen to keep pivots apart no longer does. A bar with a non-finite price has no value and is not counted in `depth`, as if it were not in the series, instead of being drawn across.
- A replay's playback state follows seeks and steps: `ReplayState::jump_to_bar` and `step_forward` mark the replay finished whenever they land on the last bar and paused when they leave it, as `step_backward` did, and `reset`, `init` and `enter_replay_mode` apply the same rule, so a reset after the end is paused and a one-bar replay starts finished. Playing after seeking back from the end continues from the bar sought to instead of the first bar, and toggling play (`ReplayAction::TogglePlayPause`, the play button and Space in `ReplayControls`) on a last bar reached by a seek or a step restarts the replay instead of doing nothing; `ReplayAction::Play` still does nothing on the last bar.
- `ReplayController::load_data` during an active replay keeps the replay time: the position moves to the last bar that opens at or before it and a playing replay keeps playing, instead of restarting paused on the first bar. On a coarser interval the bar that contains the replay time is shown whole. The demo reloads its replay this way on an interval change, so the next step no longer brings back the old interval's bars. Migration: to restart a replay on new data, stop it first or send `ReplayAction::JumpToStart` after `load_data`; a series that ends before the replay time resumes finished on its last bar.
- An indicator alert needs an Indicator A and either an Indicator B or a threshold: the alert dialog keeps Create Alert disabled and names the missing field ("Choose Indicator A", "Choose Indicator B") until it has them, and `AlertsPanel::get_indicator_alert_form` returns `None` for a blank indicator, so the panel's Create stays disabled. Both used to create an alert with nothing to compare against.
- A drawing placed with clicks keeps exactly the anchors that were clicked: the pointer preview between clicks is no longer kept as an anchor, so a parallel channel, a pitchfork or a triangle needs three clicks rather than finishing on the second with its last anchor repeated, the pattern and Elliott tools take one click per anchor, and a trend line or a polyline no longer stores an extra point where the pointer last was.
- Releasing a drag that has left the price pane, over an axis or outside the chart, finishes the drawing with its last anchor on the pane's edge, and ends a handle drag, where the drawing stayed in progress until the next drag on the pane or Escape and the handle kept following the next drag.
- A locked drawing can no longer be moved or reshaped by its handles, or removed by the eraser, the Delete key, `DrawingManager::delete_drawing` (which the drawing's context menu, properties dialog and object tree row call) or `DrawingManager::clear_all` (Remove all drawings), until it is unlocked, and the eraser no longer highlights it.
- Drawings are drawn in ascending `Drawing::z_order` and hit from the highest down, in list order where it is the same, so a layering set with `ZOrderService` takes effect and survives a save and load. `DrawingManager::bring_to_front` and `send_to_back` set `z_order` instead of moving the drawing in `DrawingManager::drawings`, a drawing created with the tools starts on the top layer, and a drawing restored through `DrawingManager::add_synced_drawing` keeps its stored z-order.
- A drawing shown only on some intervals is no longer drawn on the others, and `Chart::set_timeframe` sets the interval its visibility is checked against, where the check always used 1D and applied only to selection. The Visibility tab of the drawing properties dialog names the minute intervals 1min to 30min, as the chart's interval label does, where it offered 1m to 30m, which matched no interval.
- The trend line, ray, extended line, horizontal and vertical lines, horizontal ray, cross line and trend angle are drawn in the drawing's dashed or dotted line style, and a trend line set to extend left or right reaches that edge of the pane and is hit along the extension, where these lines were always solid and the trend line ended at its anchors.
- A drawing restored with `stored_to_drawing` keeps the id, creation time and notes of its stored record in the new `Drawing::stored_id`, `created_at` and `notes` fields, and `drawing_to_stored` saves it back under them, where a load and save renamed every drawing to its session id, reset its creation time and dropped its notes. `DrawingManager` gives a new drawing an id above every id its drawings hold or are stored under, so a drawing created after a restore no longer replaces an older stored one. Deleting a restored drawing queues `DrawingSyncAction::Delete` with its stored id, and a copy of a drawing passed to `DrawingManager::add_synced_drawing` while the original is there is saved as a new drawing rather than over the original. Migration: construct drawings with `Drawing::new`, or set `stored_id`, `created_at` and `notes` in a struct literal of `Drawing`.
- The measure, info line, price range, date range and date and price range drawings report durations from the bars' times instead of counting a bar as one minute, the volume traded between their anchors, and the price change with its sign; the info line's angle is positive for a line that rises on screen. The measure no longer prints a pips figure computed as the price change times 100, nor `Vol --`.
- The flat top/bottom and disjoint channel drawings label the price distance between their two lines as drawn on the chart's price scale, logarithmic included, with its percentage of the lower line, and the rectangle labels its width in bars and its height in price, all from the anchors' bars and prices instead of screen pixels, so the labels no longer change with zoom and pan. The trend angle prints its price change, bars and duration in place of a pixel length and slope.
- The Fibonacci channel moves its levels straight up or down from the base line, as the parallel channel moves its second line, so each level is as far in price from the base at every bar; on a sloped base they were moved at right angles to it and slid sideways.
- Fibonacci retracements, extensions and channels are selected along the level lines they draw and nowhere else: over the extent the lines are drawn, including the part extended to the chart edge, and for the channel along its sloped levels instead of fixed horizontal ones. The undrawn diagonal between the anchors no longer selects them.
- Rays, extended and cross lines, horizontal rays, price and date ranges, the measure, polylines, paths, brushes, arrows, trend angles, info lines, the harmonic, head and shoulders, triangle and Elliott wave patterns, triangles, circles, ellipses, arcs, curves, every pitchfork and the pitchfan, the Gann fan, box and squares, the flat top/bottom and disjoint channels, and the Fibonacci time zones and speed resistance fan are selected anywhere along the lines they draw, from the same geometry their renderers draw, where they could be selected only near an anchor. The measure, date range, date and price range, Gann box and squares, the two channels, and filled triangles, circles and ellipses are also selected inside the areas they fill; brushes and the highlighter are selected across the width they are drawn at. The Gann fan is hit along its nine lines to the chart edge instead of eight lines that stopped at the second anchor, and an unfilled circle is no longer selected at its centre.
- A ray whose second anchor is left of its first is drawn, and selected, from its first anchor through its second to the left edge of the pane; it ran from its first anchor to the right edge, away from its second.
- `DrawingStorage::to_json` refuses a drawing whose tool is a cursor, such as the eraser, with `SaveError::Cursor`, instead of saving a drawing that `from_json` skips on the next load.
- `DrawingManager::pending_sync` receives one `DrawingSyncAction` per drawing for every change to a stored field: drawings finished by a drag, a double-click or Enter, clearing all drawings, hiding or locking them all, bringing to front, sending to back, undo, redo and a copy added with `add_synced_drawing` now queue theirs too. The new `DrawingManager::edit_drawing` applies an edit, records it for undo and queues it, and `DrawingManager::drag_edit_drawing` with `end_drag_handle` makes a continuous edit, such as a colour picker drag, one undo step and one update. The host drains the queue; the chart never does. Migration: `clear_all` now queues a `Delete` for each drawing it removes, so a host that saves from the queue deletes their stored records; to unload drawings without deleting them, clear `drawings`, then call `cancel_curr_drawing` and `deselect`.
- Choosing the eraser in the drawing bar lets a click delete the drawing under the pointer. The chart erased only while a crate-private flag was on that nothing set; it now follows `DrawingManager::eraser_mode`, which the new `DrawingManager::set_eraser_mode` switches on (leaving the active tool) and off, and which choosing a tool with `set_active_tool` switches off.
- In eraser mode a click deletes the drawing under the pointer while another drawing is selected too, instead of selecting it; a locked drawing is still not erased, and Escape switches the eraser off.
- The drawing bar's "Save as Template..." opens a template name dialog that stays open until the name is saved or the dialog is cancelled, instead of closing on its first frame because its empty name read as a cancel. The visibility flyout closes when the dialog opens. `TemplatesButton::show_name_dialog` returns `Some(DrawingToolbarAction::None)` on Cancel or Escape, where it returned `None`, so a caller can tell a cancel from a name still being typed.
- The drawing bar's Icons/Emojis button opens a menu of icons and emoji that emits `DrawingToolbarAction::InsertEmoji`, drawn with the app's fonts (the demo enables egui's `monochrome_emoji_fonts` and lists Noto Emoji and emoji-icon-font in its font licences), and the next click on the chart places the one picked, as does every later click in keep-drawing mode; the button opened nothing, and a click after `DrawingManager::start_icon_insertion` placed a default `*` instead of the picked icon.
- The drawing bar scrolls when the window is shorter than its buttons, so Templates, Hide and Remove stay reachable, and its flyouts scroll when they are taller than the window. A flyout opens beside its button, closes on an outside click or Escape without also clearing the chart's drawing state, and closes after a pick unless keep drawing is on.
- The drawing bar's buttons and flyout rows no longer overlap their neighbours by 3 and 5 px, so a click near the lower edge of a button no longer activates the next one. Tooltips open to the right of the bar instead of over the next button.
- The drawing bar shows the state it was left in: the cursor button shows the chosen cursor or the eraser, the cursor menu checks the current one and the values tooltip option, the magnet menu has an Off row and checks the current mode, and the magnet button is filled while snapping is on. Zoom and the cursor are no longer highlighted at the same time.
- The selected drawing bar button and flyout row use the accent fill with the accent text colour, readable in every theme; hovered and selected rows differ. Section headers and shortcut hints in the flyouts are 11 px and use the secondary text colour, and a flyout is as wide as its widest row, so "Elliott Triple Combo Wave (WXYXZ)" no longer meets the border.
- The drawing bar's menus share one look: rows 28 px high with a 16 px icon column, the theme's menu fill, border and shadow. The Hide menu reads "Hide drawings", "Hide indicators", "Hide positions" and "Hide orders", the Remove menu shows its two rows in the error colour, and the Text flyout's sections read "TEXT & NOTES" and "CONTENT" in the order the bar uses.
- Typing "/" in a text field no longer opens the drawing bar's tool search; with no field focused it opens a search field beside the bar, with a close button.
- Drawing bar buttons and flyout rows have a role, a name and their selected state for assistive technology, and rows show a focus ring.

### Changed

- `UserSettings` is `#[non_exhaustive]`, so that fields can be added without a breaking change. Migration: build `UserSettings` with `Default` and assign fields. `IndicatorDialogAction` implements `Clone` and `Debug`, and `SettingsAction` implements `Debug`.

- Migration for scale settings: `ScalesAndLinesSettings`, `ChartConfig` and `PriceAxisOptions` literals add the new display fields or use `..Default::default()`. Older saved settings take their defaults. `chart::render_axis_currency` takes an optional unit before the currency choices; pass `None` for the previous header. `PriceScale::set_bar_ratio` accepts a positive finite number of scale units per bar, with `None` unlocking it; logarithmic axes measure natural logarithms of prices.

- `EventVisibility` struct literals add `show_earnings_breaks`, `show_news_notifications` and `show_ideas` or end with `..Default::default()`; saved visibility without them takes the defaults.

- `chart::render_alert_levels` takes `active_only` after `labels`; pass `true` to keep drawing only active alerts. `AlertsSettings` literals add `show_active_only` and `notification_seconds` or use `..Default::default()`; older saved settings take defaults. Hosts apply lifetime through `AlertsPanel::set_notification_seconds`; sound playback uses the existing host audio path.

- `ChartTypeStyles`, `CandleColorConfig`, `StatusLineOptions` and `ChartSettingsState` gain the requested candle, status and dividend fields. Struct literals add the fields or use `..Default::default()`; older saved settings take defaults. `chart::render_candle` takes the rising/falling direction after the bar and `&CandlesStyle` last; pass `bar.is_bullish()` and `&CandlesStyle::default()` for the previous drawing behaviour.

- Chart settings use seven tab pictograms, a 206 point sidebar with 40 point rows, neutral selection and an undimmed backdrop. Shared dialog titles use semibold 20 point text, and footer actions are at least 44 by 34 points with a primary fill in the theme text colour. ([#991](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/991))

- The chart layout menu shows arrangement diagrams grouped by chart count from 1 through 16, including available alternatives, and highlights the active arrangement. Hover labels name each arrangement and explain synchronization effects. Short windows scroll the menu. ([#997](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/997))

- The indicator browser lists Profiles and Patterns and searches across built-in pages. `IndicatorFactory::register_in` accepts host categories, including `PERSONAL` for My scripts and `COMMUNITY` for shared studies. Destinations without compatible studies stay hidden, host-only catalogues open their populated page, and Favorites retains its explanatory empty state. Migration: exhaustive `IndicatorCategory` matches handle `MyScripts`; volume and time price profiles now use the `Profiles` factory category (`IndicatorFactory::PROFILES`) instead of `Volume`. ([#972](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/972))

- A click, or Enter or Space, on an indicator browser result adds the study at once with its default inputs, and the browser stays open for the next one; an added study's inputs are edited through its settings with `IndicatorSettingsDialog`. A source the study rejects is reported above the results. Migration: the parameter panel is removed with `IndicatorParams`, `IndicatorDialog::{selected, params, params_mut, input_summary}` and `IndicatorDialogConfig::{sel_color, sel_text_color}`; caption an added study from its `Indicator::inputs`, and call `IndicatorDialog::close` on `IndicatorDialogAction::AddIndicator` to keep adding one study per visit. ([#971](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/971))

- Symbol details inset the title and price by 10 points inside the host panel and enlarge the main price to 26 points. The supplied `SymbolDetails::category` appears below the name, key statistics sit on a tinted card, and prices use the watchlist's thousands grouping. ([#990](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/990))

- Watchlist rows are 30 points high with an 18 point symbol mark and aligned names. `WatchlistPanel::set_mark` supplies a mark; rows without one show an initial on a neutral disc. Prices and changes use thousands grouping, so 43237.45 reads 43,237.45, and changes omit the sign when they round to zero. ([#989](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/989))

- Chart layout uses the shared dark toolbar tooltip with consistent padding and no shortcut chip when no shortcut is bound. ([#1011](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1011))

- Sell and Buy quote buttons use outlines and matching text on the chart background, with a minimum width of 59 points. Bid and Ask axis tags default to hidden while saved choices remain intact; volume shows a compact last-value tag when study values are enabled. ([#988](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/988))

- The demo allocates 25% of the price pane to overlay volume, keeping the columns on the plot bottom and the height configurable through `ChartConfig::volume_height_fraction`. ([#987](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/987))

- Light crosshair tags use black fills and white text, with dashed crosshair ink at approximately #9C9C9C on the white plot. ([#1007](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1007))

- The light preset draws grid lines in #D1D1D1 instead of #DCDCDC, so the dotted grid stays visible on the white plot. Horizontal and vertical grid lines keep their separate visibility settings. ([#1010](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/1010))

- Crate and public API documentation now describe host-supplied history and streaming data, current feature boundaries and a populated quick start. Stale capability claims, counts and implementation inventories are removed, and the README and package description use the same scope. ([#933](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/933))

- Charts follow active theme changes for inherited background, grid, text, candle, border and wick colours, retaining host edits and per-chart overrides. Chart titles, OHLC and pane legends, secondary series, trading labels and chart controls read the active text, spacing and radius scales. Clearing an override rejoins the current context palette. Migration: a default `ChartBuilder` now follows the active theme; call `with_theme(Theme::dark())` to retain a fixed dark chart beside a differently themed UI. Settings dialogs read `Theme` directly; UI chrome, shared buttons, forms, lists and dialog padding use the applied text, spacing and radius scales. Applying a theme also updates egui's built-in text sizes while retaining font families. Study defaults now use `studies::palette`, preserving their existing colours independently of UI status colours; unused study and extended colour tokens have been removed. Migration: replace the removed `theme::components::{DialogStyle, SettingsDialogStyle, ContextMenuStyle}` and their theme re-exports with `Theme` fields or egui visuals; replace `DESIGN_TOKENS.typography` and `TypographyTokens` with `theme.text`; pass `&Ui` instead of `&Visuals` to `StyledPanelFrame` constructors so separate frames also read the applied scales. Remove `SemanticTokens::{buttons, alert, modal, command_palette}` and their corresponding `ButtonSemanticTokens`, `AlertSemanticTokens`, `ModalSemanticTokens` and `CommandPaletteSemanticTokens` literals; `UiSemanticTokens` retains only the engine fallbacks `text_muted_dark` and `border_dark`. `TextSizes` literals add `micro`, `marker` and `dialog_title` or use `..Default::default()`; saved themes default missing roles. See [theme integration](docs/theme.md). ([#356](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/356))

- The next release is 0.3.0. Public implementation modules are private, extensible options and output records are non-exhaustive, and growing chart, interval, drawing, study and host-intent enums require fallback matches. The timezone formatter wrapper is crate-private; hosts keep using `TimeFormatterBuilder::with_timezone`. Main stateful types retain their private fields and accessors. Public configuration and data fields remain intentionally editable. CI compares the API with the latest published crate version and also gates release publication. Before 1.0, an incompatible API or exposed egui upgrade requires a new minor release; from 1.0 it requires a major release. The 0.3 line uses egui 0.37; compatible egui patch upgrades may ship in patch releases. ([#253](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/253))

  | Old path or construction | New path or construction |
  | --- | --- |
  | `widget::builder::ChartBuilder` | `widget::ChartBuilder` or the crate-root `ChartBuilder` |
  | `widget::indicator_pane::ChartMapping` | `chart::coords::ChartMapping` |
  | `widget::indicator_pane::{IndicatorCoordParams, IndicatorPane, IndicatorPaneConfig, PaneInteraction}` | `widget::{IndicatorCoordParams, IndicatorPane, IndicatorPaneConfig, PaneInteraction}` |
  | `widget::keyboard::{ShortcutMap, ShortcutRequest}` | `widget::{ShortcutMap, ShortcutRequest}` |
  | `widget::layout::{ChartLayout, ChartLayoutMode, ChartSyncOptions}` | `widget::{ChartLayout, ChartLayoutMode, ChartSyncOptions}` |
  | `widget::export::{ChartImage, ImageExportOptions}` | `widget::{ChartImage, ImageExportOptions}` |
  | `chart::selection::{SeriesId, ChartElementId}` | `widget::{SeriesId, ChartElementId}`; use `Chart::{selected_element, selected_bar, clear_selection}` instead of selection state helpers |
  | `chart::selection::{SelectionState, SelectableId}` | Use `Chart::{selected_element, selected_bar, clear_selection}` for a chart, or a host-owned `Option<Id>` for standalone selection |
  | Complete literals of `config::{ChartConfig, ChartOptions, CrosshairOptions, TooltipOptions}`, `widget::ImageExportOptions` | Start from constructors or `Default` and assign fields; struct literals, including update syntax, are unavailable. Output geometry and image records are returned by the chart |
  | Complete literals of `widget::{RenderedIndicatorPane, ChartLayoutRects, PaneInteraction, ChartImage}` | Obtain output geometry and image records from the chart and pane methods; `PaneInteraction::default()` remains available |
  | Exhaustive matches on growing chart, timeframe, drawing, keyboard, event, study and UI intent enums | Add a fallback arm for future variants |
  | `TimeframeSelectorConfig::{item_height, bg_color, hover_color, sel_color, text_color, muted_color, favorite_color}` | Removed unused options; rows use the menu layout and colours use the active theme. `btn_width` and `dropdown_width` remain supported |
  | `TimeframeAction::AddCustom` | Removed unused intent; custom intervals emit `TimeframeAction::Select` |

- Runtime module dependencies are acyclic. Shared bar interpolation, streaming study inputs, profile calculations and formatting live below rendering; study geometry and timestamp drawing methods live with their chart and widget consumers. Chart settings and study recipes no longer depend on UI modules, localization is shared at the crate root, and UI extension traits live beside the UI kit. CI rejects module cycles and inherent methods defined outside their type's module. The crate remains one package. ([#93](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/93))

  | Old path or member | New path or member |
  | --- | --- |
  | `model::{ChartState, ZoomState}` | `studies::{ChartState, ZoomState}` |
  | `studies::{OrderFlowInput, OrderFlowKind, ProfileDisplay, StudyProfile, VolumeProfile, VolumeProfileConfig}` | `data::{OrderFlowInput, OrderFlowKind, ProfileDisplay, StudyProfile, VolumeProfile, VolumeProfileConfig}` |
  | `drawings::{ProfileDisplay, VolumeProfileConfig}`, `drawings::domain::{ProfileDisplay, VolumeProfileConfig}`, `drawings::domain::options::{ProfileDisplay, VolumeProfileConfig}` | `data::{ProfileDisplay, VolumeProfileConfig}` |
  | `studies::StudyFormat` | `scales::StudyFormat` |
  | `services::PriceTargets` | `data::PriceTargets` |
  | `ui::dialogs::templates::StudyTemplate` | `studies::StudyTemplate` |
  | `widget::localization` | `localization` |
  | `ext::{UiExt, ResponseExt, ButtonWidgetExt, TextEditWidgetExt, RoundingExt}` | `ui_kit::ext::{UiExt, ResponseExt, ButtonWidgetExt, TextEditWidgetExt, RoundingExt}` |
  | `Theme::apply_to_config(config)` | `config.with_theme(&theme)` |
  | `ChartSettingsState::{background_color, grid_color, sync_legacy_fields}` | Use `chart_basic_styles.background_color` and `grid_lines.horizontal_color` directly; the redundant mirrors and synchronization method are removed |
  | `ui::top_toolbar::components::settings_menu::data::EventsSettings` and its settings-menu re-export | `model::EventVisibility` |
  | `ui::top_toolbar::{CandleColorConfig, StatusLineOptions, ChartSettingsState, PrecisionMode}` | `widget::settings::{CandleColorConfig, StatusLineOptions, ChartSettingsState, PrecisionMode}` |
  | `ui::top_toolbar::components::{CandleColorConfig, StatusLineOptions, ChartSettingsState, PrecisionMode}` | `widget::settings::{CandleColorConfig, StatusLineOptions, ChartSettingsState, PrecisionMode}` |
  | `ui::top_toolbar::components::settings_menu::{CandleColorConfig, StatusLineOptions, ChartBasicStylesSettings, GridLinesSettings, CrosshairSettings, WatermarkSettings, ScalesAppearanceSettings, ButtonsSettings, MarginsSettings, ScalesAndLinesSettings, TradingSettings, TradingNotifications, AlertsSettings, ChartSettingsState, BackgroundType, GridLinesMode, ButtonVisibility, WatermarkMode, PrecisionMode}` | `widget::settings::{CandleColorConfig, StatusLineOptions, ChartBasicStylesSettings, GridLinesSettings, CrosshairSettings, WatermarkSettings, ScalesAppearanceSettings, ButtonsSettings, MarginsSettings, ScalesAndLinesSettings, TradingSettings, TradingNotifications, AlertsSettings, ChartSettingsState, BackgroundType, GridLinesMode, ButtonVisibility, WatermarkMode, PrecisionMode}` |
  | `ui::top_toolbar::components::settings_menu::data::{CandleColorConfig, StatusLineOptions, ChartBasicStylesSettings, GridLinesSettings, CrosshairSettings, WatermarkSettings, ScalesAppearanceSettings, ButtonsSettings, MarginsSettings, ScalesAndLinesSettings, TradingSettings, TradingNotifications, AlertsSettings, ChartSettingsState}` | `widget::settings::{CandleColorConfig, StatusLineOptions, ChartBasicStylesSettings, GridLinesSettings, CrosshairSettings, WatermarkSettings, ScalesAppearanceSettings, ButtonsSettings, MarginsSettings, ScalesAndLinesSettings, TradingSettings, TradingNotifications, AlertsSettings, ChartSettingsState}` |
  | `ui::top_toolbar::components::settings_menu::types::{BackgroundType, GridLinesMode, ButtonVisibility, WatermarkMode, PrecisionMode}` | `widget::settings::{BackgroundType, GridLinesMode, ButtonVisibility, WatermarkMode, PrecisionMode}` |

- Breaking API cleanup: interval controls, settings, drawings, studies and trading share the domain types below. The small `prelude` exports common host types and traits, with widget exports following their feature. There are no compatibility aliases. Symbol search returns the host's `Instrument` with its metadata intact. ([#95](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/95))

  | Old path or member | New path or member |
  | --- | --- |
  | `ui::top_toolbar::Timeframe`, `ui::top_toolbar::components::Timeframe`, `ui::top_toolbar::components::timeframe_selector::Timeframe` | `model::Timeframe`; use its variants or parse `"7min"`, and pass it directly to the selector |
  | `drawings::LineStyle`, `drawings::domain::LineStyle`, `drawings::domain::options::LineStyle` | `model::LineStyle` |
  | `config::CrosshairLineStyle`, `config::SessionBreakStyle` | `model::LineStyle`; crosshair and session defaults remain dotted and dashed respectively |
  | `studies::PlotPattern` | `model::LineStyle` |
  | `ui::top_toolbar::components::settings_menu::LineStyle`, `ui::top_toolbar::components::settings_menu::data::LineStyle` | `model::LineStyle` |
  | `ui::widget_bar::LineStyle`, `ui::widget_bar::panels::LineStyle`, `ui::widget_bar::panels::object_tree::LineStyle`, `ui::widget_bar::panels::object_tree::types::LineStyle` | `model::LineStyle::name()` replaces `as_str()` |
  | `ui::dialogs::DrawingLineStyle`, `ui::dialogs::drawing_properties::DrawingLineStyle`, `ui::dialogs::drawing_properties::actions::DrawingLineStyle` | `model::LineStyle`; `name()` replaces `label()`, `all().iter().copied()` yields values |
  | `model::TradeSide`, `model::quote::TradeSide` | `model::OrderSide`; unknown aggressors remain `None` |
  | `model::StrategySide::{Long, Short}` | `model::OrderSide::{Buy, Sell}`; migrate saved strategy sides from `"Long"`/`"Short"` to `"Buy"`/`"Sell"` |
  | `model::Symbol` | `model::Instrument`; `name` becomes `symbol`, `display_name` becomes optional `description`, `exchange` becomes optional, and subscription state belongs to the host; use `with_exchange` and `with_symbol_type` instead of `with_market` |
  | `ui::symbol_header::SymbolInfo`, `SymbolHeader::symbol_info` | `model::Instrument`, `SymbolHeader::instrument`; pass the interval label separately to `SymbolHeader::new(instrument, timeframe)` |
  | `ui::top_toolbar::ScaleMode`, `ui::top_toolbar::components::ScaleMode`, `ui::top_toolbar::components::settings_menu::ScaleMode`, `ui::top_toolbar::components::settings_menu::types::ScaleMode` | `scales::PriceScaleMode`; `Auto` becomes `Normal` |
  | `ui::top_toolbar::components::settings_menu::types::{CandleColorConfig, StatusLineOptions}` | `widget::settings::{CandleColorConfig, StatusLineOptions}`; use `..Default::default()` for additional status fields |
  | `Indicator::line_cnt`, `CustomIndicator::with_line_cnt`, `LineBreakConfig::line_cnt` | `Indicator::line_count`, `CustomIndicator::with_line_count`, `LineBreakConfig::line_count` |
  | `ui::top_toolbar::components::settings_menu::TradingSettings::show_poss` | `TradingSettings::show_positions`; migrate the saved JSON key too |

  The selector accepts model intervals directly, including subsecond presets, and preserves full nonzero execution counts and custom seconds in its labels.

  Interval preferences now serialize `model::Timeframe` values instead of `{value, unit}` objects. Convert each old entry with its unit (`Minute` uses `"min"`, `Month` uses `"month"`), parse it and serialize the resulting value. Presets and arbitrary intervals use the same nonzero model representation. Existing line-pattern JSON variant names are unchanged.

- IANA time zones and daylight-saving rules are behind the `timezones` feature, enabled by default, so builds with `default-features = false` omit `chrono-tz`. UTC, system local time and signed fixed offsets remain available. All time zone APIs now use `model::TimezoneId`, including `TimezoneMode::{Timezone, Exchange}`, `TimezoneMode::to_tz`, `BarClock::with_timezone`, `TimeAndSalesConfig::timezone` and `SymbolDetailsPanel::set_timezone`. Migration: replace a `chrono_tz` constant with a parsed name such as `"America/New_York".parse()?`; add `"timezones"` to an explicit feature list to keep named exchange zones and presets. Without it, Trading Sessions uses fixed standard-time offsets (+09:00, +00:00 and -05:00), configurable through `with_session`. Use offsets such as `"+05:45".parse()?` or `TimezoneId::fixed_offset(seconds)`; unknown or unavailable names are rejected instead of approximated. Persisted named-zone strings retain their format. Session presets and the settings time zone list also use fixed offsets without `timezones`. CI checks licences and duplicate versions in both the library and demo against `deny.toml`, with pinned exceptions for existing upstream version splits. ([#256](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/256))

- Ordinary solid study lines draw each uninterrupted run as one joined path instead of a separate shape for every pair of bars, reducing painting and tessellation work on charts with many studies while keeping gaps, colours and stroke widths. Directional colours and gradient fills retain their segment rendering. The README links performance notes with application-owned glibc allocator settings for large native charts. ([#472](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/472))
- Replay no longer holds a separate simulated balance or position. Migration: use `services::PaperAccount` through `Broker` instead of `TradingSimulationState`, `ReplayState::trading_sim`, the simulation actions on `ReplayAction`, or the trading fields on `ReplayBehavior`. ([#221](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/221))

- `WatchlistPanel::set_quote` and `SymbolDetailsPanel::set_quote` take a `model::Quote`, which carries the update time, extended-hours trade and session volume, and `SymbolHeader::quote` is an `Option<Quote>` whose bid and ask show when both are known. `ExtendedHoursQuote` and `ExtendedSession` moved from `ui::widget_bar` to `model`. The symbol details panel takes its price decimals from the instrument, or from the size of the price. Migration: build `Quote::new(last, prev_close, time)` in place of a `WatchlistQuote` and pass its decimals to `WatchlistPanel::set_price_decimals`; replace `SymbolDetailsPanel::set_extended_hours` with `Quote::with_extended`, and `set_volume(volume, average)` with `Quote::with_volume` and `set_average_volume`; set `SymbolHeader::quote` to `Some(Quote::new(..).with_bid_ask(bid, ask))` in place of a `QuoteData`. ([#222](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/222))
- The chart's order book keys levels by the nearest integer tick of the instrument's tick size, so a feed that computes prices with rounding errors updates and removes the level it means instead of adding a neighbour. A crossed book is kept as the feed sends it, and `Sink::push_quote` now keeps a crossed quote, the top of such a book, instead of rejecting it. ([#229](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/229))
- `Sink::push_depth_delta` takes the delta by reference, so a feed can clear and refill one `OrderBook` for every update instead of allocating its levels per message, and the chart's book keeps each side in a sorted array that reuses its capacity, so applying a delta allocates nothing once the sides reached their depth. Migration: pass `&changes`. ([#228](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/228))
- The web demo builds with the `wasm-release` profile, whole-program optimisation at `opt-level = "s"`, which cuts its download by 15%; native builds keep `release`. The library defines the same profile for its own web builds, and `scripts/web-size.sh` builds the demo and the bare chart for the web and prints their raw, gzip and brotli sizes. `examples/bare_chart.rs` now also runs in the browser, where it applies the dark theme, so its web build carries Roboto alone instead of egui's default fonts. The demo renders with eframe's `glow` backend instead of `wgpu`, which takes another 17% off its download, and exports images through an offscreen framebuffer. The crate documentation lists these steps for an application's web build, with chrono-tz's `filter-by-regex` to keep only the time zones it needs. ([#251](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/251))
- Zoomed out until several bars share a physical pixel, a time-based chart draws them merged, about one bar per pixel column, instead of every bar. Candles, bars, columns, HLC area and high-low merge each group into its first open, highest high, lowest low and last close with its highest volume. Line, step, area and baseline charts keep the first, lowest, highest and last value of each group at their own bars, which draws the same line through the column. Groups with whitespace or per-bar colours retain their source bars for drawing, so gaps and individual styles stay visible. Volume, the time axis, sessions and hit testing read the merged groups, which are aligned to multiples of a power of two so panning keeps them, and are rebuilt only when the bars, the zoom step or the price source change. One chart of 1,000,000 bars all in view now paints 3,043 shapes in a 12 ms frame instead of 3,000,104 shapes in 1,864 ms, measured by the new scenario of `benches/frame_time.rs`. Heikin-Ashi, price-based and TPO charts, and study plots other than the volume on the main pane, still draw every bar. ([#834](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/834))
- Bars charts draw their bars and ticks with `ChartConfig::wick_width`, one point by default, instead of a fixed 1.5 point stroke, so they stay as crisp as candle wicks. Turn off `BarsStyle::thin_bars` for bars that widen with the zoom. ([#832](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/832))
- Panes keep their proportions when the chart is resized: dragging a separator and a study's preferred pane height now set stretch factors instead of fixed pixel heights. A double click on a separator still restores the default shares. ([#827](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/827))
- `PriceScaleMargins` are shares of the pane height rather than of the data range, as the chart settings dialog already presented them: the default 10% above and below keeps the data in the middle 80% of the pane, and margins that leave no room for the data are ignored. `PriceMarkGeneratorConfig` defaults to a 20 pixel `min_spacing` and 100 `max_marks`, so `target_density` alone sets the spacing, which stays 40 pixels by default. ([#833](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/833))
- `DetrendedPriceOscillator` defaults to the conventional length 21 in its non-centred form, the close minus the 21-bar SMA of 11 bars earlier, aligned with the current bar. The new `centered` input, or `with_centered(true)`, selects the centred form, the close 11 bars back minus the SMA, which is now stored at the bar that completes the average and drawn back through `plot_offset`, so it no longer reads later bars and fills the bars a previous rule left blank. Bars with a non-finite close are skipped and the pane draws a zero level. Migration: for the previous series use `DetrendedPriceOscillator::new(20).with_centered(true)` and read a centred value with `plot_value`, or at its source bar plus `period / 2 + 1`. ([#176](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/176))
- `PivotPoints` is the Pivot Points Standard study: levels for each day, week, month, quarter or year of exchange trading days come from the previous period's open, high, low and close, counted in the instrument's time zone and session as VWAP counts them, instead of a rolling window of bars. `PivotTimeframe::Auto`, the default, picks the period from the chart interval: daily up to 15 minutes, weekly below a day, monthly below a week and yearly above. The Traditional, Fibonacci, Woodie (with the current period's open), Classic, DM and Camarilla types output P, R1, S1 through R5, S5, NaN where a type has no such level, for the last 15 periods by default (`with_pivots_back`). Lines are constant within a period and not drawn across the change to the next. `AnchorPeriod` names the calendar periods. Migration: `PivotPoints::new(period)` becomes `PivotPoints::new()` with `with_method`, `with_timeframe` and `with_pivots_back`; `PivotMethod::Standard` is `Traditional` and `DeMark` is `DM`, which now has P, R1 and S1 only; the catalogue name `"Pivot Points"` is `"Pivot Points Standard"`; values are eleven lines in the order above instead of `[Pivot, S1, S2, S3, R1, R2, R3]`, and `set_colors` sets them line by line. Saved study templates of the previous study no longer load. ([#176](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/176))
- The widget bar's bottom section keeps host entries at the foot of the bar above its line, with Help below the line, so a lower group of host panels stands apart from Help. Without host entries the bottom section is unchanged. The demo opens with its watchlist and the chart symbol's details in a 300 point panel, lists the watchlist before Alerts and the Object Tree, and moves Data Window, Time and sales and Session notes to the lower group; compact windows still start with the chart alone.
- The built-in `Volume` study starts with its moving average off and draws its columns in the theme's rising and falling volume colours at reduced opacity; the chart's volume switch applies the active theme's `volume_up` and `volume_down`. Its legend row reads Vol and the bar's volume in the study's declared volume format (154, 64.55M), coloured like that bar's column. The study no longer fixes three decimals. Migration: turn the average on with `Volume::with_average(Some(VolumeAverage::Simple))` or the Moving average input, and set a precision override in the study settings to keep three decimals. ([#817](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/817))
- `KineticScrollOptions::dumping_coeff` is renamed to `damping_coeff`, the share of kinetic scroll velocity kept per frame; its default and effect are unchanged. Migration: replace `dumping_coeff` with `damping_coeff` in field access and struct literals. ([#369](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/369))
- Chart menu orders choose sides relative to the last price, use the quantity and capabilities from `ChartMenus::set_order_entries`, and open the ticket for confirmation. At the market price they may fill immediately. Migration: replace `ContextMenuAction::SellLimit` and `BuyStop` with `Order(OrderRequest)`; `ChartContextMenu` literals add `market_price`, `tick_size`, `order_quantity` and `order_types`, or use `new()`. ([#218](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/218))
- Chart navigation buttons default to `NavigationButtons::Never`, leaving the plot clear on hover. Set `OnHover` or `Always` to show the existing controls; keyboard navigation stays available. The settings dialog starts with the same hidden choice, and the row hides if it cannot fit in the pane.
- The plot uses the chart interior with a floating legend and the configured axis widths instead of fixed 40 point insets. `ChartConfig::padding` defaults to 0. `ChartConfig::volume_baseline_inset` makes the volume baseline configurable, retaining the 12 point default; set it to 0 and `volume_height_fraction` to 0.25 for a flush quarter-height overlay. Axis ticks that would cross the plot edge are omitted. Migration: struct literals of `ChartConfig` set `volume_baseline_inset` or use `..Default::default()`. The unused `styles::sizing::chart::{TOP_PADDING_WITH_OHLC, TOP_PADDING_NO_OHLC, BOTTOM_PADDING_NO_TIME, MIN_CHART_WIDTH, MIN_CHART_HEIGHT}` constants are removed; use `Chart::calculate_layout_rects` for configured bounds.
- Light chart grids use 1 point dots with 3 point gaps in #dcdcdc, for both price and time lines. Explicit grid colours are kept; dark chart grids use the same dotted strokes.
- The light preset uses neutral text, separators, control states and panel gaps, white plot and axis surfaces, and #089981 up candles and #f23645 down candles. Semantic success and error colours stay separate from the candle palette.
- `theme::apply_to_egui` installs the bundled Roboto Latin subset with tabular digits, at weight 400 with semibold symbols and time-axis years. Toolbar text is 14 px, legend values 13 px with 16 px titles, axes and price tags 12 px, and the bottom bar 14 px, with its right controls reserved at their actual width. `TimeframeToolbarSizingTokens` and `SizingTokens::timeframe_toolbar` are removed because the reservation follows the controls. Migration: remove those token fields from struct literals; to replace the bundled face, call `egui::Context::set_fonts` after applying the theme.
- Volume bars take #26a69a up and #ef5350 down from the theme at 60% opacity in dark. The library defaults stand 12 points above the price pane bottom, reach 12% of the height above that baseline and span 80% of the bar pitch, with candle bodies defaulting to 60%. Reported layout bounds, hit testing and selection handles follow the overlaid bars.
- Bollinger Bands default to #2962ff upper and lower bands and a #ffa726 basis, and overlay study lines default to 1 point instead of 2. Study colour overrides and oscillator pane widths stay the same.
- The dark preset uses #000000 for panels, plot and axes, #2a2a2a separators, #dbdbdb text and toolbar icons, #b8b8b8 secondary text and time labels, #707070 muted text, #575757 disabled text, white price labels, #222222 grid lines, #089981 up candles, #f23645 down candles and #3179f5 active toggles. Light and high contrast preset values stay the same.
- On Renko, Kagi, Line Break, Point and Figure and range bar charts, the indices the chart reports and accepts count elements instead of bars: the `TimeScale` and `visible_range` of `ChartState`, `ChartState::visible_data` (one bar per element), `Chart::set_start_idx`, `get_start_idx`, `get_visible_bars`, `get_chart_mapping`, `selected_bar`, `get_hover_bar_idx`, `set_synced_crosshair_bar_idx` and `RightClickTarget::Element::bar_idx`. `Chart::set_chart_type`, and `set_chart_type_params` on these types, clear the zoom history when the value changes. Migration: read the bars from `Chart::bars`, and find the bars of an element by its time.
- Studies, drawings and markers are not drawn on Renko, Kagi, Line Break, Point and Figure and range bar charts, whose bricks, lines and columns do not map onto the bars they are anchored to. They cannot be selected, edited or hit there, and studies do not count in the price fit. They are kept, studies stay up to date, and they show again on the other chart types; a note in the price pane says what is hidden. These chart types draw no volume and no longer reserve a volume area. `Chart::set_chart_type` clears the selection when the type changes.
- The indicator dialog lists every study of its `IndicatorFactory` instead of 24 hardcoded entries, each row showing the full name and the explanation from the study's `Indicator::desc` (the registered name when the description has no "Full name - " prefix), sorted by that name and searchable by its registered name and category, and creates the one added through the factory, so a study the host registers is offered too. `IndicatorDialog::with_factory` sets the factory and `IndicatorDialogAction::AddIndicator` carries the created study. Migration: replace `with_indicators` with `with_factory`, and handle `AddIndicator(study)` by adding `study` to the chart instead of calling `create_indicator` on `AddConfiguredIndicator`; `IndicatorType`, `ConfiguredIndicator`, `IndicatorInfo::builtin_indicators` and `IndicatorInfo::indicator_type` are removed, and `IndicatorInfo::id` is the factory name, so favourites and `ToggleFavorite` ids saved under the old ids ("sma", "bb", "stoch") must be mapped to factory names ("SMA", "BB", "Stochastic").
- Volume bubbles are drawn by the chart from the trades its feed sends: `Chart::set_volume_bubbles` turns them on with a `chart::overlays::VolumeBubblesConfig`. Trades are summed per bar and price level (`price_step`, else the instrument's tick size) as they arrive, and a frame reads only the bubbles of the visible bars, the levels of a bar that fall on one pixel row drawing as one bubble, so its cost follows the bubbles in view instead of growing with the square of the trades held: 16,000 trades that did not merge took 1.6 s per frame before; now 100,000 held trades over 1,667 one-minute bars, 40 levels a bar, draw 200 bars in 45 ms including tessellation, and 100,000 trades at 100,000 prices all in view in 15 ms. The bubbles hold no trade the chart's bounded trade store has dropped, and Renko, Kagi, line break, point and figure and range charts draw none, since their x axis is not time. Sizes scale over the visible bubbles, a volume more than `clamp_std_devs` standard deviations (3 by default) above their mean draws at the maximum diameter so one large print does not shrink the rest, and `color_by` colours a bubble by its dominant aggressor side (`BubbleColor::Side`) or blends the sell and buy colours by its delta (`BubbleColor::Delta`). Migration: `VolumeBubbles`, `TradeBubble`, `AggregatedBubble`, `VolumeBubblesConfig::aggregate_nearby` and `VolumeBubblesConfig::aggregate_threshold` are removed: push trades into the feed's `Sink` with `push_tick` and pass the configuration to `Chart::set_volume_bubbles`; struct literals of `VolumeBubblesConfig` set `color_by`, `clamp_std_devs` and `price_step` or use `..Default::default()`. ([#234](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/234))
- The last-price line is drawn as a price line: the same width, with the standard dashed pattern (8 points on, 4 off) instead of its shorter dashes.
- The export dialog uses the kit dialog chrome: a left-aligned title with a close button, one form grid with sentence-case labels, an instruction as the path hint, and a footer with Export as the primary action. Enter exports, and Escape closes it unless a field has the focus, which it leaves instead. An empty path keeps the dialog open, shows the error under the field and focuses it. The file path field is named by its label.
- `config::KeyboardAction::all_by_category` returns only the actions whose keys are handled, in five categories, and `KeyboardAction::label` uses sentence case ("Zoom in more", "Jump to latest").
- The go to date picker is a modal titled "Go to date" in the dialog chrome, over a dimmed backdrop, and Escape closes it. It always shows six weeks, with the days of the previous and next month muted and selectable, so it keeps its height in every month. Chevron buttons at the edges of the grid change the month, the footer has a ghost Today button and Go as the primary action, a range shows both of its ends filled and the days between in a light accent, and today has a ring. When the picker opens, the date field has the focus with its text selected, and Enter applies Go. Each day reports its date and selected state to accessibility tools and shows a focus ring. Today is the current UTC date, the time zone of the chart's time axis by default. ([#299](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/299))
- The indicator dialog is a kit `Modal`: the shared title row, frame and scrim, Escape and a click outside close it, and it keeps its configured height, clamped to the window and following it when the window shrinks, with only the result list scrolling. The search field is the kit filter field with a search icon and a clear button, focused on open; Escape clears a search before it closes the dialog. Tabs use `TabBar` and categories `SidebarTab` with their own icons. Rows show a star icon with a tooltip, the name at 13 px over an 11 px description in the secondary text colour, cut off before outlined badges that sit inside the row padding clear of the scroll bar, and the theme's selection colours when selected. ArrowDown in the search field moves to the first row, the arrows move between rows, Enter selects a row and Enter on the selected row adds it. Each empty list has its own empty state. The configuration panel lays out its parameters in a `FormGrid` and ends in a `DialogFooter` whose Back button returns to the list instead of closing the dialog.
- `Chart::set_renko_brick_size`, `renko_brick_size`, `set_kagi_reversal_amount` and `kagi_reversal_amount` are replaced by `set_chart_type_params` and `chart_type_params`, which hold the sizes of all price-based chart types. `ChartTypeParams::baseline_price` is replaced by `baseline: BaselineLevel`, and the defaults are now 1.0 for every size (the unused struct said 4.0 and 10.0). Migration: `let mut p = chart.chart_type_params().clone(); p.renko_brick_size = 2.0; chart.set_chart_type_params(p);`.
- `ChartType` has a new variant, `Columns`. Migration: an exhaustive `match` on `ChartType` needs a `ChartType::Columns` arm.
- `ui_kit::EmptyState::show` returns `bool`, whether the action was clicked, instead of `()`. Migration: an `EmptyState::show` call in a place that expects `()`, such as the last expression of an `if` without `else`, takes a semicolon.
- egui-charts depends on egui, egui_extras and ecolor 0.37 instead of 0.33 and needs Rust 1.95. Migration: move the application to egui and eframe 0.37 and Rust 1.95. An eframe application implements `App::ui`, which receives the root `Ui`, instead of `App::update`, and shows its panels inside that `Ui` with `show(ui, ..)` on `egui::Panel::top`, `bottom`, `left` or `right` (which replace `TopBottomPanel` and `SidePanel`) and on `CentralPanel`. egui creates the root `Ui` before the application runs, so a call to `apply_to_egui` in `App::ui` is followed by `ui.set_style(ui.ctx().global_style())`. The id and id salt parameters of `UiExt::combo_select`, `combo_select_width`, `combo_str_select` and `combo_str_select_width`, `ColorPicker::new`, `color_picker_btn`, `ModalHandler::new`, `Modal::new`, `SimpleModal::new`, `show_modal`, `PromptDialog::new`, `prompt`, `prompt_with_message`, `ConfirmDialog::new`, `confirm`, `confirm_danger`, `AutocompleteEdit::id_salt`, `Section::new`, `ResponsiveToolbar::horizontal` and `vertical`, and `RangeSwitcher::with_id` require `Hash + Debug`, as egui's ids do, so an id type of the application's own needs `Debug`. Code that builds `tokens::SizingTokens` with a struct literal sets the new `control_height`. egui 0.37's `default_fonts` contain no emoji: enable egui's `monochrome_emoji_fonts` feature or install a font that has them. Panel sizes and other widget state that egui persisted (window sizes, collapsed headers, scroll offsets) reset once, because egui derives panel and child ids differently. An eframe build with default features off enables eframe's `clipboard` feature for copy and paste.
- `apply_to_egui` sets the button padding (4 x 1), control height (18) and corner radius (2) of egui's built-in widgets, so they keep the size they had with egui 0.33 instead of egui 0.37's 8 x 2, 20 and 4 to 6. An application that sets these itself does so after `apply_to_egui`.
- egui fills the title bar of the window on top with `widgets.open.weak_bg_fill`; since 0.35 it does so also for windows with their own frame, such as the Go to and compare symbol dialogs. `apply_to_egui` sets it to the theme's `btn_bg_hover` instead of egui's grey, which also fills an open combo box or submenu button.
- `SymbolSearchDialog` and `ExportDialog` open centred horizontally in the window, a fifth of the way down, and can still be moved. They grow downwards as their content changes, so the search field stays in place while results appear. Since egui 0.34 a window without a position opens at the top-left corner of the window, over the application's toolbars, instead of in the space its panels leave.
- The floating selection toolbar's settings, duplicate, lock, visibility and delete buttons show the crate's SVG icons instead of emoji, which egui 0.37's default fonts no longer contain; no bundled font had the duplicate glyph.
- The chart widget is behind a new feature, `widget`, which `icons` and `ui` enable. `default-features = false` now builds the headless engine (`model`, `scales`, `data`, `services`, `tokens` and the `Indicator` trait layer of `studies`), which depends on `ecolor` instead of egui and on no windowing or rendering crate. Migration: a build that sets `default-features = false` to drop the icons adds `features = ["widget"]` to keep the chart.
- `Timeframe::Custom` holds a `NonZeroU64`, so no timeframe has a zero duration: deserializing `{"Custom":0}` fails, as parsing `"0s"` already did, and the serialized form is unchanged. Migration: build a custom timeframe with `Timeframe::Custom(NonZeroU64::new(seconds)?)` or by parsing a label such as `"45s"`, and read its length with `seconds.get()` or `duration_ms()`.
- egui-charts no longer enables egui's default features. Applications built on eframe keep the default fonts (eframe enables them). Migration: an application in which no dependency enables egui's `default_fonts` feature (for example eframe with default features off and no direct egui dependency) must enable it or install its own fonts.
- The chart installs only egui_extras' SVG image loader, and only with the `icons` feature, instead of every image loader (file, HTTP, PNG, JPEG, GIF, WebP). Migration: an application that loads other images must call `egui_extras::install_image_loaders` with the egui_extras loader features it needs enabled.
- `rand`, `getrandom`, `wasm-bindgen` and `web-sys` are no longer dependencies, `egui_extras` is needed only by the `icons` feature and `thiserror` only by `ui`, which removes the `image` crate, the HTTP crates and the random number crates from a default build. Migration: a wasm32 application that relied on this crate enabling getrandom's `wasm_js` backend must enable it itself.
- `Theme` holds the colours and scales directly: `theme.semantic.ui` is `theme.ui` and `theme.semantic.chart` is `theme.chart`, and `theme::UiSemanticTokens` and `theme::ChartSemanticTokens` are `theme::UiColors` and `theme::ChartColors`. `UiColors` gains `focus`, the keyboard focus colour, and `danger_bg`, `danger_bg_hover`, `danger_bg_active` and `danger_text`, which kit Danger buttons and Danger list items now fill from instead of the danger button tokens. Fields that duplicated another are removed: `toolbar_bg`, `btn_bg` and `btn_bg_disabled` are `panel_bg`; `toolbar_separator` and `border_subtle` are `border`; `icon` is `text_secondary` and `icon_hover` is `text`; `icon_active`, `border_focus` and `text_link` are `accent`; `candle_up_border` and `candle_up_wick` are `candle_up`, and likewise for down; `volume_up` and `volume_down` are methods using `volume_alpha`. In the dark theme the separators and resting icons that read the removed fields are drawn in the border and secondary text colours, and hovered icons in the text colour; in the light theme the separators are the border colour and the resting icons the secondary text colour. Migration: replace `.semantic.ui.` with `.ui.` and `.semantic.chart.` with `.chart.`, `theme::UiSemanticTokens` with `theme::UiColors` and `theme::ChartSemanticTokens` with `theme::ChartColors`, and a removed field with the one it duplicated; `theme.is_dark_ui()` is `theme.dark`; the colour accessors such as `theme.background()` are the fields, such as `theme.chart.bg`.
- `Theme::default()` is the dark theme. It was Classic, light UI chrome with the dark chart.
- `ContextMenuStyle`, `DialogStyle` and `SettingsDialogStyle` are built with `from_theme(&Theme)` instead of `from_semantic`.
- The bottom toolbar, timeframe bar, date picker, range switcher and right panel tabs follow any change of the applied theme, including an edited preset, instead of only a change of preset.
- `scales::PriceScale` is the chart's price axis and keeps its visible range in scale units: `PriceScale::new(options)`, `autoscale`, `zoom`, `set_range`, `reset`, `range`, `is_manual`, `options` and `options_mut`, and `map`, which returns the frame's `scales::PriceMap` for a `scales::PriceTransform` (mode and base price). `PriceMap::price_to_y` and `y_to_price` do not clamp to the pane. Migration: `PriceScale::new(options)` replaces `new(height)` and `with_options`; `map(transform, top, bottom).price_to_y(price)` and `.y_to_price(y)` replace `price_to_coord` and `coord_to_price`; `autoscale`, `set_range`, `reset` and `range` replace `auto_scale`, `set_manual_range`, `reset_auto_scale`, `price_range` and `visible_price_range`; `options_mut` replaces `set_options`; the pane extent and `PriceTransform::base` passed to `map` replace `set_height` and `set_first_val`.
- `PriceScaleOptions` no longer has `auto_scale`; use `PriceScale::is_manual` and `PriceScale::reset`. `PriceScaleMargins` fields are `f64` and default to 0.1 at the top and the bottom, the margins the chart has always drawn.
- `ChartMapping` holds the frame's `price: PriceMap` instead of `price_min` and `price_max`, and `ChartMapping::new` takes it as its last argument. `price` keeps its own vertical extent, so `ChartMapping::with_rect` moves only the x mapping and the bounds checks. Migration: pass `PriceMap::new(PriceTransform::default(), PriceRange::new(min, max), rect.min.y, rect.max.y, false)` for a linear axis.
- `ChartState` owns the main price scale: `price_scale`, `price_scale_mut` and `fit_price_scale` replace `price_range`, `set_price_range`, `set_price_auto_scale` and `is_price_auto_scale`. Migration: `state.price_scale_mut().set_range(min, max)`, `.reset()` and `state.price_scale().is_manual()`; the fitted range is `state.price_scale().range()` after `state.fit_price_scale()`. `ZoomState::price_range` holds a manual range in scale units.
- `PriceMarkGenerator::generate_marks` takes the frame's `&PriceMap` instead of a price range, height, mode and pane bounds. In percentage and indexed modes `PriceMark::price` holds the percentage or index value, not a price.
- `Chart::handle_drawings` takes the frame's `&PriceMap` instead of the lowest and highest price.
- `model::Tick` is `#[non_exhaustive]` and has an `id` field. Migration: build it with `Tick::new(ts, price, size, side)`, adding `.with_id(id)` when the feed has trade ids.
- `model::SymbolType` is `#[non_exhaustive]`. Migration: add a wildcard arm to matches on it.
- `tokens::UiSemanticTokens` replaces `warning` and `success` with per-theme status colours `{success,warning,error,info}_{light,dark}`, and gains `text_disabled_light`, `text_disabled_dark`, `border_control_light`, `border_control_dark`, `selection_bg_light` and `selection_text_light`; `theme::UiColors` has `border_control`, the outline `apply_to_egui` gives inactive and hovered widgets. Migration: read `DESIGN_TOKENS.semantic.ui.warning_dark` and `success_dark` for the old values, or the theme's `ui.warning` and `success` to follow the active theme; struct literals must set the new fields.
- The theme's `accent_active` (pressed accent) is a darker accent, (31, 78, 205), instead of the grey `buttons.primary_bg_active`, and `accent_hover` is (35, 88, 230) instead of the lighter `blue_600`. `tokens::BrandSemanticTokens` gains `accent_active`; struct literals must set it. The `buttons.danger_bg`, `danger_bg_hover` and `danger_bg_active` tokens are darker reds.
- Replaced the bundled icon set with original artwork drawn on a 24 px grid. Toolbar, chart-type and widget-bar icons now render at 24 px, and close buttons, the cross cursor, alert and toast states, move to pane, add to watchlist, the replay next-bar button, the price range tool, the arrow tool and the note, callout, comment, table, image and other annotation tools show their own icons.
- `Drawing::render`, `Drawing::render_original`, and `DrawingManager::render_all` take a `drawings::rendering::ChartView` holding the chart's bars and coordinate mapping. Migration: build `ChartView { bars, bar_to_x, price_to_y }` from the series and the closures passed to `update_all_screen_coords`, and pass it as the last argument.
- `drawings::rendering::ChartView` has a `revision` field, under which results computed from the bars are cached. Migration: set it to `ChartState::revision()` of the state the bars come from, or to a value that changes whenever a bar before the last one changes.
- The regression trend draws one pair of bands at its configured distance (two standard deviations by default) instead of bands at one and two.
- Long and short position tools size their quantity from `Drawing::position_size`; without a setting the quantity is one unit, so P&L reads per unit, instead of a fixed 100. The target and stop labels no longer repeat the quantity, and a quantity that is not whole is shown with up to four decimals, or four significant digits below 0.1. Migration: `Drawing::quantity` is replaced by `Drawing::position_size`; `quantity: Some(q)` becomes `position_size: Some(PositionSize::Quantity(f64::from(q)))`.
- `Chart::handle_drawings` no longer takes the last close price.
- `TooltipOptions::default()` leaves `background_color`, `text_color` and `tracking_bar_background` as `Color32::PLACEHOLDER`, which resolves from the active theme; set them explicitly to keep fixed colours.
- `SymbolHeaderConfig::default()` text, label and separator colours are `Color32::PLACEHOLDER` and follow the egui style; `SymbolHeaderConfig::from_theme` now uses the theme it is given.
- `apply_to_egui` styles the selection per theme. Dark themes fill it with the accent and set `visuals.selection.stroke` to white; light themes fill it with a pale accent, (219, 229, 255), and set the stroke to a darker accent, (31, 78, 205), which also outlines a focused text input. Migration: code that read `selection.bg_fill` or `selection.stroke.color` as the accent colour should read the theme's `ui.accent`; text painted on `selection.bg_fill` should use `selection.stroke.color`, not white.
- `apply_to_egui` sets `item_spacing` to 8 x 4, `window_margin` to 12 and `menu_margin` to 8 x 4 instead of zero. Layouts that relied on zero spacing should set `ui.spacing_mut().item_spacing = Vec2::ZERO` locally.
- `apply_to_egui` sets `visuals.window_fill` to `UiColors::panel_bg_floating`, which in dark themes is now (42, 46, 57), one step above `panel_bg`, instead of equal to it. Popups and windows also get a hairline border and a smaller shadow.
- The chart's context menus follow the theme applied with `theme::apply_to_egui` (`Theme::dark()` until one is applied); `ChartBuilder::with_theme` sets the chart's colours only.
- `ChartBuilder` no longer defaults the symbol to `BTCUSDT`: a chart built without `with_symbol` has an empty symbol, shows no symbol and asks its feed for nothing until `Chart::set_symbol` is called. Migration: call `with_symbol` to keep the previous behaviour.
- `SymbolInfo::default()` (in `ui::symbol_header`) has empty fields instead of AAPL, Apple Inc, NASDAQ and 1D. Migration: set the fields explicitly.
- `CompareSymbolsDialog::show` takes the symbols to suggest (`show(ctx, &symbols)`, the same `&[model::Symbol]` that `SymbolSearchDialog::show` takes) and lists only those, with an empty state when there are none, instead of a built-in list of tickers the data source may not offer. Added symbols have an empty `CompareSymbol::exchange`, since `Symbol` carries no exchange. Migration: pass the symbols your data source offers.
- `ButtonVariant::bg_color`, `fg_color` and `border_color` resolve colours from the theme applied with `apply_to_egui` and the `Ui`'s visuals instead of fixed design tokens, and `apply_to_style` sets them on a `Ui` instead of a `Style`; `fg_color` returns a real colour for Ghost and Outlined instead of `Color32::PLACEHOLDER`. Migration: pass `ui` as the first argument (call `apply_to_style` inside `ui.scope`); `border_color` no longer takes `hovered`.
- `IconButton`, `SplitButton` and `Button` icons are 16, 20 or 24 px for small, medium and larger buttons (20 px at the default 28 px) instead of 60% of the button size; `icon_scale` still overrides this.
- `SplitButton` occupies the same square cell as an `IconButton` of its size, with the chevron in the cell's corner, instead of a cell 10 px wider.
- `dialog_close_button` is an `IconButton` showing `icons::CLOSE` with a "Close" tooltip.
- `DialogFrame`, `dialog_header`, `DialogFooter`, `Modal`, `ConfirmDialog`, `PromptDialog` and `ModalHandler` share one dialog chrome: the theme's window fill, border, radius and shadow with 16 px padding, a title row with a left-aligned 14 px title and an icon close button centred on it, and a footer whose buttons are 28 px high, 8 px apart, with a filled primary and an outlined secondary. The size passed to `DialogFrame::new` is the preferred content size inside the padding: the dialog is at least that large, grows with its content and is limited to the application window. A `DialogFooter` shown directly in a `DialogFrame::new` frame sits at the bottom of the frame, with any free space above its rule. Modals are centred and sized to their content, and `Modal::width` and `Modal::min_height` set the outer size including the padding (before, the content size); `ConfirmDialog` is cancelled by Escape, the close button or a backdrop click, and `ModalHandler` closes on Escape.
- `UiExt::clickable_label` and the alert count badge of the widget bar take their colours from the active theme: the clickable label is drawn in `ui.accent`, and the badge text is `ui.danger_text`. They used fixed token colours, so a custom accent or the high-contrast preset did not reach them.
- `tokens::DESIGN_TOKENS` is a plain `static` of type `DesignTokens` defined in Rust instead of a `once_cell::sync::Lazy` that parsed an embedded RON file on first access, which took 10 to 40 ms and 1,224 allocations in a release build. The `ron` and `once_cell` dependencies are removed. Migration: field access is unchanged; code that dereferenced the static (`*DESIGN_TOKENS`, `Lazy::force(&DESIGN_TOKENS)`) uses `DESIGN_TOKENS` or `&DESIGN_TOKENS`.
- The drawing storage format is version 2: a `StoredDrawing` holds its anchors and the `Drawing` (`StoredDrawing::drawing`) with the tool type and all settings. `DrawingStorage::from_json` migrates version 1 files, with defaults for the settings they lacked, and fails on any other version instead of relabelling it. It loads each drawing on its own and returns `(DrawingStorage, Vec<drawings::persistence::SkippedDrawing>)`: a drawing it cannot read (a tool type from a newer release or an unknown version 1 tool name, a cursor tool, a missing field, a `null` written for a non-finite number, a number outside the range of its field, a setting out of bounds) is left out and reported with its id and error, while the file as a whole fails only on invalid JSON, an unknown version or an invalid symbol, timeframe or metadata. Saving the returned storage writes only the drawings that loaded; keep the original file if the skipped ones must be recovered. Migration: build a stored drawing with `StoredDrawing::new(id, drawing)`, read its settings from `stored.drawing`, and take the storage from the first element of the `from_json` result, reporting or logging the skipped drawings.
- `drawing_to_stored(drawing, bar_to_time)` and `stored_to_drawing(stored, time_to_bar, next_id)` map anchors between bar index and timestamp instead of taking screen-space closures, and `stored_to_drawing` returns the `Drawing` instead of an `Option`; screen points follow on the next `update_screen_coords`. Migration: pass the chart's bar index to timestamp mapping and its inverse. Both must take fractional bar indices and extrapolate by the bar interval before the first and after the last bar instead of clamping, or anchors outside the loaded bars are moved onto the edge bar and saved there.
- `DrawingStorage::to_json` refuses a drawing holding NaN or an infinity in its stroke width, font size, an anchor price, its position size or a Fibonacci level value, and returns `drawings::persistence::SaveError::NonFinite` with the drawing's id and the field, instead of writing `null` and losing the drawing on the next load. `from_json` skips such a drawing too, so every storage it returns can be saved. Migration: `to_json` returns `Result<String, SaveError>` instead of `Result<String, serde_json::Error>`; a serialization error is `SaveError::Json`.
- `DrawingStorage::from_json` skips a drawing whose id an earlier loaded drawing already has and reports it as a duplicate, so the loaded ids are unique and the first one in the file wins. `SkippedDrawing::index` is the entry's position in the file, which tells skipped entries with the same id or no id apart. Migration: struct literals of `SkippedDrawing` must set `index`.
- `DrawingStorage::from_json` skips a drawing with fewer anchors than its tool draws with and reports it with the tool and the anchor count, since such a drawing loaded as completed, drew nothing and could not be selected or deleted on its own, and a drawing whose timeframe filter names no timeframe loads as visible on every timeframe. `DrawingStorage::to_json` refuses the same drawing with `SaveError::MissingAnchors`, so every storage it accepts loads back whole.
- `DrawingStorage::add_drawing` replaces the drawing with the same id, keeping its position and `created_at`, instead of storing a second one that the next load skipped as a duplicate, so saving a drawing again after an edit stores the edited version once. `DrawingStorage::to_json` refuses duplicate ids that reach the storage another way (the public `drawings` field, a struct literal or an id changed through `get_drawing_mut`) with `SaveError::DuplicateId`, instead of saving a drawing the next load skips. Migration: give each drawing its own id to keep both.
- The cross, dot and arrow cursors are named "Cross Cursor", "Dot Cursor" and "Arrow Cursor" (`DrawingToolType::as_str` and `Display`), so "Arrow" names only the arrow drawing and "Cross" no longer names one tool while parsing to another. The floating toolbar's all-tools menu shows the new names. Migration: code that compares a cursor's `as_str()` or `to_string()` with "Cross", "Dot" or "Arrow" uses the new names.
- Parsing a `DrawingToolType` fails with `drawings::ParseToolTypeError`, whose `input` field holds the name as given and whose message reads `unknown drawing tool "name"`, instead of a `String`. The names that parse, and the tools they parse to, are unchanged. Migration: code that used the error as a `String` reads `error.input` or calls `error.to_string()`.
- `OrderId` and `OrderSide` now live in `model`.
- A last-bar update or an append of `SMA` reads the window from the bars instead of keeping a copy of it, so it no longer copies the whole close series when the period is longer than the series.
