- `DonchianChannels`, `IchimokuCloud`, `Stochastic` and `WilliamsR` find the highest high and lowest low of each window in time linear in the number of bars, independent of the period, instead of rescanning the window for every bar; their values are unchanged. `IchimokuCloud` takes about 24 ms instead of 52 ms on 100,000 bars.
- The default feature is `ui`, so `cargo add egui-charts` gives the chart with its toolbars, panels, dialogs, icons and built-in studies instead of the chart and its icons. Migration: an application that only shows charts sets `default-features = false, features = ["widget"]`, adding `"icons"` for the icon set and `"studies"` for the built-in studies.
- The built-in right-click menus are shown by `ui::context_menu::ChartMenus` (feature `ui`), which takes the chart's right-click; a host that handles right-clicks itself reads `Chart::take_right_click` and does not show `ChartMenus`.
- The built-in studies, `IndicatorFactory` and the `studies::factory` module need the new `studies` feature, which `ui` enables. `Indicator`, `IndicatorValue`, `IndicatorRegistry` and `CustomIndicator` remain in every build, so custom studies work in a bare chart. Migration: a build with `default-features = false` that uses built-in studies adds `"studies"`; it needs no egui, so a headless build keeps computing the same studies.
- `ext` (`UiExt`, `ResponseExt`, `ButtonWidgetExt`, `TextEditWidgetExt`, `RoundingExt`) needs the `ui` feature instead of `widget`. Migration: enable `ui`.
- `ui::context_menu::MenuItem` is generic over its action, `MenuItem<A = ContextMenuAction>`, holds its shortcut as an `egui::KeyboardShortcut` instead of a string, and gains `danger`. Migration: replace `with_shortcut("Cmd R")` with `with_shortcut(KeyboardShortcut::new(Modifiers::COMMAND, Key::R))`, which egui formats for the platform; struct literals set `danger`.
- The top toolbar is 32 px high, with 28 px buttons, 20 px icons and 13 px labels, except Save, which keeps its look. When the window is too narrow, Indicators, Alert and Replay show only their icons, then the least important controls move into a menu at the right end, so no control overlaps another. `TOOLBAR_HEIGHT`, `BUTTON_SIZE` and `ICON_SIZE` in `ui::top_toolbar::config` are 32, 28 and 20. The keyboard focus ring fills the 2 px around a control instead of sitting 2 to 4 px outside it, so a control that fills its strip keeps all four sides of the ring.
- The symbol pill and the interval and chart type buttons are sized to their laid-out labels and drawn alike: an icon where they have one, the label and a chevron, with an outline at rest, the hover fill on hover and the pressed fill while pressed or open. The pill is visible at rest in the light theme, shows the symbol in the primary text colour and no longer overdraws its last letter, and the interval button no longer shows a missing-glyph box. The three report themselves to accessibility tools as buttons and show the keyboard focus ring. `TimeframeSelectorConfig::btn_width` and `ChartTypeSelectorConfig::btn_width` are minimum widths, 0 by default.
- The symbol header shows the last close with its change instead of the bar's open, high, low and close, which the chart legend shows. Its up and down colours default to the theme's success and error text colours, which read at 4.5:1 or better where the candle colours do not (`Color32::PLACEHOLDER` in `SymbolHeaderConfig::default`, resolved while painting), and `SymbolHeaderConfig::from_theme` sets them.
- Widget bar icons are 20 px, as in the top toolbar, and their tooltips open to the left of the button instead of over the button below. The bottom bar's date button icon is 16 px.
- `BarData::from_csv` requires the header row `ts,open,high,low,close,volume` (any case, spaces around names ignored) and returns an error naming it otherwise, instead of taking the first row as a header unchecked and reading the columns by position, which lost the first bar of a file without a header and swapped the columns of a file in another order. An empty file is refused too. Migration: give the file that header, with the columns in that order.
- Every CI job has a `timeout-minutes` limit, so a hung job fails in minutes instead of after the 6 hour default.
- CI runs default, all-feature and headless library tests on stable and Rust 1.95.0 on Linux, macOS and Windows. Headless Chrome also runs the widget's pan, pointer zoom, drawing selection and degenerate-history tests with all features and with the widget alone. ([#254](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/254))
- `ChartState::set_data` returns the index of the first bar that differs from the previous bars, the shorter length when one series is a prefix of the other, or `None` when they are identical.
- `Chart::show` draws the chart's studies, overlays on the main pane and the others in panes below it, and its drawings, and the chart updates its studies once per frame from the first bar changed since the last one. Migration: move the studies in with `add_indicator` and the drawings with `drawings_mut()`, replace `show_with_indicators`, `show_with_drawings` and `show_with_indicators_plot` with `show`, and drop the `calculate_all` calls that followed bar updates.
- The close button in a study pane's legend removes the study. Migration: drop calls to `Chart::take_indicator_remove`, which is removed.
- `ChartElementId::OverlayIndicator(usize)` and `ChartElementId::PaneIndicator(usize)` are replaced by `ChartElementId::Indicator(SeriesId)`, the id `add_indicator` returned. Migration: find the study with `Chart::indicators`.
- `DrawingManager::shift_bar_indices`, `Drawing::shift_bar_indices`, `HistoryService::shift_bar_indices` and `DrawingCommand::shift_bar_indices` take the first bar index to shift, `from`, before the shift. Migration: pass `f32::NEG_INFINITY` to shift every point, as before.
- `Chart` holds its studies as `Box<dyn Indicator>`, which is `Send` but not `Sync`, so `Chart` is no longer `Sync`. Migration: share it behind a `Mutex`; `Arc<Mutex<Chart>>` still works.
- `Chart`'s fields are private. Migration: `chart.config` becomes `chart.config()` or `chart.config_mut()`, `chart.chart_options` becomes `chart.chart_options()`, `chart.marks = markers` becomes `chart.set_markers(SeriesId::MAIN, markers)`, `chart.state.data().bars` becomes `chart.bars(SeriesId::MAIN)`, `chart.state.price_scale_mut()` becomes `chart.price_scale_mut()` and `chart.last_rendered_price_rect` becomes `chart.get_rendered_price_rect()`. Other reads of the state, such as `revision`, `visible_range`, `visible_data`, `time_scale` and `has_zoom_history`, go through `chart.state()`; `time_scale_mut`, `fit_price_scale` and the zoom history's `push_zoom_state`, `pop_zoom_state` and `clear_zoom_history` have no public replacement, since the chart scrolls, fits and zooms itself.
- The floating replay bar opens docked at the bottom of its dock rect instead of at the window's top left corner over the top toolbar, which it did wherever the platform does not report the window position (the web, Wayland). It uses the kit's 28 px icon buttons, a 28 px speed control named "Playback speed" and a secondary "Exit replay" button, shows the symbol and progress at 12 px in the theme's text colours with the progress always shown, keeps its width as the progress and speed change, and casts the popup shadow.
- `ReplayControls`, `CompactReplayControls` and `ReplayToolbar` use the kit's icon buttons instead of text glyphs and egui image buttons. `ReplayToolbar` shows its progress percentage beside the bar instead of over the fill, and the single-step buttons of `ReplayControls` and `ReplayToolbar` show arrows instead of the skip icons.
- `ReplayColors::default` takes the dark theme's colours, and the replay widgets paint with the active theme's colours while the configured colours are the defaults.
- `ReplayStatusIndicator` takes its colours from the active theme, reads "Replay", "Paused" and "Complete" at the theme's small text size, and takes no space for `ReplayStatus::Inactive`.
- `ChartBuilder::with_initial_bars` takes a `usize`. Migration: `Some(n)` becomes `n`; for `None`, build without a feed.
- `Chart::set_symbol` and `Chart::set_timeframe` reload a chart built with a feed at its next `show` when the value changes: the series and its studies are cleared and the new one is loaded, once however many of the two changed. Without a feed they set the label only. `Chart::set_symbol` takes `impl Into<String>`. Migration: `set_timeframe_label(tf.as_str())` becomes `set_timeframe(tf)`.
- `ChartBuilder::build` returns a `Chart` and never blocks. Migration: `tc.chart.x()` and `tc.x()` become `chart.x()`, drop `tc.update()` (`show` applies updates), `tc.take_context_action()` becomes `menus.show(ctx, &mut chart)` with a `ChartMenus` kept next to the chart (feature `ui`).
- `ChartBuilder` moved to `widget::builder`. Migration: import `egui_charts::ChartBuilder` or `egui_charts::widget::ChartBuilder` instead of `egui_charts::chart::builder::ChartBuilder`.
- `ChartBuilder::price_chart` hides the horizontal and vertical grid lines; it used to clear a flag the chart does not read.
- While bars stream, each frame recomputes at most one study in full, the one that has waited longest, and a study then waits 0.25 s, or four times what that recompute took if longer, before its next one. An incremental study follows a new bar or a new close of the last bar on the same frame. `Chart::indicators` can therefore return values that lag the bars by that wait, and after the bars change from the first one on, as when `set_data` loads other bars or older bars are loaded in front, a study has no values until its turn, one study per frame. Migration: a host that reads study values right after `set_data`, for alerts or exports, reads them on a later frame once the study has values again, or calls `calculate` on a clone of the study (`Indicator::clone_box`) with `Chart::bars`.
- While the view is scrolled back, new bars no longer move the visible bars.
- `Exportable::export_png` takes no arguments, since the export has no size or background options. Migration: `export_png(w, h, transparent)` becomes `export_png()`.
- `TopToolbarAction::IntervalSelected` carries a `model::Timeframe` instead of the menu label, so every interval the menu offers, a custom one included, reaches the host: before, 12 of the 21 labels had no `Timeframe::from_resolution` mapping, and `FromStr` read bare minute labels as custom timeframes. Migration: match on the value instead of parsing the label; an interval with no preset of its length arrives as `Timeframe::Custom(seconds)`.
- `HideMenu::show_submenu` takes whether to show the positions and orders rows. Migration: pass `true` to keep them.
- `TopToolbar` and `WidgetBar` have a private field and can no longer be built with a struct literal. Migration: start from `TopToolbar::default()` or `WidgetBar::default()` and set the public fields.
- The drawing bar draws its menus in one flyout. `SubmenuBuilder::new` takes only the `Ui`, `SubmenuBuilder::with_category_rect` is gone and `show` draws the rows into that `Ui`; `SubmenuItem` gains `is_checked` and `is_danger` and implements `Default`; `SubmenuBuilder::checked` and `danger` set them on the item added last. `ToolCategory::render_submenu(ui, sel_tool, favorites)` and `categories::trend_lines::render_tool_submenu(ui, sections, sel_tool, favorites)` draw rows into the `Ui` they are given, take the favourites as `&mut Vec<DrawingToolType>` and no longer take an anchor rect or an id; `ToolCategory::all_tools`, `sections` and `render_submenu` have default implementations that read `data::get_category_sections`. Migration: show the rows inside your own popup, or use `DrawingToolbar`.
- `MagnetButton::show`, `HideMenu::show` and `RemoveMenu::show` return the button's `Response`, where they returned tuples of flags and a rect. `MagnetButton::show_submenu(ui, magnet_on, magnet_type)`, `HideMenu::show_submenu(ui, trading, state)`, `RemoveMenu::show_submenu(ui)` and `TemplatesButton::show_submenu(ui, templates)` draw into the `Ui` they are given. `components::render_svg_at_rect_themed` no longer moves the layout cursor.

### Removed

- The loose ALMA smoothness check, drawing-tool inventory pins, a duplicate tracking default check and an enum-distinctness tautology. ([#258](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/258))
- `model::QuoteSource` and `QuoteSourceError`, a polled quote source nothing consumed, `ui::widget_bar::WatchlistQuote` and `ui::symbol_header::QuoteData`. Migration: implement `services::Quotes` and attach it with `WatchlistPanel::set_service`, and use `model::Quote` in place of the two quote types. ([#222](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/222))
- `TradeSide::Unknown` and `model::RangeTickData`. A trade of unknown side has `Tick::side` set to `None`, the one way to say it, and `to_range_bars_from_ticks` builds range bars from `Tick`, the crate's one trade type. Migration: replace `Some(TradeSide::Unknown)` with `None`, and build `Tick::new(ts, price, volume, None)` in place of a `RangeTickData`. ([#228](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/228))
- Unread design token fields: `UiSemanticTokens::{panel_bg_dark, panel_bg_secondary_dark, panel_bg_floating_dark, text_dark, text_secondary_dark, text_disabled_dark, text_disabled_light, border_subtle_dark, border_control_dark, border_control_light, btn_bg_dark, btn_bg_hover_dark, btn_bg_active_dark, selection_bg_light, selection_text_light, error_light, warning_dark, info_dark}`, `ChartSemanticTokens::{bg_selection, bg_selection_light, bg_light, bg_axis_light, grid_line_light, grid_line_major_light, crosshair_line_light}`, `IndicatorSemanticTokens::{bb_fill, rsi_overbought, rsi_oversold, macd_hist_pos, macd_hist_neg}`, `DrawingSemanticTokens::{default_line, handle, handle_hover, handle_active, line_hover, line_selected, label_bg, fib_0, fib_236, fib_382, fib_618}`, `TpoSemanticTokens::opening_range`, `ToolbarSizingTokens::top_height` and `ShadowTokens::offset_md`. Migration: remove these fields from token struct literals; read applied colours from `ThemeExt::active_theme` and keep host-specific constants where no theme field applies. `UiSemanticTokens::border_dark` and `DrawingSemanticTokens::fib_50` retain their rendering readers, and `ChartSemanticTokens::watermark_alpha` remains available as the documented watermark migration target. ([#367](https://github.com/userFRM/egui-charts/issues/367))

- Unread layout timestamps, cloud/default flags and the `LayoutInfo` wrapper, unused `LayoutAction::OpenMenu` and `SetDefault`, and unused `LayoutMenuConfig` colours and item height. Migration: supply `LayoutMenu::layouts` as `Vec<String>` and update that list directly instead of calling `add_layout`; `LayoutMenuConfig` retains only `menu_width`.

- The "Correlation", "CorrLogRet", "Ratio", "Spread" and advance/decline "A/D Line" studies and their Rust types, since one symbol's bars cannot supply a second series or market breadth, and "Vol Index" (`VolatilityIndex`), whose annualised ATR percentage follows no published definition. Accumulation/Distribution keeps its "A/D Line" output. Migration: the two-series and breadth studies return when studies can read another symbol (#175); a host can compute them today as a custom study in Rust. An annualised ATR percentage can also be written as a custom study.
- `ui::chart_controls::ChartControlBar`, `ChartControlBarState`, `ChartControlBarConfig` and `ChartControlAction`, which nothing drew or handled and whose icons did not match their actions. Migration: remove any use of them; the chart draws and handles its navigation buttons itself, and `ChartConfig::navigation_buttons` sets when they show.
- `studies::list_builtin_indicators`, whose table listed seven names the factory could not create, and the factory names "CVD", "C2C Vol", "HL2", "HLC3" and "Price Channel", which computed the same studies as "A/D", "HV", "MP", "TP" and "Donchian", and "Ehlers Fisher", a variant of the Fisher Transform recursion with length 10 and a clamp on the normalised price. `IndicatorFactory` is now the one catalogue: `IndicatorFactory::category` gives each built-in's category, and a study added with `register` is in `IndicatorFactory::CUSTOM`. Migration: list studies with `IndicatorFactory::list` and describe each with `Indicator::desc` and `IndicatorFactory::category`; create the registered twin of a removed name, or build `CVD`, `CloseToCloseVol`, `HL2`, `HLC3`, `PriceChannel` or `EhlersFisher` directly.
- `chart::overlays::{OrderLine, OrderLineOverlay, OrderLineAction, OrderLineType}`, which nothing in the crate drew, with the re-exports `chart::overlays::{OrderId, OrderSide}` and the sizing tokens `ChartsExtSizingTokens::{order_line_label_width, order_line_label_height}`. Migration: draw each order's price as a `model::PriceLine` with its label as the title, for example `PriceLine::new(order.price, color).with_title(label)`, and pass the lines to `Chart::set_price_lines(SeriesId::MAIN, lines)`; the chart places them through its own price scale, so no `price_to_y` mapping is needed. Selecting, dragging and cancelling an order line (`OrderLineAction`) and its cancel button have no replacement: price lines do not respond to the pointer. Import `OrderId` and `OrderSide` from `model`. Code that read the two tokens uses the values they held, 120.0 and 20.0, and `ChartsExtSizingTokens` literals drop the two fields.
- `BoxZoomMode` and `BoxZoomState::mode`: no chart could select the measure mode, and a box released in it showed nothing. Migration: remove any use of them; box zoom always zooms, and the `Measure` drawing tool measures the price change, percentage and bar count between two points.
- `DatePickerConfig`, which no API ever passed to a `DatePicker`. Migration: remove any use of it; the go to date picker takes its colours from the active theme.
- `IndicatorDialogConfig::bg_color` and `IndicatorDialogConfig::sidebar_color`, which had no effect: the dialog takes its surface from the kit modal and its sidebar from `SidebarTab`. Migration: drop both fields from struct literals; the dialog surface is `Theme::ui.panel_bg_floating`, which `apply_to_egui` applies as egui's `Visuals::window_fill`.
- `ChartSettingsState::color_based_on_prev_close`, which had no effect.
- `Timeframe::total_seconds`, `Timeframe::as_seconds` and `Timeframe::to_seconds`, which returned 0 for `Ms100`, `Ms250` and `Ms500`. The demo's sample bars at those timeframes were one second apart and now follow the timeframe. Migration: use `Timeframe::duration_ms()`, or `duration()` for a `chrono::Duration`; time intervals always have a positive duration.
- `ThemePreset`, `ThemeManager`, `Theme::{classic, midnight, from_preset, name, is_dark_ui, is_dark_chart}`, `Theme::components` with `ComponentStyles` and the unused `BtnStyle`, `ToolbarStyle`, `PanelStyle`, `MenuStyle`, `InputStyle`, `ChartStyle`, `CandleStyle` and `VolumeStyle`, the colour accessors on `Theme` (`background`, `grid`, `text`, `bullish`, `tpo_*`, `footprint_*` and the others), `theme::all_presets`, `theme::all_themes`, and `theme::SemanticTokens` with `IndicatorSemanticTokens` and `DrawingSemanticTokens`, whose fields nothing read. Migration: use `Theme::dark()`, `Theme::light()` or `Theme::high_contrast()`, and `theme::presets()` for the list; the Classic look is `Theme { chart: Theme::dark().chart, ..Theme::light() }`; Midnight was identical to Dark; persist a theme choice in the application (with the `serde` feature, serialise the `Theme`). TPO and footprint colours are in `DESIGN_TOKENS.semantic.tpo` and `DESIGN_TOKENS.semantic.footprint`.
- Fields of the old theme colour structs that nothing drew with, and their constructors and alpha helpers. Migration: `UiSemanticTokens::icon_disabled` is `ui.text_disabled`; `ChartSemanticTokens::bg_legend` is `chart.bg_tooltip`; `price_line` is `chart.axis_text`; `price_label_bg` and `price_label_text` are `chart.crosshair_label_bg` and `chart.crosshair_label_text`; `watermark` was the secondary axis text in dark themes and the muted text in light ones at `DESIGN_TOKENS.semantic.chart.watermark_alpha`; `bg_selection` has no replacement. For `candle_up_alpha(a)` and `candle_down_alpha(a)`, build the colour from `chart.candle_up` or `chart.candle_down` with `Color32::from_rgba_unmultiplied`. For `UiSemanticTokens::from_design_tokens(dark)` and `ChartSemanticTokens::from_design_tokens(dark)`, take `ui` or `chart` from `Theme::dark()` or `Theme::light()`; the `from_design_tokens` constructors of `SemanticTokens`, `IndicatorSemanticTokens` and `DrawingSemanticTokens` went with those types.
- Design tokens that nothing read: `tokens::ListItemSemanticTokens`, `HeatmapSemanticTokens`, `ToastSemanticTokens`, `TierSemanticTokens`, `AuthDialogSizingTokens`, `BurgerMenuSizingTokens`, `ChartSizingTokens`, `CursorSizingTokens`, `EmojiPickerSizingTokens`, `FootprintSizingTokens`, `RightSidebarSizingTokens`, `WatermarkSizingTokens` and `WidgetSizingTokens` with the `SemanticTokens` and `SizingTokens` fields that held them, and 111 unread fields of the other token structs. Migration: code that named a removed token uses the value it held, listed in `src/tokens/design_tokens.ron` of 0.2.0; colour tokens there name a palette entry (for example `blue_500`) whose RGB value is in the file's `palette` section.
- `theme::ThemeData`, `theme::ThemeContextExt`, `theme::ThemeUiExt`, `theme::context::{get_icon_color, get_icon_hover_color, get_icon_active_color}`, `ext::DesignTokens`, `ext::HasDesignTokens`, `ext::ContextExt` and the `theming` module, which were five ways to read the same colours, and `styles::margin` and the `styles::sizing` modules `toolbar`, `auth_dialog`, `footprint`, `indicator` and `candle`, which nothing used. Migration: `ctx.active_theme()` and `ui.active_theme()` (trait `theme::ThemeExt`) return the theme last applied, or the dark theme, instead of an `Option`, and `ctx.theme_data()` and `DesignTokens::from_context(ctx)` are `ctx.active_theme()`; `set_theme_data` is `apply_to_egui`; a `ThemeData` or `DesignTokens` colour is the `Theme` field of the same meaning (`ui_text` and `text_primary()` are `ui.text`, `chart_bg_axis` and `chart_axis_bg()` are `chart.bg_axis`, `crosshair()` is `chart.crosshair_line`, `volume_bullish` is `chart.volume_up()`), and `page_bg` was `ui.panel_bg_secondary` in dark themes and `chart.bg` in light ones; colours that egui models are read from `ui.visuals()`. By replacement: `visuals.panel_fill` for `theming::toolbar_bg`, `panel_fill()` and `theme_panel_bg()`; `visuals.widgets.hovered.bg_fill` for `theming::hover_color`, `theming::btn_bg_hover`, `hover_bg()` and `theme_hover_bg()`; `visuals.widgets.active.bg_fill` for `theming::sel_color`, `theming::btn_bg_pressed`, `active_bg()` and `theme_active_bg()`; `Color32::TRANSPARENT` for `theming::btn_bg_normal`; `visuals.widgets.noninteractive.fg_stroke.color` for `theming::icon_color`, `icon_normal` and `muted_color`, `icon_color()`, `text_secondary()`, `theme_icon()` and `theme_text_secondary()`; `visuals.widgets.hovered.fg_stroke.color` for `theming::icon_hover_color` and `theming::icon_active`, `icon_hover()` and `theme_icon_hover()`; `visuals.strong_text_color()` for `theming::active_icon_color`, `HasDesignTokens::text_color()` and `theme_text()`; `visuals.text_color()` for `theming::text_color`; `visuals.widgets.noninteractive.bg_stroke.color` for `theming::separator_color`, `border_color()` and `theme_border()`; `visuals.weak_text_color()` for `ThemeData::ui_text_muted` and `text_muted()`; `visuals.warn_fg_color` for `warning_color()` and `theme_warning()`; the theme's `ui.accent` for `theme_accent()`, `theme_icon_active()`, `accent_color()`, `icon_active()` and `theming::publish_button_bg`, and `ui.accent_hover`, `ui.accent_active` and `ui.accent_text` for `publish_button_bg_hover`, `publish_button_bg_pressed` and `publish_button_text`; `ui.accent_text` for `ThemeData::ui_accent_text`; `ui.danger_text` for `theming::badge_text_color`; `ui.success` for `success_color()` and `theme_success()`; `chart.bg`, `chart.grid_line`, `chart.axis_text`, `chart.candle_up` and `chart.candle_down` for `chart_bg()`, `chart_grid()`, `theme_chart_text()`, `bullish_color()` and `bearish_color()` and their `theme_` forms; `DESIGN_TOKENS.semantic.footprint` for the footprint colour getters; `theme.dark` for `is_dark_ui()` and `ContextExt::is_dark_mode()`, and `theme.chart.bg.intensity() < 0.5` for `is_dark_chart()`. `theming::active_hover_color`, `pill_bg`, `lighten_color` and `darken_color` have no replacement; for an opaque colour, `lighten_color(c, f)` is `c.lerp_to_gamma(Color32::WHITE, f)` up to rounding. The `ContextExt` device queries returned constants, and `is_touch_active` is `ctx.input(|i| i.any_touches())`. For `styles::margin`, use the `egui::Margin` constructors. Widgets drawn before any `apply_to_egui` call read the dark theme, not the light colours of `ThemeData::default()`.
- The unused `panel_bg`, `panel_border` and `header_bg` fields of `tokens::ModalSemanticTokens`. Migration: use the theme's `ui.panel_bg_floating` and `ui.border`.
- `scales::{DualPriceScaleManager, PosedPriceScaleOptions, SeriesScaleAssignment, PriceDisplayMode, AutoScaleMode}`, which nothing used. `scales::PriceScaleId` stays.
- `ChartMapping::x_only` and `ChartMapping::with_price_range`, which nothing called. Migration: build the mapping with the `PriceMap` it needs.
- `widget::apply_price_zoom` and `widget::y_to_price`, which assumed a linear axis. Migration: `PriceScale::zoom` and `PriceMap::y_to_price`.
- `Chart::get_rendered_price_range`. Migration: `Chart::get_chart_mapping().price` maps prices exactly as the last frame drew them.
- `Chart::apply_price_zoom`, a step of `Chart::show`. Migration: zoom the price axis with `chart.price_scale_mut().zoom(factor, anchor)`.
- `PriceScaleOptions::{align_labels, border_visible, visible, ticks_visible, entire_text_only}`, which nothing read. Migration: remove them from struct literals; `ChartConfig::show_right_axis` shows or hides the price axis.
- `ui_kit::buttons::button_bg` and `ui_kit::buttons::icon_color`, which had no pressed state. Migration: use `ButtonVariant::Ghost.bg_color(ui, hovered, pressed)`, or `ButtonVariant::Primary` for a selected button, and `ButtonVariant::fg_color`.
- The Volume Footprint and Session Volume chart types, which approximated per-price volume from bar totals, with their `icons::icons::CHART_VOLUME_FOOTPRINT` and `icons::icons::CHART_SESSION_VOLUME` icons, and the unused `model::footprint` module.
- The `chart::series_api` module (`ISeriesApi`, `IPriceScaleApi`, `ITimeScaleApi`, `IStudyApi`, their implementations and option types), which nothing constructed.
- The series context menu "Hide" entry (`SeriesContextMenuAction::Hide`), which had no effect.
- `DrawingManager::update_pos_prices`, `Drawing::format_timestamp`, and the `Drawing` fields `is_closed`, `curr_price`, `forecast_start_price`, `forecast_target_price`, `forecast_start_time`, and `forecast_target_time`; position and forecast results come from the chart's bars.
- Unused icon constants `AREA_CHART`, `LINE_CHART`, `ORDER_BOOK`, `TIME_AND_SALES`, `HIDE`, `RISK_REWARD_SHORT`, `TREND_BASED_FIB_EXTENSION`, `ZOOM_TOGGLE`, `EMOJI_EAGLE`, `EMOJI_ORNAMENT`, `WIDGET_BAR_NOTIFICATIONS`, `WATCHLIST_44`, `SCREENER_44`, `PINE_SCRIPT_44`, `COMMUNITY_44` and `PRODUCTS_44`. Migration: use `CHART_AREA`, `CHART_LINE`, `WATCHLIST`, `EYE_HIDE` (or `EYE` for the visible state), `SHORT_POSITION` and `FIB_EXTENSION`; the others have no replacement.
- The unused series renderers `chart::series::{LineSeries, AreaSeries, BaselineSeries, HistogramSeries, BarSeries}` and the `Series` trait with `SeriesData` and `SeriesType`, which the chart never drew through. No replacement; the chart renders its series from `ChartType`.
- The `model::enums` module of wire-format enums, which nothing used, with `ChartType::to_chart_style` and `From<ChartStyle> for ChartType`. Migration: use `scales::PriceScaleMode` for `PriceScaleMode`, `ChartType` for `ChartStyle`, and `model::LineStyle` for `LineStyle` (it has no large-dashed or sparse-dotted style); `CrosshairMode` and `ColorType` are unrelated to `config::CrosshairMode` and `model::ColorType` and, like `ChartMode`, `Timezone`, `MagnetMode`, `Orientation`, `Size`, `Resolution`, `ChartTheme`, `PriceLineSource`, `ScaleLocation`, `BackgroundType`, `OverrideLineStyle`, `OverridePriceAxisLastValueMode`, `ChartStyleParameters`, `ClearMarksMode`, `MarkLocation`, `StudyPlotType`, `StudyDisplayTarget`, `StudyInputType`, `StudyTargetPriceScale`, `LineStudyPlotStyle`, `OhlcStudyPlotStyle` and `FilledAreaType`, have no replacement.
- `ui::top_toolbar::components::symbol_search::popular_symbols`, a fixed list of index, crypto, commodity and forex tickers. Migration: pass your own symbols to `CompareSymbolsDialog::show`.
- The `templates` module. Migration: import `SettingsTemplate` and `TemplateManager` from `ui::model`.
- `CVDMode::TickSplit`, which computed the same estimate as `CVDMode::ClosePosition` while claiming footprint data; use `ClosePosition`. `CVD::with_histogram`, which had no effect, with no replacement.
- The Iceberg, Liquidity and Stop Run studies: `IcebergDetector`, `IcebergDetection`, `LiquidityTracker`, `LiquidityLevel`, `LiquiditySweep`, `LiquidityZone`, `LiquidityZoneType`, `SweepDirection`, `StopRunIndicator`, `StopRunDetection` and `StopRunType`, and the factory names "Iceberg", "Liquidity" and "Stop Run". Bars carry no trades and no order book, so these studies guessed hidden orders, swept liquidity and stop runs from bar shapes and from volume spread evenly over each bar's range. Studies compute fixed aggregations of the data the chart receives; CVD, Net Volume and the volume profiles are unchanged. Migration: detect these events in your application or on your server, from your own trade and order book data. `IndicatorFactory::create` returns `None` for the three names, so code that builds studies from stored names should skip unknown ones.
- The `ui::watermark` module (`Watermark`, `WatermarkManager`, `TextWatermark`, `TextWatermarkLineOptions`, `ImageWatermark`, `ImageScaleMode`, `HorizontalAlign`, `VerticalAlign` and its own `WatermarkPos`), which the chart never drew. Migration: `ui::watermark` is removed; the chart's watermark is configured through `ChartConfig` (`show_watermark`, `watermark_text`, `watermark_color`, `watermark_font_size` and `watermark_pos`, a `config::WatermarkPos`). Image watermarks, custom pixel positions and a font size, colour or weight per line have no replacement; `watermark_text` may contain `\n` for several lines in one style.
- `StoredColor`, `StoredValue`, `StoredDrawing::{tool_type, color, line_width, selected, locked, visible, properties}`, `StoredDrawing::{set_property, get_property}` and `StoredCoord::{from_screen, to_screen}` from `drawings::persistence`. Migration: read and set the settings on `StoredDrawing::drawing`; selection is not stored.
- `Deserialize` for `drawings::persistence::DrawingStorage`, which skipped the version check, the migration and per-drawing loading. Migration: load with `DrawingStorage::from_json`. To keep drawings inside your own serde state, store the `to_json` text and pass it to `from_json`.
- The `scripting` feature and module, the script editor dialog in `ui::dialogs` with its action enum, and the indicator dialog's "My scripts" category (`IndicatorCategory::MyScripts`), which listed the built-in indicators. The interpreter ignored the source argument of most `ta` functions, never reported a crossover and ran one statement per block, and the editor ran scripts on no bars and could not add one to a chart. Migration: write custom studies in Rust with `studies::CustomIndicator`; 0.2.0 keeps the module.
- The `backtest` feature and module, and the indicator dialog's "Strategies" tab (`IndicatorTab::Strategies`), which listed the built-in indicators. The backtester filled orders at the close of the signal bar, stamped trades with the wall-clock time, dropped orders larger than the cash without notice and left several metrics unset. The dialog is titled "Indicators" and the series context menu entry reads "Add indicator". Migration: run backtests in the host or on a server against the headless engine with the built-in studies (`default-features = false, features = ["studies"]`); 0.2.0 keeps the module.
- `IndicatorPalette`, `IndicatorColorSchemes` and `ColorCategory`, which no study or component used. Study colours come from `Indicator::colors` and the theme.
- `ToastKind::bg_color` and `ToastKind::text_color`, fixed colours that ignored the theme. Migration: `ToastKind::color(&theme.ui)` gives the kind's status colour; a toast draws on `visuals.window_fill` with `theme.ui.text`.
- `AlertKind::bg_color`, `AlertKind::border_color` and `AlertKind::icon_color`, fixed colours tuned for the dark theme. Migration: `AlertKind::color(&theme.ui)` gives the kind's status colour; an alert fills with `color.gamma_multiply(0.1)` and outlines with `color.gamma_multiply(0.4)`.
- `Chart::update_data` and `Chart::data`. Migration: `chart.update_data(BarData::from_bars(bars))` becomes `chart.set_data(SeriesId::MAIN, bars)`, or `chart.update(SeriesId::MAIN, bar)` for each new or changed bar, and `chart.data().bars` becomes `chart.bars(SeriesId::MAIN)`. The `bool` that `update_data` returned has no replacement: the chart follows the live edge itself when `ChartOptions::time_scale.shift_visible_range_on_new_bar` is set.
- The `validation` module (`DataValidator` and its result types) and `Chart::{enable_validation, disable_validation, set_validator, is_validation_enabled}`, which only logged problems: every bar is validated and invalid ones are dropped. Migration: remove the calls; `Chart::feed_state(SeriesId::MAIN).rejected_bars` counts the dropped bars.
- `ChartBuilder::extended` and `ChartBuilder::with_drawing_tools`: every chart has studies and drawings. Migration: use `ChartBuilder::new()`.
- The consuming `Chart::config(self, config)`, whose name now reads the configuration. Migration: build with `Chart::with_config(data, config)` or set `*chart.config_mut() = config`.
- `Chart::timescale_marks`, which was never drawn.
- `examples/tauri_integration.rs`, replaced by `examples/live_feed.rs`.
- `TradingChart`: use `Chart`, built with `ChartBuilder` (see Changed). Its studies and drawings live on the chart: `tc.indicators.add(s)` becomes `chart.add_indicator(s)`, `tc.drawing_manager` becomes `chart.drawings_mut()`, `tc.remove_indicator(i)` becomes `chart.remove_series(id)`, `tc.set_indicator_visible(i, v)` becomes `chart.set_indicator_visible(id, v)` with the study's `SeriesId`, `tc.remove_drawing(id)` becomes `chart.drawings_mut().delete_drawing(id)`, `tc.get_last_bar()` becomes `chart.bars(SeriesId::MAIN).last()`, `tc.get_symbol()` and `tc.symbol` become `chart.symbol()`, `tc.timeframe` becomes `chart.timeframe()` (an `Option`), and `tc.data_src` has no replacement: pass the feed to `ChartBuilder::with_feed`.
- `data::DataSource`, `DataSourceError`, `DataUpdate`, `HistoricalDataRequest`, `SymbolSearchResult`, `PaginatedSearchResult`, `BarMark` and `TimescaleMark`, and the `DataSource` re-export at the crate root. Migration: implement `data::Datafeed`: answer `history` through the `Reply` (`HistoryRequest::before` is an exclusive `DateTime<Utc>`, `None` for the latest bars), and for live data push into the `Sink` given to `subscribe`. Errors map to `FeedError`: `ConnError`, `FetchError` and `IoError` to `Network`, `ParseError` and `InvalidTimeRange` to `InvalidData`, `SymbolNotFound` to `SymbolNotFound`, `RateLimited` to `RateLimited { retry_after: None }` and `NotSupported` to `NotSupported`. Symbol search is the `services::SymbolSearch` service.
- `ChartBuilder::{with_data_src, with_auto_fetch, with_auto_fetch_on_symbol_change, with_auto_fetch_on_timeframe_change}`: use `with_feed`; a feed always reloads on a change.
- `ChartBuilder::{with_left_price_scale, with_left_axis_width, with_right_axis_width}`, which set fields the chart never read.
- `ui::context_menu::ContextMenuAction::RemoveIndicator`, which no menu produced. Remove a study with `Chart::remove_series`.
- `model::TickSource` and `model::OrderBookSource`, which the chart never polled: push trades with `Sink::push_tick` and the order book with `Sink::push_depth_snapshot` and `Sink::push_depth_delta`.
- `model::LibrarySymbolInfo`, `SymbolFormat`, `SubsessionInfo` and the `model::symbol_info` module, which nothing read: describe an instrument with `model::Instrument`. `SymbolType` is unchanged at `model::SymbolType`. `model::DataStatus` is now the type of `Instrument::data_status`, with the variants `Realtime` (formerly `Streaming`), `Delayed` and `EndOfDay`; `Pulsed` has no replacement, and the type no longer implements `Default`, `Display` or serde's traits.
- `model::bar::{MAX_BARS, MAX_VISIBLE_BARS}` and `BarData::{trim_to_limit, push_with_limit}`, which nothing applied.
- `TopToolbarAction::{ChartGridChanged, SetSyncOptions}` and the `TopToolbar::{chart_grid_selector, sync_button}` fields, which the toolbar never drew: no control emits them. `ChartGridSelector` and `SyncButton` stay in `top_toolbar::components`.
- `DrawingToolbarAction::{ClearSelection, AddFavorite, RemoveFavorite, OpenSettings, DeleteTemplate}`, which no control emits.
- `TimeframeToolbarAction::{TimeframeChanged, GoToDate, TimezoneChanged, SessionChanged, ZoomFitAll, ZoomToRange, ToggleTradingPanel, TradingPanelFullscreen}`, which no control emits, and `ui::timeframe_toolbar::{TradingPanelRow, TradingPanelRowAction, TradingPanelRowConfig}`, which nothing drew, with the `PanelSizingTokens::{trading_panel_row_height, trading_panel_row_icon_size, trading_panel_row_btn_size}` tokens that sized it and `TimeframeToolbarState::{trading_panel_expanded, toggle_trading_panel, set_trading_panel_expanded}`, which nothing read. Migration: the date picker emits `DatePickerAction::GoToDate`.
- `ObjectTreeAction::{ShowSelected, LockSelected, UnlockSelected, UpdateProperties, Reorder, BringSelectedToFront, SendSelectedToBack, PanTo, SaveAsTemplate, LoadTemplate, DeleteTemplate, ToggleDataWindow}`, which no control emits.
- `ContextMenuAction::{GoToDate, ChangeSymbol, ChangeInterval, SymbolInfo, InsertIndicator, CompareOrAddSymbol, DrawingTools, HideMarksOnBars, PriceScale, TimeScale}`, which no menu entry emits.
- `SettingsAction::LayoutStyleChanged` and `SeriesSettingsAction::ResetToDefault`, which no control emits.
- The top toolbar's Menu, Trade and Publish buttons with `TopToolbarAction::{OpenMenu, OpenTradingPanel, Publish}`, and `top_toolbar::buttons::{PillButtonFilled, PillButtonOutlined}`, whose only users they were. No service backs them: the order ticket brings its own buttons, and a desktop app has its own main menu.
- The chart context menu's Paste, Add order, Lock vertical cursor line by time and Table view entries with `ContextMenuAction::{Paste, AddOrder, LockVerticalCursor, TableView}`, which nothing implemented.
- The series context menu's Add order, Add financial metric, Security info, Metrics, Paste, Table view, Visual order, Move to, Pin to scale, Add to watchlist and Add text note entries with their `SeriesContextMenuAction` variants, which nothing implemented. The menu offers Add alert, Add indicator, Copy price and Settings.
- The drawing context menu's Copy entry and `DrawingContextMenuAction::Copy`: there is no drawing clipboard. Migration: match `Clone` to duplicate a drawing.
- `MenuItem::submenu` and `MenuItem::is_submenu`, with the `ContextMenuSizingTokens::submenu_arrow_font_size` token of their arrow: no menu row opens a submenu.
- `ExportConfig::{include_title, include_timestamp, include_watermark, width, height, transparent_background}` and the export dialog's checkboxes and size options for them, which no export applied, and `ExportFormat::Svg` with `Exportable::export_svg`, which nothing rendered. Migration: build `ExportConfig { format }`; an image export has the size of the chart on screen.
- `TimeframeUnit::Tick` and the tick intervals of the interval menu: no data contract carries tick bars.
- `DrawingToolType::Paintbrush`, which drew exactly as `Brush` and was on no menu. Migration: use `DrawingToolType::Brush`. Drawings saved in the crate's JSON format (`DrawingStorage`, versions 1 and 2) and other formats that store the tool by name, and names parsed with `FromStr`, load `Paintbrush` as a brush. Data that stores the variant index (bincode, postcard) must be re-saved in a name-based format before upgrading.
- The drawing bar's Favourites button, `utilities::FavoritesButton`, `DrawingToolbarAction::ToggleFavoritesToolbar` and `ToolbarState::show_favorites_toolbar`: the button filled and nothing else changed. The stars in the flyouts keep the favourites list. `categories::trend_lines::get_shortcut` and `categories::fibonacci::get_shortcut` are gone; the flyouts read `DrawingToolShortcuts`. The Remove menu no longer has a separate "Remove All Studies" row; `DrawingToolbarAction::RemoveAllStudies` remains.

## [0.2.0] - 2026-06-04

### Added

- Click-to-select for indicators and series on a single unified selection model, with on-chart selection handles.
- Right-click context menus for the chart background, a series, and a drawing, opened directly from the chart.
- Hover tooltip with an OHLC readout, in floating, tracking, and magnifier modes.
- Session-break dividers and optional background shading, with a `ChartBuilder::with_session_breaks` toggle (timeframe-aware day, week, and month boundaries).
- TPO / Market Profile chart type with point-of-control and a contiguous 70% value-area computation, rendered as letters or blocks.
- Remove an indicator from its pane legend; `Chart::take_indicator_remove` surfaces the request to host applications.
- Host interaction plumbing: `Chart::take_right_click` and `RightClickTarget`, `TradingChart::take_context_action` and `ChartContextAction`.
- `TradingChart` controller helpers: `config_mut`, `apply_series_settings`, `remove_indicator`, `set_indicator_visible`, and `remove_drawing`.
- The bundled demo is now a complete application: every toolbar action, all dialogs, the object-tree and alerts panels, and bar replay are wired, and it is published as a live WebAssembly build.

### Fixed

- Backtest equity now signs short positions correctly, and the Sharpe, Sortino, and Calmar ratios are no longer inflated by a percent-versus-fraction unit mismatch.
- RSI output length now matches the bar count and is no longer misaligned by one bar.
- The indicator factory constructs every built-in indicator by name rather than a small subset.
- Heikin-Ashi candles are computed over the full series, so they no longer change shape on pan and zoom.
- Coordinate and formatter transforms guard against flat price ranges and zero denominators.
- Drawing hit-testing is unified through a single path, and an undone add now survives history pruning so redo still works.
- Selection handles render correctly when the chart is scrolled.
- Embedded SVG icons render because the image loaders are now installed automatically, and notification timing no longer panics on WebAssembly.
- The bundled examples compile again.
- Issue #1: the focus ring appeared when the pointer merely hovered the chart. Issue #2: the examples did not compile.

### Changed

- Renamed `ui::stubs` to `ui::model`.
- Corrected the documented metrics to match the code.
- CI now builds the examples and runs the documentation and feature-gated tests.

### Removed

- The unused `egui_plot` dependency.
- Superseded legacy renderers and the unused render pipeline.

Thanks to @intelligentnet and @sstscrypto for the reports that became issues #2 and #1.
